# Endotoxin slice 5: method prep rules on the 2 mL worksheet: Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** The lab keeps an editable list of prep rules: per catalog peptide and weight band, a total dilution, a step 1 ratio and a cartridge. These rules set a 2 mL worksheet row's defaults. Each rule locks onto the row at print, at the first result, or at completion. Locked rows can be moved deliberately with a per-row Re-apply that keeps their history. Dennis can override a rule per row.

**Architecture:**
- A new `endo_prep_rules` table, with CRUD routes, and a pure matcher (`backend/endo_prep_rules.py`) mirrored in TypeScript (`src/lib/endo-prep-rules.ts`). One shared JSON fixture pins the two sides to identical answers.
- Worksheet items gain a lock snapshot, a "no rule" switch, a special-prep note and a history list.
- Every computation and server check uses effective values: typed beats rule beats standard.
- Lock events hook into the existing print, reading-save, reader-import and complete routes.
- Re-apply is a preview route plus an apply route.
- The frontend adds the rule tier to the prep maths, a rules page, Box 2 tags with the switch, the note, a history view and the Re-apply dialog, and bench-sheet marks.

**Tech Stack:**
- Backend: FastAPI + SQLAlchemy 2 (SQLite in unit tests, host dev Postgres in route tests), pytest.
- Frontend: React 19 + TypeScript + TanStack Query + the app's shadcn/Radix components, vitest + Testing Library. npm only.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-endo-slice5-method-rules-design.md` (approved 2026-10-14). Read it before any task; section numbers below refer to it. The groundwork map is `.superpowers/slice5-method-rules-map.md` (git-ignored, in the worktree).

## Global Constraints

- **Branch and pushing.** Branch `feat/endo-slice3` in worktree `C:/tmp/Accu-Mk1-endo-s3` (slice 5 continues on it; base c9ccd773). Never push or merge.
- **Additive only.** With an empty rules table, every row, computation, printout, server check and response behaves byte-for-byte as before, except that new keys are added to responses. Stock worksheets, bac water and blends are never ruled.
- **Precedence.** Per field, typed beats rule, which beats standard (`prep_total_dilution`, `prep_steps[0][1]`, `cartridge_type_id`). A row with `prep_rules_off` uses typed, then standard.
- **Matching.** Peptide = the parent's single catalog peptide (`sample_peptide_id`; None for a blend). Weight = `prep_weight_mg`, else `declared_weight_mg`. The band is `weight > min_mg` and `weight <= max_mg`, with null meaning no bound; a row with no weight matches only a line with both bounds null. Of several matches, the narrowest band wins, where width = (`max_mg` or infinity) - (`min_mg` or 0); a tie goes to the lowest `sort_order`. Only active rules match.
- **Locks.** A lock snapshot is a full copy of the matched line, or `"rule": null` for the standard: `{"rule": ..., "locked_at": iso, "locked_by": "print"|"result"|"complete"|"reapply"|"switch", "user_id": int|null}`.
- **Rows the server must never change.** A promoted row and any row on a completed worksheet are never changed by Re-apply. Rule edits never reach a locked row.
- **No N+1.** The worksheet serializer issues at most one extra query per worksheet for peptide ids, never one per item (the 09-15 pool outage).
- **Response models.** FastAPI `response_model` silently drops undeclared keys, so every new response key is declared on its model.
- **Text and colour.** No em dashes anywhere (code, comments, strings, tests, commit messages). Every new in-app colour has a `dark:` variant.
- **Backend tests.** Run `cd /c/tmp/Accu-Mk1-endo-s3/backend && python -m pytest tests/<file>.py -q -p no:cacheprovider`, one pytest process at a time, never the whole suite. Record a baseline before editing and gate on new failures only.
- **Frontend checks.** `npx vitest run <file>`, `npx tsc --noEmit -p .`, `npx eslint <files> --max-warnings 0`, and `npx prettier --check <new files>`. In files that were not prettier-clean at base, format only your own lines.
- **Commits.** Use the pathspec form: `git -C /c/tmp/Accu-Mk1-endo-s3 commit -m "<type>(endo-s5): <summary>" -m "Co-Authored-By: Claude <Sonnet|Opus> 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>" -- <paths>`. Keep each file's line endings (`core.autocrlf=false`).
- Host dev Postgres hazard: the host dev Postgres never gets slice 5's table or columns. After Task 1, never run `tests/test_sla_schema.py`, `tests/test_priority_migration.py` or `tests/test_priority_embed.py` on this machine (they run the boot migrations against the host DB on import); after Task 3, never run `tests/test_worksheets_inbox.py` or `tests/test_worksheet_department_schema.py` here (they load worksheet items from the host DB). All five run in Task 10 on the stack.
- **Git maintenance.** Never run repo-wide git maintenance (`git worktree prune`, `git gc`, branch deletes) on the shared `.git`.

## Review Focus

1. **A rule edited after the sheet was printed.** The printed rows keep the dilution the bench used. Only the deliberate Re-apply moves them. Pinned in Task 4.
2. **A row with a typed override, a "no rule" row and a ruled row on one worksheet.** Re-apply lists only the ruled row, and Apply never changes a typed value. Pinned in Tasks 5 and 8.
3. **A ruled row promoted to the parent.** The promote check (slice 3 I-3) accepts it, using the locked rule's total. Pinned in Task 3.
4. **Two overlapping lines for one peptide (`<= 30 mg` and `any weight`).** The narrower wins on both sides, identically. Pinned by the shared fixture in Tasks 1 and 6.
5. **The rules page saving a line that breaks the step 1 floor (total < 2 x ratio).** Refused with a clear message. A valid line with an overlap saves with a warning. Pinned in Tasks 2 and 7.

## Tasks and interfaces (the contract every part follows)

### Backend: `backend/endo_prep_rules.py`

```python
STEP1_RATIO = 10  # mirrors src/lib/endo-prep-2ml.ts STEP1_RATIO
LOCK_EVENTS = ("print", "result", "complete", "reapply", "switch")

def snapshot(rule) -> dict
    # keys: id, peptide_id, min_mg, max_mg, total_dilution, step1_ratio,
    # cartridge_type_id, status, note, sort_order, active

def load_rules(db, *, active_only: bool = True) -> list[dict]   # snapshots, ordered by sort_order, id

def match_rule(rules: list[dict], *, peptide_id: Optional[int], weight_mg: Optional[float],
               is_bac: bool, endo_prep: Optional[str], rules_off: bool) -> Optional[dict]

def row_rule(item, *, rules: list[dict], peptide_id: Optional[int], weight_mg: Optional[float],
             is_bac: bool, endo_prep: Optional[str]) -> Optional[dict]
    # rules_off -> None; locked (item.prep_rule) -> item.prep_rule["rule"]; else match_rule

def effective_values(item, rule: Optional[dict], *, std_total: float) -> dict
    # {"total_dilution": float, "step1_ratio": float, "cartridge_type_id": Optional[int],
    #  "ruled": {"total_dilution": bool, "step1_ratio": bool, "cartridge_type_id": bool}}
    # typed > rule > standard; cartridge None means "the usual chain" (bac 0.01, run, method default)

def row_facts(db, worksheet, items) -> dict[int, dict]
    # item_id -> {"peptide_id", "weight_mg", "is_bac"}; one batched peptide query per call

def lock_rows(db, worksheet, items, *, event: str, user_id: Optional[int]) -> int
    # locks every item that is not locked and not rules_off; returns the count; does not commit

def release_on_clear(item) -> None      # only when prep_rule["locked_by"] == "result"

def append_history(item, *, why: str, user_id: Optional[int], effective: dict) -> None   # why: "reapply" | "override"

def reapply_candidates(db, worksheet) -> list[dict]
    # [{"item_id", "sample_id", "old": <lock snapshot>, "new_rule": <rule snapshot or None>}]

def apply_reapply(db, worksheet, item_ids: list[int], current_user) -> list[dict]
    # [{"item_id", "applied": bool, "reason": Optional[str]}]
```

### Backend: other

- `coa.spec_rules.sample_peptide_ids(db, parent_pks: list[int]) -> dict[int, Optional[int]]` has the exact semantics of `sample_peptide_id`, in one query.
- `models.EndoPrepRule` (table `endo_prep_rules`) has the columns of spec section 3.
- `WorksheetItem` gains:
  - `prep_rule: Optional[dict]` (JSON)
  - `prep_rules_off: bool` (default false, not null)
  - `prep_note: Optional[str]`
  - `prep_history: Optional[list]` (JSON)

### Routes

- **Rules:**
  - `GET /endo-prep-rules` returns `list[EndoPrepRuleOut]`, including inactive rules, ordered by `sort_order` then `id`. Each `EndoPrepRuleOut` is the snapshot keys plus `peptide_name: Optional[str]`, `cartridge_name: Optional[str]`, `updated_at`.
  - `POST /endo-prep-rules` (body `EndoPrepRuleIn`) returns `{"rule": EndoPrepRuleOut, "warnings": list[str]}` with 201.
  - `PATCH /endo-prep-rules/{rule_id}` (partial body) returns the same shape.
  - `POST /endo-prep-rules/reorder` with `{"ids": list[int]}` returns `list[EndoPrepRuleOut]`.
  - Audit operations are `endo_prep_rule_created`, `endo_prep_rule_updated` and `endo_prep_rules_reordered`.
- **Worksheet items:**
  - The item serializer adds `prep_rule`, `prep_rules_off`, `prep_note`, `prep_history` and `sample_peptide_id` to every item of a 2 mL endotoxin worksheet. `sample_peptide_id` comes from one batched query.
  - `PATCH /worksheets/{worksheet_id}/items/{item_id}` accepts `prep_rules_off` and `prep_note`.
  - An override of `prep_total_dilution`, `prep_steps`, `cartridge_type_id` or `prep_rules_off` on a row that has a result appends history (`why: "override"`) and writes the audit row `endo_prep_override`.
- **Re-apply:**
  - `GET /worksheets/{worksheet_id}/prep-rules/reapply` returns `{"candidates": [...]}`.
  - `POST /worksheets/{worksheet_id}/prep-rules/reapply` with `{"item_ids": list[int]}` returns `{"results": [...]}`.
  - Each applied row gets the audit row `endo_prep_rule_reapplied`.
- **History entries** use the shape of spec section 7: `at`, `by`, `why`, `lock`, `total_dilution`, `step1_ratio`, `cartridge_type_id`, `result_reading`, `reader_test_id`, `result_entered_at`.

### Frontend

**`src/lib/endo-prep-rules.ts`:**

```ts
export interface EndoPrepRule { id: number; peptide_id: number; min_mg: number | null; max_mg: number | null;
  total_dilution: number | null; step1_ratio: number | null; cartridge_type_id: number | null;
  status: 'trial' | 'validated'; note: string | null; sort_order: number; active: boolean }
export interface PrepRuleLock { rule: EndoPrepRule | null; locked_at: string;
  locked_by: 'print' | 'result' | 'complete' | 'reapply' | 'switch'; user_id: number | null }
export interface PrepHistoryEntry { at: string; by: number | null; why: 'reapply' | 'override';
  lock: PrepRuleLock | null; total_dilution: number; step1_ratio: number; cartridge_type_id: number | null;
  result_reading: string | null; reader_test_id: string | null; result_entered_at: string | null }
export function matchRule(rules: EndoPrepRule[], q: { peptideId: number | null; weightMg: number | null;
  isBac: boolean; endoPrep: string | null; rulesOff: boolean }): EndoPrepRule | null
export function rowRule(item: WorksheetItemRow, rules: EndoPrepRule[],
  q: { peptideId: number | null; weightMg: number | null; isBac: boolean; endoPrep: string | null }): EndoPrepRule | null
```

**`src/lib/api.ts`:**
- Client functions:
  - `getEndoPrepRules()`
  - `createEndoPrepRule(body)`
  - `updateEndoPrepRule(id, body)`
  - `reorderEndoPrepRules(ids)`
  - `getPrepRulesReapply(worksheetId)`
  - `applyPrepRulesReapply(worksheetId, itemIds)`
- `WorksheetItemRow` gains:
  - `prep_rule?: PrepRuleLock | null`
  - `prep_rules_off?: boolean`
  - `prep_note?: string | null`
  - `prep_history?: PrepHistoryEntry[] | null`
  - `sample_peptide_id?: number | null`

**Prep maths:**
- `EndoSample` gains `rule: EndoPrepRule | null`.
- `totalOf` reads `typed ?? rule.total_dilution ?? params.stdTotalDilution`.
- `stepsOf` reads step 1 as `typedR1 ?? rule.step1_ratio ?? STEP1_RATIO`.
- `resolveRow` reads the cartridge as typed item cartridge, then the rule's cartridge (when it is allowed), then the existing chain.

### Task 1: The rule model and the matcher (backend)

The `endo_prep_rules` table, its boot statement, the pure matcher of spec section 4, the batched peptide lookup the serializer needs (Task 3), and the shared fixture that pins the Python matcher to the TypeScript one (Task 6).

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/endo_prep_rules.py`
- Create: `backend/tests/fixtures/endo_prep_rule_cases.json`
- Test (create): `backend/tests/test_endo_prep_rules.py`
- Modify: `backend/models.py`, inserting `EndoPrepRule` above `class MethodAttachment(Base):` (about line 756).
- Modify: `backend/database.py` `_run_migrations`, inserting after the `lod_unit` statement (about line 2624), above the `retest_of_sample_id` statement.
- Modify: `backend/coa/spec_rules.py`, inserting `sample_peptide_ids` after `sample_peptide_id` (about line 163).
- Test (modify): `backend/tests/test_spec_rules.py`, changing the import (lines 14 and 15) and appending after the last test (about line 361).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `coa.spec_rules.sample_peptide_id(db, parent_pk) -> Optional[int]`: the semantics the twin copies.
  - `worksheets_endo.STEP1_STANDARD_RATIO` (10): test only; it pins `STEP1_RATIO`.
  - Test helpers: `tests.test_hplc_native_schema._captured()`, `tests.test_spec_rules._mk_peptide / _mk_service / _mk_identity_service / _mk_family`, `tests.test_endo_v2_worksheets._count_sql`, and the conftest `db_session` fixture (SQLite).
- Produces:
  - `models.EndoPrepRule`, table `endo_prep_rules`: `id`, `peptide_id` (FK `peptides.id`, not null, no ON DELETE), `min_mg`, `max_mg`, `total_dilution`, `step1_ratio` (floats, null), `cartridge_type_id` (FK `consumable_types.id`, null), `status` (`String(10)`, default `'trial'`), `note` (text), `active` (default true), `sort_order` (int, default 0), `created_at`, `updated_at`, `updated_by_id` (FK `users.id`, ON DELETE SET NULL).
  - In `endo_prep_rules`:
    - `STEP1_RATIO = 10`
    - `LOCK_EVENTS = ("print", "result", "complete", "reapply", "switch")`
    - `snapshot(rule) -> dict`, with exactly the keys `id, peptide_id, min_mg, max_mg, total_dilution, step1_ratio, cartridge_type_id, status, note, sort_order, active`
    - `load_rules(db, *, active_only=True) -> list[dict]`: snapshots ordered by `sort_order`, then `id`, in one query
    - `match_rule(rules, *, peptide_id, weight_mg, is_bac, endo_prep, rules_off) -> Optional[dict]`: it returns the dict from `rules` itself, not a copy, so a lock must copy it. A snapshot is flat, so `dict(rule)` is a full copy.
  - `coa.spec_rules.sample_peptide_ids(db, parent_pks: list[int]) -> dict[int, Optional[int]]`: one query; every requested pk is a key; an empty list returns `{}` with no query.
  - Fixture `backend/tests/fixtures/endo_prep_rule_cases.json`:
    - `rule_sets`: a dict of named lists of whole snapshots (snake_case), with `"main"` and `"empty"`.
    - `cases`: each is `{"name", "rules": <rule set name>, "query": {"peptide_id", "weight_mg", "is_bac", "endo_prep", "rules_off"}, "expect": <winning rule id or null>}`.
    - Query keys are the Python keyword arguments; Task 6 maps them to camelCase.
  - Matcher details Task 6 must mirror:
    - Only lines with `active` true match.
    - A row with no weight is `weight_mg is None` (TS `== null`). 0 mg is a weight.
    - The winner is the minimum of `(width, sort_order, id)`, where width = `(max_mg ?? Infinity) - (min_mg ?? 0)`.
    - `endo_prep !== '2ml'` (null included) is never ruled.

```json
    {"name": "c: Tesamorelin 10 mg", "rules": "main", "query": {"peptide_id": 2, "weight_mg": 10, "is_bac": false, "endo_prep": "2ml", "rules_off": false}, "expect": 3},
    {"name": "d: NAD+ 1000 mg", "rules": "main", "query": {"peptide_id": 3, "weight_mg": 1000, "is_bac": false, "endo_prep": "2ml", "rules_off": false}, "expect": 4},
    {"name": "e: the narrower band wins (Sermorelin 5 mg)", "rules": "main", "query": {"peptide_id": 4, "weight_mg": 5, "is_bac": false, "endo_prep": "2ml", "rules_off": false}, "expect": 5},
    {"name": "f: Sermorelin 50 mg falls to the any-weight line", "rules": "main", "query": {"peptide_id": 4, "weight_mg": 50, "is_bac": false, "endo_prep": "2ml", "rules_off": false}, "expect": 6},
    {"name": "a peptide with no line (Ipamorelin)", "rules": "main", "query": {"peptide_id": 5, "weight_mg": 10, "is_bac": false, "endo_prep": "2ml", "rules_off": false}, "expect": null},
    {"name": "no rules at all", "rules": "empty", "query": {"peptide_id": 2, "weight_mg": 10, "is_bac": false, "endo_prep": "2ml", "rules_off": false}, "expect": null},
    {"name": "a blend has no single catalog peptide", "rules": "main", "query": {"peptide_id": null, "weight_mg": 10, "is_bac": false, "endo_prep": "2ml", "rules_off": false}, "expect": null},
    {"name": "bac water is never ruled", "rules": "main", "query": {"peptide_id": 2, "weight_mg": 10, "is_bac": true, "endo_prep": "2ml", "rules_off": false}, "expect": null},
    {"name": "a stock worksheet is never ruled", "rules": "main", "query": {"peptide_id": 2, "weight_mg": 10, "is_bac": false, "endo_prep": "stock", "rules_off": false}, "expect": null},
    {"name": "a worksheet with no endo_prep counts as stock", "rules": "main", "query": {"peptide_id": 2, "weight_mg": 10, "is_bac": false, "endo_prep": null, "rules_off": false}, "expect": null},
    {"name": "a row switched to no rule", "rules": "main", "query": {"peptide_id": 2, "weight_mg": 10, "is_bac": false, "endo_prep": "2ml", "rules_off": true}, "expect": null},
    {"name": "an inactive line never matches", "rules": "main", "query": {"peptide_id": 6, "weight_mg": 10, "is_bac": false, "endo_prep": "2ml", "rules_off": false}, "expect": null},
    {"name": "two open bands tie on width: the lower sort_order wins", "rules": "main", "query": {"peptide_id": 7, "weight_mg": 60, "is_bac": false, "endo_prep": "2ml", "rules_off": false}, "expect": 9},
    {"name": "a closed band beats an open one listed before it", "rules": "main", "query": {"peptide_id": 8, "weight_mg": 60, "is_bac": false, "endo_prep": "2ml", "rules_off": false}, "expect": 11},
    {"name": "above the closed band only the open line matches", "rules": "main", "query": {"peptide_id": 8, "weight_mg": 600, "is_bac": false, "endo_prep": "2ml", "rules_off": false}, "expect": 10},
    {"name": "equal width and sort_order: the lower id wins", "rules": "main", "query": {"peptide_id": 9, "weight_mg": 18, "is_bac": false, "endo_prep": "2ml", "rules_off": false}, "expect": 12},
    {"name": "0 mg is a weight, not a missing one", "rules": "main", "query": {"peptide_id": 1, "weight_mg": 0, "is_bac": false, "endo_prep": "2ml", "rules_off": false}, "expect": 1}
  ]
}
```

```python
"""Endo slice 5, Task 1: the prep rule table, its boot statement and the
matcher (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-endo-slice5-method-rules-design.md
sections 3, 4 and 11). The shared fixture tests/fixtures/endo_prep_rule_cases.json
pins match_rule to the TypeScript matchRule (Task 6). In-memory SQLite
(`db_session`, tests/conftest.py)."""
import json
import re
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

import worksheets_endo
from endo_prep_rules import LOCK_EVENTS, STEP1_RATIO, load_rules, match_rule, snapshot
from models import EndoPrepRule
from tests.test_hplc_native_schema import _captured
from tests.test_spec_rules import _mk_peptide

FIXTURE = Path(__file__).parent / "fixtures" / "endo_prep_rule_cases.json"
DATA = json.loads(FIXTURE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", DATA["cases"], ids=[c["name"] for c in DATA["cases"]])
def test_match_rule_gives_the_fixture_answer(case):
    rule = match_rule(DATA["rule_sets"][case["rules"]], **case["query"])
    assert (rule["id"] if rule else None) == case["expect"]


def test_fixture_lines_are_whole_snapshots():
    # Exactly the snapshot keys, so the TypeScript side reads the fixture
    # lines as GET /endo-prep-rules serves them.
    for line in DATA["rule_sets"]["main"]:
        assert snapshot(EndoPrepRule(**line)) == line


def test_constants_match_the_contract_and_the_step1_floor_check():
    assert STEP1_RATIO == worksheets_endo.STEP1_STANDARD_RATIO == 10
    assert LOCK_EVENTS == ("print", "result", "complete", "reapply", "switch")


def test_a_new_rule_takes_the_table_defaults(db_session):
    pep = _mk_peptide(db_session, "RETA")
    rule = EndoPrepRule(peptide_id=pep.id)
    db_session.add(rule)
    db_session.flush()
    assert snapshot(rule) == {
        "id": rule.id, "peptide_id": pep.id, "min_mg": None, "max_mg": None,
        "total_dilution": None, "step1_ratio": None, "cartridge_type_id": None,
        "status": "trial", "note": None, "sort_order": 0, "active": True}
    assert rule.created_at is not None and rule.updated_by_id is None
    json.dumps(snapshot(rule))   # a lock stores it in a JSON column


def test_load_rules_lists_by_sort_order_then_id(db_session):
    pep = _mk_peptide(db_session, "RETA")

    def add(sort_order, active=True):
        rule = EndoPrepRule(peptide_id=pep.id, sort_order=sort_order, active=active,
                            total_dilution=300.0)
        db_session.add(rule)
        db_session.flush()
        return rule.id

    second = add(2)
    first = add(1)
    tie_low_id = add(3)
    tie_high_id = add(3)
    retired = add(0, active=False)
    db_session.commit()

    assert [r["id"] for r in load_rules(db_session)] == [first, second, tie_low_id, tie_high_id]
    assert [r["id"] for r in load_rules(db_session, active_only=False)] == [
        retired, first, second, tie_low_id, tie_high_id]
    assert load_rules(db_session)[0] == snapshot(db_session.get(EndoPrepRule, first))


def test_boot_migration_creates_the_table_above_the_pinned_last_statement():
    stmts = _captured()
    create = [s for s in stmts if "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS endo_prep_rules" in s]
    assert len(create) == 1
    # Every model column is in the statement, so the two cannot drift apart unseen.
    for column in EndoPrepRule.__table__.columns:
        assert re.search(rf"\b{column.name}\b", create[0]), column.name
    assert stmts.index(create[0]) < len(stmts) - 1
    assert stmts[-1] == "ALTER TABLE lims_samples ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS retest_of_sample_id TEXT"
```

```python
def test_sample_peptide_ids_answers_like_sample_peptide_id_in_one_query(db_session):
    """Every case above, batched: the worksheet serializer asks for a whole

    on_vial = _mk_family(db_session, "P-BATCH-2", sub_analyses=[svc_b])

    row.peptide_id, row.slot = pep_b.id, 1
    db_session.flush()
    pks = [p.id for p in (single, on_vial, blend, no_anchor, retracted, native, split)] + [987654]

    with _count_sql(db_session) as seen:
        got = sample_peptide_ids(db_session, pks + [single.id])   # a repeat is harmless

    assert len(seen) == 1
    assert got == {pk: sample_peptide_id(db_session, pk) for pk in pks}
    assert got == {single.id: pep_a.id, on_vial.id: pep_b.id, blend.id: None,
                   no_anchor.id: None, retracted.id: pep_a.id, native.id: pep_b.id,
                   split.id: None, 987654: None}


def test_sample_peptide_ids_of_nothing_runs_no_query(db_session):
```

The class:

```python
class EndoPrepRule(Base):
    """A 2 mL endotoxin prep rule (endo slice 5, spec 2026-10-14 section 3).
    For one catalog peptide and a weight band (weight > min_mg and <= max_mg,
    a null bound meaning none) it sets the row's default total dilution,
    step 1 ratio and cartridge; a null value means the standard. status is a
    label only (D4). Never deleted: active=False retires a line. Matching and
    the row lock read endo_prep_rules.snapshot(), never this row."""
    __tablename__ = "endo_prep_rules"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    # No ON DELETE, as analysis_service_specs.peptide_id: a peptide that has a
    # rule cannot be deleted.
    peptide_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("peptides.id"), nullable=False)
    min_mg: Mapped[Optional[float]] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    max_mg: Mapped[Optional[float]] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    total_dilution: Mapped[Optional[float]] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    step1_ratio: Mapped[Optional[float]] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    cartridge_type_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("consumable_types.id"), nullable=True)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(10), nullable=False, default="trial",
                                        server_default="trial")
    note: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=True,
                                         server_default=text("true"))
    sort_order: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0,
                                            server_default="0")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow,
                                                 onupdate=datetime.utcnow)
    updated_by_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True)

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return f"<EndoPrepRule(id={self.id}, peptide_id={self.peptide_id})>"
```

All the names it uses (`Float`, `Text`, `text`, `ForeignKey` and the rest) are already imported at the top of `models.py`.

The peptide FK has no ON DELETE, which is deliberate: it follows `analysis_service_specs.peptide_id`. See Drafting deviations (part A).

and above the comment `# HPLC-native slice 6 (M8): links a retest's sample row back to the`. Insert this:

```python
        id                SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
        peptide_id        INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES peptides(id),
        min_mg            DOUBLE PRECISION,

        total_dilution    DOUBLE PRECISION,

```

The statement contains no colon, because `test_boot_migration_statements_have_no_bindparams` refuses bind parameters. Its column list mirrors the model, and the Step 1 test pins every model column name in it.

Postgres behaviour was checked by reading, not run: the host dev Postgres must not get the table.
- `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` is a no-op on every later boot.
- On the first boot of the release that also ships v2, `consumable_types` does not exist yet when `_run_migrations` runs. The statement logs one `migration_skipped`, and `create_all` then builds the table from the model.
- Task 10 checks the table on the stack.

**From this task on, do not run these three files on the host:**
- `tests/test_sla_schema.py`
- `tests/test_priority_migration.py`
- `tests/test_priority_embed.py`

They call `database._run_migrations()` unpatched when imported, against the engine `DATABASE_URL` names (the host dev Postgres). They would create `endo_prep_rules` there, which the host must not get. They run in Task 10 on the stack.

Every other test that reads the boot list goes through the fake connection (`_captured`, `captured_migration_statements`), or executes only marker-filtered statements, so it never runs this one.

**3c. The matcher.** Create `backend/endo_prep_rules.py`:

```python
"""Endotoxin method prep rules (endo slice 5, spec
docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-endo-slice5-method-rules-design.md).

A rule sets a 2 mL row's default total dilution, step 1 ratio and cartridge
for one catalog peptide and a weight band. Outside the table a rule is always
a snapshot dict, so a row lock can hold a full copy that later edits never
reach. match_rule is mirrored line for line by src/lib/endo-prep-rules.ts
matchRule; backend/tests/fixtures/endo_prep_rule_cases.json pins the two to
identical answers.

worksheets_endo imports this module, so this module imports worksheets_endo
(and main) only inside functions."""
import math
from typing import Optional

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from models import EndoPrepRule

# Step 1's standard Tris ratio 1:N. Mirrors src/lib/endo-prep-2ml.ts
# STEP1_RATIO; tests/test_endo_prep_rules.py pins it to
# worksheets_endo.STEP1_STANDARD_RATIO.
STEP1_RATIO = 10
# What locked a row's rule (spec section 6).
LOCK_EVENTS = ("print", "result", "complete", "reapply", "switch")

_SNAPSHOT_KEYS = ("id", "peptide_id", "min_mg", "max_mg", "total_dilution", "step1_ratio",
                  "cartridge_type_id", "status", "note", "sort_order", "active")


def snapshot(rule) -> dict:
    """A rule row as a plain JSON-safe dict: what matching reads, what the
    rules API serves and what a row lock copies whole."""
    return {key: getattr(rule, key) for key in _SNAPSHOT_KEYS}


def load_rules(db: Session, *, active_only: bool = True) -> list[dict]:
    """Every rule as a snapshot, in list order (sort_order, then id). One query."""
    q = select(EndoPrepRule).order_by(EndoPrepRule.sort_order, EndoPrepRule.id)
    if active_only:
        q = q.where(EndoPrepRule.active.is_(True))
    return [snapshot(r) for r in db.execute(q).scalars()]


def _in_band(rule: dict, weight_mg: Optional[float]) -> bool:
    """weight > min_mg and weight <= max_mg, a null bound meaning none. A row
    with no weight sits only in a line with both bounds null."""
    lo, hi = rule["min_mg"], rule["max_mg"]
    if weight_mg is None:
        return lo is None and hi is None
    return (lo is None or weight_mg > lo) and (hi is None or weight_mg <= hi)


def _width(rule: dict) -> float:
    """(max_mg or infinity) - (min_mg or 0). Every open band is infinitely
    wide, so any closed band beats it (Dennis's quirk, spec section 4)."""
    hi = math.inf if rule["max_mg"] is None else rule["max_mg"]
    return hi - (rule["min_mg"] or 0)


def match_rule(rules: list[dict], *, peptide_id: Optional[int], weight_mg: Optional[float],
               is_bac: bool, endo_prep: Optional[str], rules_off: bool) -> Optional[dict]:
    """The active rule that sets this row's defaults, or None (spec section 4).
    Never ruled: a stock worksheet (endo_prep other than '2ml', NULL included),
    bac water, a sample with no single catalog peptide (a blend, or none) and
    a row switched to "no rule". Of several matching lines the narrowest band
    wins; a tie goes to the lowest sort_order, then the lowest id. Returns the
    snapshot from `rules` itself, not a copy."""
    if rules_off or is_bac or endo_prep != "2ml" or peptide_id is None:
        return None
    hits = [r for r in rules
            if r["active"] and r["peptide_id"] == peptide_id and _in_band(r, weight_mg)]
    if not hits:
        return None
    return min(hits, key=lambda r: (_width(r), r["sort_order"], r["id"]))
```

**3d. The batched peptide lookup.** In `backend/coa/spec_rules.py`, insert immediately after this anchor (the last line of `sample_peptide_id`), with two blank lines before the new function:

```python
    return ids[0] if len(ids) == 1 else None
```

this function. It sits above `def evaluate(`; `func`, `select` and `Optional` are already imported there.

```python
def sample_peptide_ids(db: Session, parent_pks: list[int]) -> dict[int, Optional[int]]:
    """sample_peptide_id for many parents in ONE query (endo slice 5: the
    worksheet item serializer, never one query per row). The same anchor,
    joins and retracted filter; each row counts for every requested parent it
    belongs to, through its own lims_sample_pk or its vial's parent, exactly
    as the single-parent OR does. Every requested pk is a key: its one
    distinct anchor, else None (none, or a blend). No pks, no query."""
    from models import AnalysisService, LimsAnalysis, LimsSubSample

    pks = sorted(set(parent_pks))
    if not pks:
        return {}
    anchors: dict[int, set] = {pk: set() for pk in pks}
    anchor = func.coalesce(LimsAnalysis.peptide_id, AnalysisService.peptide_id)
    rows = db.execute(
        select(LimsAnalysis.lims_sample_pk, LimsSubSample.parent_sample_pk, anchor)
        .select_from(AnalysisService)
        .join(LimsAnalysis, LimsAnalysis.analysis_service_id == AnalysisService.id)
        .outerjoin(LimsSubSample, LimsSubSample.id == LimsAnalysis.lims_sub_sample_pk)
        .where(
            anchor.is_not(None),
            LimsAnalysis.review_state != "retracted",
            LimsAnalysis.lims_sample_pk.in_(pks) | LimsSubSample.parent_sample_pk.in_(pks),
        )
        .distinct()
    ).all()
    for host_pk, vial_parent_pk, peptide_id in rows:
        for pk in (host_pk, vial_parent_pk):
            if pk in anchors:
                anchors[pk].add(peptide_id)
    return {pk: next(iter(ids)) if len(ids) == 1 else None for pk, ids in anchors.items()}
```

```bash
git -C /c/tmp/Accu-Mk1-endo-s3 commit -m "feat(endo-s5): prep rule table, matcher, shared fixture and batched peptide ids" -m "Co-Authored-By: Claude <Sonnet|Opus> 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>" -- backend/models.py backend/database.py backend/endo_prep_rules.py backend/coa/spec_rules.py backend/tests/fixtures/endo_prep_rule_cases.json backend/tests/test_endo_prep_rules.py backend/tests/test_spec_rules.py
```

The `add` puts the three new files in the index, so the pathspec commit can name them. Write the executing model's name in the trailer, as Global Constraints says.

### Task 2: The rules API (backend)

`GET`, `POST` and `PATCH /endo-prep-rules`, and `POST /endo-prep-rules/reorder`, with spec section 3's save checks (400s) and warnings, plus the audit rows.

**Choices this task pins (read before coding):**
- **Permission.** The dependency is `auth.get_current_user`: any signed-in user, with no role check. It is exactly what guards editing the consumables catalog today:
  - `POST /consumables`, main.py about line 4085;
  - `PATCH /consumables/{type_id}`, about line 4115.

  It also guards the Endotoxin method editor:
  - `PUT /hplc/methods/{method_id}`, about line 5032;
  - `PUT /hplc/methods/{method_id}/consumables`, about line 5248;
  - `POST /hplc/methods/{method_id}/new-revision`, about line 5304;
  - `POST /hplc/methods/{method_id}/activate`, about line 5366.

  The actor is `getattr(current_user, "id", None)`, the `catalog/change_log.py` idiom.
- **Audit.**
  - Rows go to `audit_logs` (`models.AuditLog`), because the contract's operation names (`endo_prep_rule_created`, `endo_prep_rule_updated`, `endo_prep_rules_reordered`) are `AuditLog.operation` values.
  - `catalog_change_log`, the consumables trail, has only an `action` column.
  - `details` uses the endotoxin module's idiom, `{"user_id", "old", "new"}`, as in `worksheets_endo.pin_endo_method` and `apply_item_endo_fields`. `old` and `new` are whole `snapshot()` dicts, or id lists for a reorder.
- **"The active Endotoxin method revision".**
  - A rule belongs to no worksheet, so `resolve_endo_method` cannot serve this check.
  - `endo_prep_rules._active_endo_methods` takes every active method that is the `is_default` method of an endotoxin service: `main._bench_kind_for_keyword(keyword) == "endo"`, the rule `worksheets_endo._endo_method_id` uses.
  - It returns them in the `endo_method` wire shape (`worksheets_endo._method_payload`), and the allowed set is `worksheets_endo.cartridge_ids_allowed_by`.
  - When no such method exists, nothing is warned and the standard total is Dennis's 250 (`endo_run_params(None)`). That is prod until launch step sets the method 26 default.
- **The step 1 floor.** It follows `check_item_endo_fields`:
  - refused when `total < 2 x ratio`;
  - the total is the rule's, else the active method's `std_total_dilution` (the smallest, if several);
  - the ratio is the rule's, else `STEP1_RATIO`.
- **Checks run on the line as it will be stored, for the fields sent.** A PATCH merges the sent fields over the stored snapshot. Each check runs only when a field it reads was sent. A line whose cartridge was retired later can therefore still be deactivated or re-noted, and so can a line whose ratio a new standard no longer fits.
- **Warnings** come only for an active line:
  - its band overlaps another active line of the same peptide, where `(a1, b1]` and `(a2, b2]` overlap if `a1 < b2 and a2 < b1`, with a null bound as minus or plus infinity;
  - its cartridge is not allowed by an active Endotoxin method.
- **`sort_order`** is in neither body:
  - a POST appends at `max + 1`, or 0 on an empty table;
  - reorder needs every rule id, inactive ones included, exactly once, and sets `sort_order` to each id's position;
  - the rules page (Task 7) sends the full list.
- **Route tests run on SQLite.** The pattern exists in `tests/test_endo_v2_worksheets.py`: an in-memory `StaticPool` engine plus `app.dependency_overrides` for `get_db` and `get_current_user` (`MagicMock(id=1)`). `tests/test_endo_worksheet_promote.py` copies those two fixtures and imports the helpers, and this task does the same. Nothing touches the host dev Postgres, so no test here waits for Task 10. Never write `with TestClient(app)`: it runs the lifespan, whose `init_db` writes to the host Postgres.

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/endo_prep_rules.py`, appending `check_rule` and its helpers after `match_rule`.
- Modify: `backend/main.py`:
  - add one import line after line 65;
  - add the models and routes above `# ─── Analysis Service Endpoints ───` (about line 4161).
- Test (create): `backend/tests/test_endo_prep_rules_api.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - From Task 1: `endo_prep_rules.snapshot`, `load_rules` and `STEP1_RATIO`, and `models.EndoPrepRule`.
  - From `worksheets_endo`: `ENDO_CARTRIDGE`, `_method_payload(db, method_id)`, `cartridge_ids_allowed_by(db, method)` and `endo_run_params(run_params)`.
  - From `main`: `_bench_kind_for_keyword(keyword)` and `_blank_to_none(value)`.
  - `auth.get_current_user` and `models.AuditLog`.
  - Test helpers: `tests.test_endo_v2_worksheets._cartridge / _count_sql / _method / _service` and `tests.test_spec_rules._mk_peptide`.
- Produces:
  - `endo_prep_rules.check_rule(db, values: dict, sent, *, rule_id: Optional[int] = None) -> list[str]`. It raises `ValueError(reason)` on a refusal and returns the warnings.
  - `endo_prep_rules.OVERLAP_RULE`: the overlap warning's tail, which says how `match_rule` settles an overlap.
  - The routes, all guarded by `Depends(get_current_user)`:
    - `GET /endo-prep-rules` returns `list[EndoPrepRuleOut]`: every rule, inactive ones included, ordered by `sort_order` then `id`, in one query.
    - `POST /endo-prep-rules` takes an `EndoPrepRuleIn` body and returns 201 with `EndoPrepRuleSaveOut`, `{"rule": EndoPrepRuleOut, "warnings": list[str]}`.
    - `PATCH /endo-prep-rules/{rule_id}` takes an `EndoPrepRulePatch` body and returns 200 with `EndoPrepRuleSaveOut`. An unknown id is a 404, `"prep rule {id} not found"`.
    - `POST /endo-prep-rules/reorder` takes `{"ids": list[int]}` (`EndoPrepRuleReorderIn`) and returns `list[EndoPrepRuleOut]`.
  - `EndoPrepRuleIn` (the POST body):
    - `peptide_id: int`, required;
    - `min_mg`, `max_mg`, `total_dilution` and `step1_ratio`: `Optional[float]`;
    - `cartridge_type_id: Optional[int]`;
    - `status`: `"trial"` (the default) or `"validated"`; anything else is 422;
    - `note: Optional[str]`, with a blank note stored as null;
    - `active: bool = True`.
  - `EndoPrepRulePatch` has the same fields, all optional:
    - a field that is sent is set;
    - an explicit null clears it back to the standard;
    - an omitted field is untouched;
    - a null `peptide_id`, `status` or `active` is a 400.
  - `EndoPrepRuleOut` has the snapshot keys plus `peptide_name: Optional[str]` (`peptides.name`), `cartridge_name: Optional[str]` and `updated_at`.
  - The 400 texts, exactly. `{x:g}` formatting gives, for example, `1:15`.
    - `"{field} cannot be empty"`
    - `"{field} must be a finite number"`
    - `"peptide {id} not found"`
    - `"total_dilution must be greater than zero"`
    - `"step1_ratio must be at least 1"`
    - `"min_mg must be below max_mg"`
    - `"cartridge {id} is not an active endotoxin cartridge"`
    - `"total_dilution 1:{total:g} is below 2 x the step 1 ratio 1:{ratio:g}, so step 2 cannot be made"`
    - `"step1_ratio 1:{ratio:g} needs a total of at least 1:{floor:g}, above the standard 1:{std:g}; set total_dilution too"`
    - `"ids must list every rule exactly once"`
  - The warning texts:
    - `"overlaps rule {id} ({band}) for this peptide; where both match, the narrower band wins (an open band is the widest) and a tie goes to the line listed first"`. Here `{band}` is one of `any weight`, `up to {max:g} mg`, `above {min:g} mg` or `above {min:g} up to {max:g} mg`.
    - The tail states the rule, not a winner. Two open bands, such as `above 15 mg` against `any weight`, go to the line listed first, and a test pins that case.
    - `"cartridge '{name}' is not allowed by the active method '{method}' rev {n}; its rows keep their usual cartridge"`.
  - The audit rows, all with `entity_type="endo_prep_rule"`:
    - `endo_prep_rule_created`: `entity_id` is the rule id; details `{"user_id", "old": None, "new": snapshot}`.
    - `endo_prep_rule_updated`: `entity_id` is the rule id; details `{"user_id", "old": snapshot, "new": snapshot}`. It is written only when the snapshot changed, and the PATCH then also sets `updated_by_id`.
    - `endo_prep_rules_reordered`: `entity_id` is None; details `{"user_id", "old": [ids], "new": [ids]}`. It is written only when the order changed.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `backend/tests/test_endo_prep_rules_api.py`:

```python
"""Endo slice 5, Task 2: the prep rules API (spec
docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-endo-slice5-method-rules-design.md section 3;
plan Review Focus 4 and 5): list, create, edit, reorder, every save check
with its 400 text, the two warnings, the audit rows and the sign-in guard.
In-memory SQLite + dependency overrides with test_endo_v2_worksheets.py's
helpers. Never `with TestClient(app)`: that runs the lifespan, whose init_db
writes to the host Postgres."""
from unittest.mock import MagicMock

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from auth import get_current_user
from database import Base, get_db
from main import app
from endo_prep_rules import OVERLAP_RULE
from models import AuditLog, EndoPrepRule
from tests.test_endo_v2_worksheets import _cartridge, _count_sql, _method, _service
from tests.test_spec_rules import _mk_peptide

SNAPSHOT_KEYS = ("id", "peptide_id", "min_mg", "max_mg", "total_dilution", "step1_ratio",
                 "cartridge_type_id", "status", "note", "sort_order", "active")


@pytest.fixture
def db():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
                           poolclass=StaticPool)
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    session = sessionmaker(bind=engine)()
    try:
        yield session
    finally:
        session.close()


@pytest.fixture
def client(db):
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: db
    app.dependency_overrides[get_current_user] = lambda: MagicMock(id=1)
    yield TestClient(app)
    app.dependency_overrides.clear()


@pytest.fixture
def reta(db):
    pep = _mk_peptide(db, "RETA", name="Retatrutide")
    db.commit()
    return pep


def _post(client, **body):
    return client.post("/endo-prep-rules", json=body)


def _refused(res, detail):
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["detail"]) == (400, detail)


def _audits(db, operation):
    return db.execute(select(AuditLog).where(AuditLog.operation == operation)
                      .order_by(AuditLog.id)).scalars().all()


# --- list and create ----------------------------------------------------------


def test_create_appends_a_line_and_the_list_serves_it(client, db, reta):
    assert client.get("/endo-prep-rules").json() == []
    c01 = _cartridge(db, "Endosafe 0.01", 0.01)
    db.commit()

    res = _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, max_mg=30, status="validated",
                note="1:250 fine to 30 mg")
    assert res.status_code == 201
    assert res.json()["warnings"] == []
    first = res.json()["rule"]
    assert first["updated_at"]
    assert {k: v for k, v in first.items() if k != "updated_at"} == {
        "id": first["id"], "peptide_id": reta.id, "min_mg": None, "max_mg": 30.0,
        "total_dilution": None, "step1_ratio": None, "cartridge_type_id": None,
        "status": "validated", "note": "1:250 fine to 30 mg", "sort_order": 0, "active": True,
        "peptide_name": "Retatrutide", "cartridge_name": None}

    second = _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, min_mg=30, total_dilution=300,
                   cartridge_type_id=c01.id, note="  ").json()["rule"]
    assert (second["sort_order"], second["status"], second["note"], second["cartridge_name"]) == (
        1, "trial", None, "Endosafe 0.01")
    assert client.get("/endo-prep-rules").json() == [first, second]

    # Inactive lines stay on the list (there is no delete).
    client.patch(f"/endo-prep-rules/{first['id']}", json={"active": False})
    assert [r["active"] for r in client.get("/endo-prep-rules").json()] == [False, True]


def test_the_list_is_one_query(client, db, reta):
    c01 = _cartridge(db, "Endosafe 0.01", 0.01)
    db.commit()
    for lo in (0, 10, 20):
        _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, min_mg=lo, max_mg=lo + 10, cartridge_type_id=c01.id)
    with _count_sql(db) as seen:
        assert len(client.get("/endo-prep-rules").json()) == 3
    assert len(seen) == 1


def test_every_route_needs_a_signed_in_user(client, reta):
    app.dependency_overrides.pop(get_current_user)
    assert client.get("/endo-prep-rules").status_code == 401
    assert _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id).status_code == 401
    assert client.patch("/endo-prep-rules/1", json={"note": "x"}).status_code == 401
    assert client.post("/endo-prep-rules/reorder", json={"ids": []}).status_code == 401


# --- save checks ----------------------------------------------------------------


@pytest.mark.parametrize("body, detail", [
    ({"peptide_id": 987654}, "peptide 987654 not found"),
    ({"total_dilution": 0}, "total_dilution must be greater than zero"),
    ({"total_dilution": -300}, "total_dilution must be greater than zero"),
    ({"step1_ratio": 0.5}, "step1_ratio must be at least 1"),
    ({"min_mg": 30, "max_mg": 30}, "min_mg must be below max_mg"),
    ({"min_mg": 40, "max_mg": 30}, "min_mg must be below max_mg"),
    # Review Focus 5: the step 1 floor (total >= 2 x ratio).
    ({"total_dilution": 15},
     "total_dilution 1:15 is below 2 x the step 1 ratio 1:10, so step 2 cannot be made"),
    ({"total_dilution": 100, "step1_ratio": 60},
     "total_dilution 1:100 is below 2 x the step 1 ratio 1:60, so step 2 cannot be made"),
    ({"step1_ratio": 130},
     "step1_ratio 1:130 needs a total of at least 1:260, above the standard 1:250; "
     "set total_dilution too"),
    ({"cartridge_type_id": 987654}, "cartridge 987654 is not an active endotoxin cartridge"),
])
def test_create_refuses_with_the_reason(client, db, reta, body, detail):
    _refused(_post(client, **{"peptide_id": reta.id, **body}), detail)
    assert db.execute(select(EndoPrepRule)).first() is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize("raw, detail", [
    ('"total_dilution": Infinity', "total_dilution must be a finite number"),
    ('"min_mg": NaN', "min_mg must be a finite number"),
])
def test_create_refuses_a_number_that_is_not_finite(client, reta, raw, detail):
    # Raw JSON: httpx's json= refuses NaN and Infinity, FastAPI's parser accepts them.
    res = client.post("/endo-prep-rules", content=f'{{"peptide_id": {reta.id}, {raw}}}',
                      headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    _refused(res, detail)


def test_create_refuses_a_retired_or_non_endo_cartridge(client, db, reta):
    retired = _cartridge(db, "Endosafe old", 0.05, active=False)
    tip = _cartridge(db, "Pipette tip", None, kind="pipette_tip")
    db.commit()
    for ct in (retired, tip):
        _refused(_post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, cartridge_type_id=ct.id),
                 f"cartridge {ct.id} is not an active endotoxin cartridge")


def test_status_is_trial_or_validated(client, reta):
    assert _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, status="final").status_code == 422


def test_the_floor_itself_and_an_all_standard_line_save(client, reta):
    # Exactly 2 x 1:10 can be made; a line with no values confirms the standard.
    assert _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, max_mg=10, total_dilution=20).status_code == 201
    assert _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, min_mg=10).status_code == 201


def test_a_ratio_without_a_total_meets_the_active_methods_standard(client, db, reta):
    _method(client, db, "Endotoxin", _service(db),
            run_parameters={"endotoxin": {"std_total_dilution": 300}})
    # 1:140 needs 1:280: too much for Dennis's 1:250, fine for this method's 1:300.
    assert _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, max_mg=10, step1_ratio=140).status_code == 201
    _refused(_post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, min_mg=10, step1_ratio=160),
             "step1_ratio 1:160 needs a total of at least 1:320, above the standard 1:300; "
             "set total_dilution too")


# --- warnings -------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_an_overlapping_band_saves_with_a_warning(client, db, reta):
    assert OVERLAP_RULE == ("for this peptide; where both match, the narrower band wins (an open "
                            "band is the widest) and a tie goes to the line listed first")
    # Review Focus 4 and 5: Dennis's e (up to 10 mg) and f (any weight) overlap.
    e = _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, max_mg=10, total_dilution=400).json()
    assert e["warnings"] == []
    f = _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, total_dilution=300)
    assert f.status_code == 201
    assert f.json()["warnings"] == [f"overlaps rule {e['rule']['id']} (up to 10 mg) {OVERLAP_RULE}"]
    # Dennis's a and b meet at 30 mg without overlapping.
    tesa = _mk_peptide(db, "TESA", name="Tesamorelin")
    db.commit()
    assert _post(client, peptide_id=tesa.id, max_mg=30).json()["warnings"] == []
    assert _post(client, peptide_id=tesa.id, min_mg=30, total_dilution=300).json()["warnings"] == []
    # An inactive line overlaps nothing, and an inactive line is not warned about.
    assert client.patch(f"/endo-prep-rules/{e['rule']['id']}",
                        json={"active": False}).json()["warnings"] == []
    assert client.patch(f"/endo-prep-rules/{f.json()['rule']['id']}",
                        json={"note": "any weight"}).json()["warnings"] == []


def test_the_overlap_warning_names_every_overlapping_band(client, db, reta):
    anyw = _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, total_dilution=300).json()["rule"]["id"]
    mid = _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, min_mg=10, max_mg=20).json()["rule"]["id"]
    res = _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, min_mg=15, total_dilution=400)
    # Above 15 mg the any-weight line wins (both open, it is listed first): the
    # warning says how an overlap is settled, not that this line wins.
    assert res.json()["warnings"] == [
        f"overlaps rule {anyw} (any weight) {OVERLAP_RULE}",
        f"overlaps rule {mid} (above 10 up to 20 mg) {OVERLAP_RULE}"]
    above = res.json()["rule"]["id"]
    res = client.patch(f"/endo-prep-rules/{mid}", json={"note": "checked"})
    assert res.json()["warnings"] == [
        f"overlaps rule {anyw} (any weight) {OVERLAP_RULE}",
        f"overlaps rule {above} (above 15 mg) {OVERLAP_RULE}"]


def test_a_cartridge_the_active_method_does_not_allow_saves_with_a_warning(client, db, reta):
    c05 = _cartridge(db, "Endosafe 0.05", 0.05)
    c01 = _cartridge(db, "Endosafe 0.01", 0.01)
    _method(client, db, "Endotoxin", _service(db), [(c05, True)])
    assert _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, max_mg=10,
                 cartridge_type_id=c05.id).json()["warnings"] == []
    res = _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, min_mg=10, cartridge_type_id=c01.id)
    assert res.status_code == 201
    assert res.json()["warnings"] == [
        "cartridge 'Endosafe 0.01' is not allowed by the active method 'Endotoxin' rev 1; "
        "its rows keep their usual cartridge"]


def test_no_cartridge_warning_without_an_endo_method_or_when_it_lists_none(client, db, reta):
    c05 = _cartridge(db, "Endosafe 0.05", 0.05)
    c01 = _cartridge(db, "Endosafe 0.01", 0.01)
    db.commit()
    assert _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, max_mg=10,
                 cartridge_type_id=c01.id).json()["warnings"] == []
    # A non-endotoxin method's cartridges say nothing about endotoxin rows.
    _method(client, db, "BPC-157 purity", _service(db, "BPC157-PURITY"), [(c05, True)])
    # An endotoxin method with no cartridge list allows every active one.
    _method(client, db, "Endotoxin", _service(db))
    assert _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, min_mg=10,
                 cartridge_type_id=c01.id).json()["warnings"] == []


# --- edit -----------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_patch_changes_only_what_it_sends_and_checks_the_stored_line(client, reta):
    rid = _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, min_mg=30, total_dilution=300,
                note="60 mg PPC low").json()["rule"]["id"]
    res = client.patch(f"/endo-prep-rules/{rid}", json={"total_dilution": 400, "status": "validated"})
    assert res.status_code == 200
    rule = res.json()["rule"]
    assert (rule["min_mg"], rule["total_dilution"], rule["status"], rule["note"]) == (
        30.0, 400.0, "validated", "60 mg PPC low")
    # An explicit null clears a value back to the standard.
    res = client.patch(f"/endo-prep-rules/{rid}", json={"total_dilution": None})
    assert res.json()["rule"]["total_dilution"] is None
    # The band check reads the stored bound beside the sent one.
    _refused(client.patch(f"/endo-prep-rules/{rid}", json={"max_mg": 20}),
             "min_mg must be below max_mg")
    # The floor reads the stored total beside a sent ratio.
    client.patch(f"/endo-prep-rules/{rid}", json={"total_dilution": 300})
    _refused(client.patch(f"/endo-prep-rules/{rid}", json={"step1_ratio": 200}),
             "total_dilution 1:300 is below 2 x the step 1 ratio 1:200, so step 2 cannot be made")
    for field in ("peptide_id", "status", "active"):
        _refused(client.patch(f"/endo-prep-rules/{rid}", json={field: None}),
                 f"{field} cannot be empty")
    res = client.patch("/endo-prep-rules/987654", json={"note": "x"})
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["detail"]) == (404, "prep rule 987654 not found")


def test_a_line_whose_cartridge_was_retired_can_still_be_deactivated(client, db, reta):
    c01 = _cartridge(db, "Endosafe 0.01", 0.01)
    db.commit()
    rid = _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, cartridge_type_id=c01.id).json()["rule"]["id"]
    c01.active = False
    db.commit()
    res = client.patch(f"/endo-prep-rules/{rid}", json={"active": False, "note": "cartridge retired"})
    assert res.status_code == 200 and res.json()["rule"]["active"] is False
    _refused(client.patch(f"/endo-prep-rules/{rid}", json={"cartridge_type_id": c01.id}),
             f"cartridge {c01.id} is not an active endotoxin cartridge")


# --- audit and reorder -------------------------------------------------------------


def test_create_and_update_are_audited_with_old_and_new(client, db, reta):
    first = _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, max_mg=30).json()["rule"]
    second = _post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, min_mg=30, total_dilution=300).json()["rule"]
    created = _audits(db, "endo_prep_rule_created")
    assert [(a.entity_type, a.entity_id) for a in created] == [
        ("endo_prep_rule", str(first["id"])), ("endo_prep_rule", str(second["id"]))]
    assert created[0].details == {"user_id": 1, "old": None,
                                  "new": {k: first[k] for k in SNAPSHOT_KEYS}}

    client.patch(f"/endo-prep-rules/{second['id']}", json={"total_dilution": 350})
    client.patch(f"/endo-prep-rules/{second['id']}", json={"total_dilution": 350})   # no change
    updated = _audits(db, "endo_prep_rule_updated")
    assert len(updated) == 1
    assert updated[0].entity_id == str(second["id"])
    assert updated[0].details == {
        "user_id": 1, "old": {k: second[k] for k in SNAPSHOT_KEYS},
        "new": {**{k: second[k] for k in SNAPSHOT_KEYS}, "total_dilution": 350.0}}
    assert db.get(EndoPrepRule, second["id"]).updated_by_id == 1


def test_reorder_sets_the_list_order_and_needs_every_rule_once(client, db, reta):
    ids = [_post(client, peptide_id=reta.id, min_mg=lo, max_mg=lo + 10).json()["rule"]["id"]
           for lo in (0, 10, 20)]
    client.patch(f"/endo-prep-rules/{ids[0]}", json={"active": False})   # ordered too
    order = [ids[2], ids[0], ids[1]]

    res = client.post("/endo-prep-rules/reorder", json={"ids": order})
    assert res.status_code == 200
    assert [(r["id"], r["sort_order"]) for r in res.json()] == [(ids[2], 0), (ids[0], 1), (ids[1], 2)]
    assert [r["id"] for r in client.get("/endo-prep-rules").json()] == order
    audits = _audits(db, "endo_prep_rules_reordered")
    assert [(a.entity_type, a.entity_id, a.details) for a in audits] == [
        ("endo_prep_rule", None, {"user_id": 1, "old": ids, "new": order})]

    for bad in ([ids[2], ids[0]], [ids[2], ids[0], ids[1], ids[1]], [ids[2], ids[0], 987654]):
        _refused(client.post("/endo-prep-rules/reorder", json={"ids": bad}),
                 "ids must list every rule exactly once")
    # The current order again changes nothing and writes no audit row.
    assert client.post("/endo-prep-rules/reorder", json={"ids": order}).status_code == 200
    assert len(_audits(db, "endo_prep_rules_reordered")) == 1
```

Leave two blank lines, then add:

```python
_REQUIRED_FIELDS = ("peptide_id", "status", "active")
# How match_rule settles an overlap, in the rules page's words (spec section 4).
OVERLAP_RULE = ("for this peptide; where both match, the narrower band wins (an open band "
                "is the widest) and a tie goes to the line listed first")
_NUMBER_FIELDS = ("min_mg", "max_mg", "total_dilution", "step1_ratio")


def _bounds(rule: dict) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """The band as an interval (lo, hi], a null bound as minus or plus infinity."""
    return (-math.inf if rule["min_mg"] is None else rule["min_mg"],
            math.inf if rule["max_mg"] is None else rule["max_mg"])


def _band_text(rule: dict) -> str:
    """The band in the rules page's words (its "above mg" and "up to mg" columns)."""
    lo, hi = rule["min_mg"], rule["max_mg"]
    if lo is None and hi is None:
        return "any weight"
    if lo is None:
        return f"up to {hi:g} mg"
    if hi is None:
        return f"above {lo:g} mg"
    return f"above {lo:g} up to {hi:g} mg"


def _active_endo_methods(db: Session) -> list[dict]:
    """The active Endotoxin method revisions: every active method that is the
    default for an endotoxin service (main's keyword rule, as
    worksheets_endo._endo_method_id reads it), in the endo_method wire shape.
    Usually one, and none until the launch step sets the default."""
    from main import _bench_kind_for_keyword
    from models import AnalysisService, HplcMethod, method_services
    from worksheets_endo import _method_payload

    rows = db.execute(
        select(method_services.c.method_id, AnalysisService.keyword)
        .join(AnalysisService, AnalysisService.id == method_services.c.analysis_service_id)
        .join(HplcMethod, HplcMethod.id == method_services.c.method_id)
        .where(method_services.c.is_default.is_(True), HplcMethod.status == "active")
    ).all()
    ids = sorted({mid for mid, keyword in rows if _bench_kind_for_keyword(keyword) == "endo"})
    return [_method_payload(db, mid) for mid in ids]


def check_rule(db: Session, values: dict, sent, *, rule_id: Optional[int] = None) -> list[str]:
    """Spec section 3's save checks on a rule as it will be stored. `values`
    holds every snapshot field after this request; `sent` names the fields
    the request sent. A refusal raises ValueError with the reason (the routes
    answer 400). Each check runs only when a field it reads was sent, so a
    line whose cartridge was retired, or whose ratio a new standard no longer
    fits, can still be deactivated or re-noted. The step 1 floor is
    check_item_endo_fields' rule on the effective values: total >= 2 x ratio,
    with the active method's standard total (250 when none resolves) and
    STEP1_RATIO standing in for an unset value. Returns the warnings, for an
    active line only: a band that overlaps another active line of the same
    peptide, and a cartridge an active Endotoxin method does not allow."""
    from models import ConsumableType, Peptide
    from worksheets_endo import ENDO_CARTRIDGE, cartridge_ids_allowed_by, endo_run_params

    sent = set(sent)
    for field in _REQUIRED_FIELDS:
        if field in sent and values[field] is None:
            raise ValueError(f"{field} cannot be empty")
    for field in _NUMBER_FIELDS:
        if field in sent and values[field] is not None and not math.isfinite(values[field]):
            raise ValueError(f"{field} must be a finite number")
    if "peptide_id" in sent and db.get(Peptide, values["peptide_id"]) is None:
        raise ValueError(f"peptide {values['peptide_id']} not found")
    total, ratio = values["total_dilution"], values["step1_ratio"]
    if "total_dilution" in sent and total is not None and total <= 0:
        raise ValueError("total_dilution must be greater than zero")
    if "step1_ratio" in sent and ratio is not None and ratio < 1:
        raise ValueError("step1_ratio must be at least 1")
    lo, hi = values["min_mg"], values["max_mg"]
    if sent & {"min_mg", "max_mg"} and lo is not None and hi is not None and lo >= hi:
        raise ValueError("min_mg must be below max_mg")
    cartridge_id = values["cartridge_type_id"]
    cartridge = db.get(ConsumableType, cartridge_id) if cartridge_id is not None else None
    if "cartridge_type_id" in sent and cartridge_id is not None and (
            cartridge is None or cartridge.kind != ENDO_CARTRIDGE or not cartridge.active):
        raise ValueError(f"cartridge {cartridge_id} is not an active endotoxin cartridge")
    methods = _active_endo_methods(db)
    if sent & {"total_dilution", "step1_ratio"}:
        step1 = STEP1_RATIO if ratio is None else ratio
        floor = 2 * step1
        if total is not None and total < floor:
            raise ValueError(f"total_dilution 1:{total:g} is below 2 x the step 1 ratio "
                             f"1:{step1:g}, so step 2 cannot be made")
        if total is None:
            std = min([endo_run_params(m["run_parameters"])["std_total_dilution"] for m in methods]
                      or [endo_run_params(None)["std_total_dilution"]])
            if std < floor:
                raise ValueError(f"step1_ratio 1:{step1:g} needs a total of at least 1:{floor:g}, "
                                 f"above the standard 1:{std:g}; set total_dilution too")
    if not values["active"]:
        return []
    mine_lo, mine_hi = _bounds(values)
    warnings = [
        f"overlaps rule {other['id']} ({_band_text(other)}) {OVERLAP_RULE}"
        for other in load_rules(db)
        if other["peptide_id"] == values["peptide_id"] and other["id"] != rule_id
        and mine_lo < _bounds(other)[1] and _bounds(other)[0] < mine_hi
    ]
    if cartridge is not None:
        warnings += [
            f"cartridge '{cartridge.name}' is not allowed by the active method "
            f"'{m['name']}' rev {m['revision']}; its rows keep their usual cartridge"
            for m in methods if cartridge_id not in cartridge_ids_allowed_by(db, m)
        ]
    return warnings
```

this block, and leave two blank lines between its end and the anchor. `Literal`, `Optional`, `datetime`, `BaseModel`, `select`, `func`, `Peptide`, `ConsumableType`, `AuditLog` and `_blank_to_none` are already in scope there.

```python
# ─── Endotoxin prep rules (endo slice 5, spec 2026-10-14 section 3) ───
#
# The same permission as the consumables catalog and the method editor above:
# any signed-in user (get_current_user). Audited in audit_logs with old and
# new snapshots, the worksheets_endo.py idiom.


class EndoPrepRuleIn(BaseModel):
    """A rule as the rules page creates it. sort_order is never sent: a new
    line goes to the end of the list, and POST /endo-prep-rules/reorder moves it."""
    peptide_id: int
    min_mg: Optional[float] = None
    max_mg: Optional[float] = None
    total_dilution: Optional[float] = None
    step1_ratio: Optional[float] = None
    cartridge_type_id: Optional[int] = None
    status: Literal["trial", "validated"] = "trial"
    note: Optional[str] = None
    active: bool = True


class EndoPrepRulePatch(BaseModel):
    """PATCH body: sent = set, an explicit null clears (back to the standard),
    omitted = untouched. peptide_id, status and active cannot be cleared."""
    peptide_id: Optional[int] = None
    min_mg: Optional[float] = None
    max_mg: Optional[float] = None
    total_dilution: Optional[float] = None
    step1_ratio: Optional[float] = None
    cartridge_type_id: Optional[int] = None
    status: Optional[Literal["trial", "validated"]] = None
    note: Optional[str] = None
    active: Optional[bool] = None


class EndoPrepRuleOut(BaseModel):
    """endo_prep_rules.snapshot() plus what the page shows. Every key is
    declared, because response_model drops undeclared ones."""
    id: int
    peptide_id: int
    min_mg: Optional[float] = None
    max_mg: Optional[float] = None
    total_dilution: Optional[float] = None
    step1_ratio: Optional[float] = None
    cartridge_type_id: Optional[int] = None
    status: str
    note: Optional[str] = None
    sort_order: int
    active: bool
    peptide_name: Optional[str] = None
    cartridge_name: Optional[str] = None
    updated_at: Optional[datetime] = None


class EndoPrepRuleSaveOut(BaseModel):
    rule: EndoPrepRuleOut
    warnings: list[str]


class EndoPrepRuleReorderIn(BaseModel):
    ids: list[int]


def _endo_prep_rules_out(db: Session, rule_id: Optional[int] = None) -> list[EndoPrepRuleOut]:
    """Every rule (or the one rule_id names) with its peptide and cartridge
    names, inactive ones included, in list order (sort_order, then id). One query."""
    from endo_prep_rules import snapshot
    q = (select(EndoPrepRule, Peptide.name, ConsumableType.name)
         .outerjoin(Peptide, Peptide.id == EndoPrepRule.peptide_id)
         .outerjoin(ConsumableType, ConsumableType.id == EndoPrepRule.cartridge_type_id)
         .order_by(EndoPrepRule.sort_order, EndoPrepRule.id))
    if rule_id is not None:
        q = q.where(EndoPrepRule.id == rule_id)
    return [EndoPrepRuleOut(**snapshot(rule), peptide_name=peptide_name,
                            cartridge_name=cartridge_name, updated_at=rule.updated_at)
            for rule, peptide_name, cartridge_name in db.execute(q).all()]


@app.get("/endo-prep-rules", response_model=list[EndoPrepRuleOut])
async def list_endo_prep_rules(db: Session = Depends(get_db),
                               _current_user=Depends(get_current_user)):
    """Every prep rule, inactive ones included, in list order."""
    return _endo_prep_rules_out(db)


@app.post("/endo-prep-rules", response_model=EndoPrepRuleSaveOut, status_code=201)
async def create_endo_prep_rule(data: EndoPrepRuleIn, db: Session = Depends(get_db),
                                current_user=Depends(get_current_user)):
    """Add a line at the end of the list. A refusal is a 400 with the reason;
    the warnings come back with the saved line (spec section 3)."""
    from endo_prep_rules import check_rule, snapshot
    fields = data.model_dump()
    fields["note"] = _blank_to_none(fields["note"])
    try:
        warnings = check_rule(db, {**fields, "id": None}, fields)
    except ValueError as e:
        raise HTTPException(400, str(e))
    user_id = getattr(current_user, "id", None)
    next_order = db.execute(
        select(func.coalesce(func.max(EndoPrepRule.sort_order), -1) + 1)).scalar_one()
    rule = EndoPrepRule(**fields, sort_order=next_order, updated_by_id=user_id)
    db.add(rule)
    db.flush()
    db.add(AuditLog(operation="endo_prep_rule_created", entity_type="endo_prep_rule",
                    entity_id=str(rule.id),
                    details={"user_id": user_id, "old": None, "new": snapshot(rule)}))
    db.commit()
    return {"rule": _endo_prep_rules_out(db, rule.id)[0], "warnings": warnings}


@app.patch("/endo-prep-rules/{rule_id}", response_model=EndoPrepRuleSaveOut)
async def update_endo_prep_rule(rule_id: int, data: EndoPrepRulePatch,
                                db: Session = Depends(get_db),
                                current_user=Depends(get_current_user)):
    """Edit or (de)activate a line; there is no delete. Only the fields sent
    change. The checks run on the line as it will be stored, and a save that
    changes nothing writes no audit row."""
    from endo_prep_rules import check_rule, snapshot
    rule = db.get(EndoPrepRule, rule_id)
    if rule is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, f"prep rule {rule_id} not found")
    fields = data.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)
    if "note" in fields:
        fields["note"] = _blank_to_none(fields["note"])
    old = snapshot(rule)
    try:
        warnings = check_rule(db, {**old, **fields}, fields, rule_id=rule_id)
    except ValueError as e:
        raise HTTPException(400, str(e))
    for name, value in fields.items():
        setattr(rule, name, value)
    new = snapshot(rule)
    if new != old:
        user_id = getattr(current_user, "id", None)
        rule.updated_by_id = user_id
        db.add(AuditLog(operation="endo_prep_rule_updated", entity_type="endo_prep_rule",
                        entity_id=str(rule.id),
                        details={"user_id": user_id, "old": old, "new": new}))
    db.commit()
    return {"rule": _endo_prep_rules_out(db, rule_id)[0], "warnings": warnings}


@app.post("/endo-prep-rules/reorder", response_model=list[EndoPrepRuleOut])
async def reorder_endo_prep_rules(data: EndoPrepRuleReorderIn, db: Session = Depends(get_db),
                                  current_user=Depends(get_current_user)):
    """Set the list order, the matcher's tie-break (spec section 4). ids lists
    every rule, inactive ones included, exactly once; position i becomes
    sort_order i. Sending the current order changes nothing."""
    rules = db.execute(select(EndoPrepRule)
                       .order_by(EndoPrepRule.sort_order, EndoPrepRule.id)).scalars().all()
    old = [r.id for r in rules]
    if sorted(data.ids) != sorted(old):
        raise HTTPException(400, "ids must list every rule exactly once")
    if data.ids != old:
        user_id = getattr(current_user, "id", None)
        by_id = {r.id: r for r in rules}
        for position, rule_id in enumerate(data.ids):
            if by_id[rule_id].sort_order != position:
                by_id[rule_id].sort_order = position
                by_id[rule_id].updated_by_id = user_id
        db.add(AuditLog(operation="endo_prep_rules_reordered", entity_type="endo_prep_rule",
                        entity_id=None,
                        details={"user_id": user_id, "old": old, "new": data.ids}))
        db.commit()
    return _endo_prep_rules_out(db)
```

**Interfaces:**

  - `endo_prep_rules.STEP1_RATIO`, `endo_prep_rules.snapshot(rule)` and `endo_prep_rules.load_rules(db)`.

- Keeps: `worksheets_endo.STEP1_STANDARD_RATIO`. Task 1's `test_constants_match_the_contract_and_the_step1_floor_check` pins `STEP1_RATIO` to it. Only the now-unused `_step1_ratio` goes.
- Consumes (existing code, reused, nothing moved):
  - `worksheets_endo_promote._sample_is_bac(item, parent, peptide_names)` and `_served_peptide_names(db, items)`: the bac rule with its identity fallback.
  - `main._worksheet_item_parent_facts(parent)["declared_weight_mg"]`: the declared weight, parsed from `LimsSample.declared_total_quantity`.
  - `main._bench_kind_for_item(role, analyses_json)`: the endotoxin-row test that also builds the serializer's `endo_item_ids`.
- Produces:

Every worksheet defaults to `"2ml"`, HPLC and PCR included, so the endotoxin-row test is what keeps the keys off other benches.
  - The item PATCH accepts `prep_rules_off` (true or false; null is a 400) and `prep_note` (trimmed; blank clears; at most 200 characters; NUL or invalid Unicode is a 400 that does not echo the text). A completed worksheet answers 409 for both. Both are audited in the existing `endo_prep_inputs_set` row.

```python
"""Endo slice 5 on the worksheet rows (spec
```
