```python
                           poolclass=StaticPool)
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    session = sessionmaker(bind=engine)()
    try:
        yield session
    finally:
        session.close()


@pytest.fixture
def client(db):
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: db
    app.dependency_overrides[get_current_user] = lambda: MagicMock(id=1, email="qa@accumark.test")
    yield TestClient(app)
    app.dependency_overrides.clear()


def _peptide(db, abbreviation="RETA", name="Retatrutide"):
    return _mk_peptide(db, abbreviation, name)


def _anchor(db, parent_sample_id, peptide):
    """Give the parent its catalog peptide: a family analysis on a
    peptide-linked service, which coa.spec_rules.sample_peptide_ids reads."""
    parent = db.execute(
        select(LimsSample).where(LimsSample.sample_id == parent_sample_id)).scalar_one()
    svc = _mk_identity_service(db, peptide.id, f"{peptide.abbreviation}-PURITY")
    db.add(LimsAnalysis(lims_sample_pk=parent.id, analysis_service_id=svc.id, keyword=svc.keyword,
                        title=svc.title, review_state="unassigned", provenance="canonical"))
    db.flush()


def _rule(db, peptide, *, sort_order=1, **values):
    """An active trial line for `peptide`, for any weight unless bounds are given."""
    rule = EndoPrepRule(peptide_id=peptide.id, status="trial", active=True,
                        sort_order=sort_order, **values)
    db.add(rule)
    db.flush()
    return rule


def _lock(rule, locked_by="result"):
    """A lock snapshot as lock_rows writes it (spec 6)."""
    return {"rule": endo_prep_rules.snapshot(rule) if rule is not None else None,
            "locked_at": "2026-10-14T09:00:00Z", "locked_by": locked_by, "user_id": 1}


# --- Task 3: the row columns --------------------------------------------------

_S5_DDL = [
    "ALTER TABLE worksheet_items ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS prep_rule JSON",
    "ALTER TABLE worksheet_items ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS prep_rules_off BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE",
    "ALTER TABLE worksheet_items ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS prep_note TEXT",
    "ALTER TABLE worksheet_items ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS prep_history JSON",
]


def test_the_row_columns_exist_with_their_defaults(db):
    cols = {c["name"] for c in inspect(db.get_bind()).get_columns("worksheet_items")}
    assert {"prep_rule", "prep_rules_off", "prep_note", "prep_history"} <= cols
    ws = _ws(db)
    item, _ = _vial_item(db, ws, 1, _service(db))
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(item)
    assert (item.prep_rule, item.prep_rules_off, item.prep_note, item.prep_history) == (
        None, False, None, None)


def test_boot_migrations_add_the_row_columns_above_the_last_statement():
    stmts = _captured()
    assert [s for s in stmts if s in _S5_DDL] == _S5_DDL
    assert stmts[-1] == "ALTER TABLE lims_samples ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS retest_of_sample_id TEXT"


# --- Task 3: a row's rule and its effective values ---------------------------

def _r(**fields):
    """A row as row_rule and effective_values read it."""
    return SimpleNamespace(**{"prep_total_dilution": None, "prep_steps": None,
                              "cartridge_type_id": None, "prep_rule": None,
                              "prep_rules_off": False, **fields})


_LINE = {"id": 7, "peptide_id": 3, "min_mg": None, "max_mg": None, "total_dilution": 300.0,
         "step1_ratio": 15.0, "cartridge_type_id": 42, "status": "trial", "note": None,
         "sort_order": 1, "active": True}


def test_effective_values_typed_beats_rule_beats_standard_per_field():
    ev = endo_prep_rules.effective_values
    none = {"total_dilution": False, "step1_ratio": False, "cartridge_type_id": False}
    assert ev(_r(), None, std_total=250) == {
        "total_dilution": 250, "step1_ratio": 10, "cartridge_type_id": None, "ruled": none}
    assert ev(_r(), _LINE, std_total=250) == {
        "total_dilution": 300.0, "step1_ratio": 15.0, "cartridge_type_id": 42,
        "ruled": dict.fromkeys(none, True)}
    # A typed total and cartridge win; step 1 with only its sample uL keeps the rule's ratio.
    assert ev(_r(prep_total_dilution=500, prep_steps=[[30], None, None], cartridge_type_id=5),
              _LINE, std_total=250) == {
        "total_dilution": 500, "step1_ratio": 15.0, "cartridge_type_id": 5,
        "ruled": {"total_dilution": False, "step1_ratio": True, "cartridge_type_id": False}}
    assert ev(_r(prep_steps=[[20, 4], None, None]), _LINE, std_total=250)["step1_ratio"] == 4.0
    # A line's null value leaves that field at the standard; a total of 0 is no total.
    assert ev(_r(prep_total_dilution=0), {**_LINE, "total_dilution": None},
              std_total=500)["total_dilution"] == 500


def test_row_rule_reads_the_switch_then_the_lock_then_todays_match(monkeypatch):
    asked = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(endo_prep_rules, "match_rule", lambda rules, **q: asked.append(q) or _LINE)
    q = {"rules": [], "peptide_id": 3, "weight_mg": 10.0, "is_bac": False, "endo_prep": "2ml"}
    assert endo_prep_rules.row_rule(_r(), **q) == _LINE
    assert asked == [{"peptide_id": 3, "weight_mg": 10.0, "is_bac": False, "endo_prep": "2ml",
                      "rules_off": False}]
    locked = {"rule": {**_LINE, "total_dilution": 400.0}, "locked_at": "2026-10-14T09:00:00Z",
              "locked_by": "print", "user_id": 1}
    assert endo_prep_rules.row_rule(_r(prep_rule=locked), **q)["total_dilution"] == 400.0
    assert endo_prep_rules.row_rule(_r(prep_rule={**locked, "rule": None}), **q) is None
    assert endo_prep_rules.row_rule(_r(prep_rules_off=True, prep_rule=locked), **q) is None
    assert len(asked) == 1  # a lock or the switch never asks for today's match


def test_row_facts_reads_peptide_weight_and_bac_for_endotoxin_rows_only(client, db):
    pep = _peptide(db)
    svc = _service(db)
    ws = _ws(db)
    plain, _ = _vial_item(db, ws, 1, svc)          # P-3001, 10 mg declared
    weighed, _ = _vial_item(db, ws, 2, svc)
    weighed.prep_weight_mg = 12.5
    bac_form, _ = _vial_item(db, ws, 3, svc)
    bac_form.prep_form = "bac"
    hplc, _ = _vial_item(db, ws, 4, _service(db, "BPC157-PURITY"), role="hplc")
    _anchor(db, "P-3001", pep)
    db.commit()
    assert endo_prep_rules.row_facts(db, ws, [plain, weighed, bac_form, hplc]) == {
        plain.id: {"peptide_id": pep.id, "weight_mg": 10.0, "is_bac": False},
        weighed.id: {"peptide_id": pep.id, "weight_mg": 12.5, "is_bac": False},
        bac_form.id: {"peptide_id": pep.id, "weight_mg": 10.0, "is_bac": True},
    }
    counts = []
    for items in ([plain], [plain, weighed, bac_form, hplc]):
        with _count_sql(db) as seen:
            endo_prep_rules.row_facts(db, ws, items)
        counts.append(len(seen))
    assert counts[0] == counts[1] <= 4, counts


def test_row_facts_reads_bac_off_the_sample_and_no_peptide_for_a_blend(client, db):
    w = _world(client, db)
    bw, _ = _row(db, w, "BW-0100-S01", sample_type="Bacteriostatic Water", reading=None)
    blend, _ = _row(db, w, "P-4001-S01", reading=None)
    _anchor(db, "P-4001", _peptide(db, "RETA", "Retatrutide"))
    _anchor(db, "P-4001", _peptide(db, "TESA", "Tesamorelin"))
    db.commit()
    assert endo_prep_rules.row_facts(db, w.ws, [bw, blend]) == {
        bw.id: {"peptide_id": None, "weight_mg": None, "is_bac": True},
        blend.id: {"peptide_id": None, "weight_mg": None, "is_bac": False},
    }


# --- Task 3: the serializer and the PATCH fields -------------------------------

_RULE_KEYS = ("prep_rule", "prep_rules_off", "prep_note", "prep_history", "sample_peptide_id")


def test_2ml_endotoxin_items_carry_the_rule_keys(client, db):
    pep = _peptide(db)
    svc = _service(db)
    ws = _ws(db)
    item, _ = _vial_item(db, ws, 1, svc)
    hplc, _ = _vial_item(db, ws, 2, _service(db, "BPC157-PURITY"), role="hplc")
    stock = _ws(db, "Stock run")
    stock.endo_prep = "stock"
    _vial_item(db, stock, 3, svc)
    _anchor(db, "P-3001", pep)
    item.prep_note = "special: 1:500 for PPC"
    db.commit()
    items = {it["id"]: it for it in client.get(f"/worksheets/{ws.id}").json()["items"]}
    assert {k: items[item.id][k] for k in _RULE_KEYS} == {
        "prep_rule": None, "prep_rules_off": False, "prep_note": "special: 1:500 for PPC",
        "prep_history": None, "sample_peptide_id": pep.id}
    # Other benches and stock worksheets are never ruled: no new keys.
    assert not set(_RULE_KEYS) & set(items[hplc.id])
    [stock_item] = client.get(f"/worksheets/{stock.id}").json()["items"]
    assert not set(_RULE_KEYS) & set(stock_item)
    listed = next(w for w in client.get("/worksheets").json() if w["id"] == ws.id)
    assert {k: listed["items"][0][k] for k in _RULE_KEYS} == {
        k: items[item.id][k] for k in _RULE_KEYS}


def test_the_peptide_lookup_is_one_query_whatever_the_row_count(client, db):
    svc = _service(db)
    small, big = _ws(db, "Small"), _ws(db, "Big")
    for n in range(1, 3):
        _vial_item(db, small, n, svc)
    for n in range(3, 11):
        _vial_item(db, big, n, svc)
    _anchor(db, "P-3001", _peptide(db))
    db.commit()
    counts = []
    for ws_id in (small.id, big.id):
        db.expire_all()
        with _count_sql(db) as seen:
            assert client.get(f"/worksheets/{ws_id}").status_code == 200
        counts.append(len(seen))
    assert counts[0] == counts[1], counts


def test_patch_sets_the_switch_and_the_note_and_audits_them(client, db):
    ws, item, _, _ = _row_world(db)
    url = f"/worksheets/{ws.id}/items/{item.id}"
    r = client.patch(url, json={"prep_rules_off": True, "prep_note": "  special: 1:500 for PPC  "})
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    db.refresh(item)
    assert (item.prep_rules_off, item.prep_note) == (True, "special: 1:500 for PPC")
    # A blank note clears it.
    assert client.patch(url, json={"prep_note": "   "}).status_code == 200
    db.refresh(item)
    assert item.prep_note is None
    assert [(a.details["old"], a.details["new"]) for a in db.query(AuditLog).filter(
        AuditLog.operation == "endo_prep_inputs_set").order_by(AuditLog.id)] == [
        ({"prep_rules_off": False, "prep_note": None},
         {"prep_rules_off": True, "prep_note": "special: 1:500 for PPC"}),
        ({"prep_note": "special: 1:500 for PPC"}, {"prep_note": None}),
    ]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("raw", [
    '{"prep_rules_off": null}',
    '{"prep_note": "' + "x" * 201 + '"}',
    '{"prep_note": "a\\u0000b"}',
    '{"prep_note": "\\ud800"}',      # a lone surrogate: a 400 that never echoes it
])
def test_patch_refuses_a_bad_switch_or_note_and_writes_nothing(client, db, raw):
    ws, item, _, _ = _row_world(db)
    r = client.patch(f"/worksheets/{ws.id}/items/{item.id}", content=raw,
                     headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    assert r.status_code == 400, r.text
    db.refresh(item)
    assert (item.prep_rules_off, item.prep_note) == (False, None)


def test_a_completed_worksheet_refuses_the_switch_and_the_note(client, db):
    ws, item, _, _ = _row_world(db)
    ws.status = "completed"
    db.commit()
    for payload in ({"prep_rules_off": True}, {"prep_note": "special"}):
        assert client.patch(f"/worksheets/{ws.id}/items/{item.id}",
                            json=payload).status_code == 409, payload


# --- Task 3: the server checks read effective values -------------------------

def _ruled_world(db, **line):
    """_row_world's open worksheet and endo vial (P-3001, 10 mg declared, no
    method: the standard total is 250), the parent anchored to Retatrutide,
    and one Retatrutide line for any weight."""
    ws, item, c05, c01 = _row_world(db)
    pep = _peptide(db)
    _anchor(db, "P-3001", pep)
    rule = _rule(db, pep, **line)
    db.commit()
    return SimpleNamespace(ws=ws, item=item, c05=c05, c01=c01, pep=pep, rule=rule,
                           url=f"/worksheets/{ws.id}/items/{item.id}")


def _floor(n):
    return (f"prep_total_dilution must be at least {n} (the step 1 ratio x the fixed 1:2 step) "
            "or step 2 cannot be made")


def test_the_floor_check_reads_the_rules_total(client, db):
    w = _ruled_world(db, total_dilution=300)
    # 1:140 x 2 = 280: makeable at the rule's 1:300, not at the standard 1:250.
    assert client.patch(w.url, json={"prep_steps": [[20, 140], None, None]}).status_code == 200
    r = client.patch(w.url, json={"prep_steps": [[20, 160], None, None]})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["detail"]) == (400, _floor(320))


def test_the_floor_check_reads_the_rules_step1_ratio(client, db):
    w = _ruled_world(db, step1_ratio=50)
    r = client.patch(w.url, json={"prep_total_dilution": 90})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["detail"]) == (400, _floor(100))
    assert client.patch(w.url, json={"prep_total_dilution": 100}).status_code == 200


def test_switching_a_row_to_no_rule_checks_its_floor_at_the_standard(client, db):
    w = _ruled_world(db, total_dilution=300)
    assert client.patch(w.url, json={"prep_steps": [[20, 140], None, None]}).status_code == 200
    r = client.patch(w.url, json={"prep_rules_off": True})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["detail"]) == (400, _floor(280))
    db.refresh(w.item)
    assert w.item.prep_rules_off is False


def test_the_floor_check_reads_a_locked_line_never_one_edited_since(client, db):
    w = _ruled_world(db, total_dilution=300)
    w.item.prep_rule = _lock(w.rule, "print")
    w.rule.total_dilution = 200      # edited after the lock
    db.commit()
    # The locked 1:300 still makes 1:140 (280); today's 1:200 would not.
    assert client.patch(w.url, json={"prep_steps": [[20, 140], None, None]}).status_code == 200
    # A row locked to the standard reads 250 whatever the lines say.
    w.item.prep_rule = _lock(None, "print")
    db.commit()
    r = client.patch(w.url, json={"prep_steps": [[20, 140], None, None]})
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["detail"]) == (400, _floor(280))


def test_a_ruled_row_promotes_on_its_locked_total_and_a_tampered_value_is_refused(client, db):
    """Review Focus 3: the promote check (slice 3 I-3) reads the locked
    line's total. 0.05 x 300 x 2 = 30 EU per vial; the standard 1:250 gives 25."""
    w = _world(client, db)
    item, _ = _row(db, w)                 # P-4001-S01, "<0.05" on the 0.05
    pep = _peptide(db)
    _anchor(db, "P-4001", pep)
    rule = _rule(db, pep, total_dilution=300)
    item.prep_rule = _lock(rule)
    db.commit()
    assert _results(_promote(client, w.ws, _promote_line(item))) == [_refused(item, BAD)]
    rule.total_dilution = 400             # an edit after the lock never reaches the check
    db.commit()
    [res] = _results(_promote(client, w.ws, _promote_line(item, 30, loq=30)))
    assert res["promoted"] is True, res
    audit = db.query(AuditLog).filter(AuditLog.operation == "endo_worksheet_promoted").one()
    assert (audit.details["total_dilution"], audit.details["loq"]) == (300, 30)
```

```python


# --- A row's rule and its effective values (spec sections 4 and 5) ----------

# The three values a rule sets on a row; snapshot() and effective_values()
# use the same names.
_VALUE_FIELDS = ("total_dilution", "step1_ratio", "cartridge_type_id")


def _typed_values(item) -> dict:
    """The row's own values (spec 5), None where it types none: a total
    above zero (the browser's totalOf), step 1's ratio prep_steps[0][1]
    (stepsOf) and the row's cartridge."""
    first = item.prep_steps[0] if isinstance(item.prep_steps, list) and item.prep_steps else None
    ratio = first[1] if isinstance(first, list) and len(first) == 2 else None
    return {
        "total_dilution": (item.prep_total_dilution
                           if (item.prep_total_dilution or 0) > 0 else None),
        # float(): a huge int ratio (10**308 passed _finite) would make an int
        # floor that the :g message cannot format (500); as a float it is inf.
        "step1_ratio": float(ratio) if ratio is not None else None,
        "cartridge_type_id": item.cartridge_type_id,
    }


def row_rule(item, *, rules: list[dict], peptide_id: Optional[int], weight_mg: Optional[float],
             is_bac: bool, endo_prep: Optional[str]) -> Optional[dict]:
    """The line a row computes with (spec 5). None when the row is switched
    to "no rule". A locked row's own copy of its line (None: locked to the
    standard), so a later edit never reaches it. Else today's match."""
    if item.prep_rules_off:
        return None
    if item.prep_rule is not None:
        return item.prep_rule.get("rule")
    return match_rule(rules, peptide_id=peptide_id, weight_mg=weight_mg, is_bac=is_bac,
                      endo_prep=endo_prep, rules_off=False)


def effective_values(item, rule: Optional[dict], *, std_total: float) -> dict:
    """A row's total dilution, step 1 ratio and cartridge (spec 5), field by
    field: typed beats rule beats standard. `rule` is row_rule's answer. The
    standard is the method's std_total_dilution, STEP1_RATIO, and for the
    cartridge None, meaning the usual chain (bac water's 0.01, the run's,
    the method default), which callers resolve as before. `ruled` names
    the values the rule supplied."""
    typed = _typed_values(item)
    standard = {"total_dilution": std_total, "step1_ratio": STEP1_RATIO,
                "cartridge_type_id": None}
    ruled = {f: typed[f] is None and (rule or {}).get(f) is not None for f in _VALUE_FIELDS}
    values = {f: typed[f] if typed[f] is not None else rule[f] if ruled[f] else standard[f]
              for f in _VALUE_FIELDS}
    return {**values, "ruled": ruled}


def row_facts(db: Session, worksheet, items) -> dict[int, dict]:
    """What matching reads off each endotoxin row (spec 4), item id ->
    {"peptide_id", "weight_mg", "is_bac"}:
    - the parent's single catalog peptide (coa.spec_rules.sample_peptide_ids;
      None for a blend or none);
    - the weight as Box 2 reads it, prep_weight_mg else the parent's
      declared weight (main._worksheet_item_parent_facts);
    - bac water as the browser's formOf reads it: the row's form, else the
      sample's bac rule (worksheets_endo_promote._sample_is_bac, identity
      fallback included).
    Rows of other benches are left out: they are never ruled or locked. A
    vial row's parent is the vial's; a legacy bare parent id is its own
    (main._serialize_worksheets' _parent_for). At most four queries whatever
    the row count, one of them the batched peptide lookup."""
    # Lazy: main and worksheets_endo_promote import this module.
    from main import _bench_kind_for_item, _worksheet_item_parent_facts
    from worksheets_endo import ENDO_FORMS
    from worksheets_endo_promote import _sample_is_bac, _served_peptide_names

    items = list(items)
    sample_ids = {it.sample_id for it in items if it.sample_id}
    if not sample_ids:
        return {}
    vials = {r.sample_id: r for r in db.execute(
        select(LimsSubSample.sample_id, LimsSubSample.parent_sample_pk,
               LimsSubSample.assignment_role)
        .where(LimsSubSample.sample_id.in_(sample_ids))).all()}
    endo = [it for it in items if _bench_kind_for_item(
        vials[it.sample_id].assignment_role if it.sample_id in vials else None,
        it.analyses_json) == "endo"]
    if not endo:
        return {}
    parent_pks = {vials[it.sample_id].parent_sample_pk for it in endo
                  if it.sample_id in vials} - {None}
    parents = db.execute(select(LimsSample).where(or_(
        LimsSample.id.in_(parent_pks or [0]),
        LimsSample.sample_id.in_({it.sample_id for it in endo})))).scalars().all()
    by_pk = {p.id: p for p in parents}
    by_sample_id = {p.sample_id: p for p in parents}

    def parent_of(it):
        pk = vials[it.sample_id].parent_sample_pk if it.sample_id in vials else None
        return by_pk.get(pk) if pk else by_sample_id.get(it.sample_id)

    parent_by_item = {it.id: parent_of(it) for it in endo}
    pks = sorted({p.id for p in parent_by_item.values() if p is not None})
    peptide_by_parent = sample_peptide_ids(db, pks) if pks else {}
    names = _served_peptide_names(db, endo)
    out = {}
    for it in endo:
        parent = parent_by_item[it.id]
        sample_bac = _sample_is_bac(it, parent, names.get(it.id, []))
        out[it.id] = {
            "peptide_id": peptide_by_parent.get(parent.id) if parent is not None else None,
            "weight_mg": (it.prep_weight_mg if it.prep_weight_mg is not None
                          else _worksheet_item_parent_facts(parent)["declared_weight_mg"]),
            "is_bac": it.prep_form == "bac" if it.prep_form in ENDO_FORMS else sample_bac,
        }
    return out
```

```python
# The endo v2 row fields the item PATCH accepts (spec 3.7). Sent = set; an
# explicit null clears; omitted = untouched (model_fields_set).
_PREP_FIELDS = ("prep_form", "prep_water_ml", "prep_total_dilution", "prep_steps")
ENDO_ITEM_FIELDS = (*_PREP_FIELDS, "cartridge_type_id", "result_reading")
# The fixed parts of the 2 mL steps that stay in code (spec 4.4): step 1's
# standard ratio and the fixed 1:2 of step 3.
STEP1_STANDARD_RATIO = 10
STEP3_RATIO = 2


def _step1_ratio(steps) -> float:
    """The row's step 1 ratio: its own (prep_steps[0][1]) when set, else 1:10."""
    first = steps[0] if isinstance(steps, list) and steps else None
    if isinstance(first, list) and len(first) == 2 and first[1] is not None:
        # float(): a huge int ratio (10**308 passed _finite) would make an int
        # floor that the :g message cannot format (500); as a float it is inf.
        return float(first[1])
    return STEP1_STANDARD_RATIO


def check_item_endo_fields(db: Session, worksheet, item, values: dict) -> dict:
    """Validate the row fields one PATCH sent. Writes nothing. Returns the
    values to store (a reading trimmed) or raises ValueError with the reason.
    Saving a reading always stores the row's cartridge (spec 3.1), so a
    reading needs one, sent now or already on the row. For the same reason a
    cartridge cannot be cleared from under a stored reading. A total below
    step 1's ratio x 2 cannot be made, because step 2 would need a ratio
    under 1:1. A row with no total of its own runs on the method's standard
    total (std_total_dilution, 250 when unset), so that one is checked the
    same way. Tube overflow is only a screen warning."""
```

with the block below:
- `_step1_ratio` goes. Nothing else used it, and its logic moves to `endo_prep_rules._typed_values`.
- `STEP1_STANDARD_RATIO` and `STEP3_RATIO` stay. Task 1's test pins `endo_prep_rules.STEP1_RATIO` to `STEP1_STANDARD_RATIO`.

```python
# The endo v2 row fields the item PATCH accepts (spec 3.7), with slice 5's
# "no rule" switch and special-prep note (spec 2026-10-14 sections 6 and 8).
# Sent = set; an explicit null clears; omitted = untouched (model_fields_set).
_PREP_FIELDS = ("prep_form", "prep_water_ml", "prep_total_dilution", "prep_steps",
                "prep_rules_off", "prep_note")
ENDO_ITEM_FIELDS = (*_PREP_FIELDS, "cartridge_type_id", "result_reading")
# The fixed parts of the 2 mL steps that stay in code (spec 4.4): step 1's
# standard ratio (endo_prep_rules.STEP1_RATIO mirrors it; a row's rule can
# set its own, slice 5) and the fixed 1:2 of step 3.
STEP1_STANDARD_RATIO = 10
STEP3_RATIO = 2
# A special-prep note prints on the row of the bench sheet.
_PREP_NOTE_MAX = 200


def check_item_endo_fields(db: Session, worksheet, item, values: dict) -> dict:
    """Validate the row fields one PATCH sent. Writes nothing. Returns the
    values to store (a reading and a note trimmed) or raises ValueError with
    the reason. Saving a reading always stores the row's cartridge (spec
    3.1), so a reading needs one, sent now or already on the row. For the
    same reason a cartridge cannot be cleared from under a stored reading. A
    total below step 1's ratio x 2 cannot be made, because step 2 would need
    a ratio under 1:1. Both are the row's effective values (slice 5, spec 5):
    its own, else its rule's (the locked line, else today's match, none when
    switched to "no rule"), else the standard (the method's
    std_total_dilution, 250 when unset; step 1 at 1:10). So the floor is
    checked again when the switch moves. Tube overflow is only a screen
    warning."""
```

### Task 4: Lock events, release, override history and the promoted-row guard

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/endo_prep_rules.py` (import block; append at the end)
- Modify: `backend/worksheets_endo.py` (after `ENDO_ITEM_FIELDS` around line 371; `apply_item_endo_fields` around lines 434 to 473)
- Modify: `backend/main.py`:
  - `complete_worksheet`, around line 25252;
  - `record_worksheet_printed`, around line 25506;
  - `import_endo_reader_results`, around line 25665;
  - `update_worksheet_item`, around line 25908.
- Test: `backend/tests/test_endo_prep_rule_rows.py` (imports; append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 3's `row_facts`, `row_rule` and `effective_values`.
  - Task 1's `match_rule`, `load_rules` and `LOCK_EVENTS`.
  - Slice 3's `worksheets_endo_promote.vial_endo_analyses(db, sample_ids)`.
- Produces:
  - `promoted_sample_ids(db, sample_ids) -> set[str]`. It returns the rows' sample ids whose vial's live endotoxin analysis is promoted (slice 3's promoted state), in one query. Task 5's Re-apply refusal uses the same function.
  - `worksheets_endo.PREP_FINAL_FIELDS = ("prep_total_dilution", "prep_steps", "cartridge_type_id", "prep_rules_off", "prep_note")` and `worksheets_endo.PREP_FINAL = "the row is promoted; its prep is final"`.
  - The item PATCH answers 409 with detail `PREP_FINAL` when it would change one of those fields on a promoted row (spec 8, the Handler's ruling on deviation B4). Readings and every other field keep today's behaviour, and sending a field's stored value again is no change.
  - `lock_rows(db, worksheet, items, *, event, user_id) -> int`
    - It locks each endotoxin row that is not locked and not `prep_rules_off`, to today's match, or to `"rule": None` when nothing matches.
    - It does nothing on a stock worksheet (`endo_prep != "2ml"`) or a completed one.
    - An unknown event raises `ValueError`.
    - It does not commit.
  - `release_on_clear(item) -> None`. It clears `prep_rule` only when `locked_by == "result"`. The item PATCH calls it only while the sheet has never been printed (deviation B8).
  - `append_history(item, *, why, user_id, effective) -> None`. It appends `{at, by, why, lock, total_dilution, step1_ratio, cartridge_type_id, result_reading, reader_test_id, result_entered_at}` to a new list.
  - Private helpers that Task 5 reuses: `_iso_now()` and `_new_lock(rule, event, user_id)`.
- Lock hooks:
  - `POST /worksheets/{id}/printed` (`"print"`, all rows, every print);
  - the item PATCH's reading save (`"result"`, that row);
  - `POST /worksheets/{id}/reader-import` (`"result"`, the rows it wrote);
  - `POST /worksheets/{id}/complete` (`"complete"`, all rows, before the status turns `completed`).
- The switch:
  - Turning `prep_rules_off` on drops the row's lock.
  - Turning it off again locks the row at once (`"switch"`) when the sheet has been printed or the row has a result.
- Overrides:
  - A change to `prep_total_dilution`, `prep_steps`, `cartridge_type_id` or `prep_rules_off` on a row with a result first calls `append_history(why="override")`.
  - It then writes the audit row `endo_prep_override`, with `details = {user_id, worksheet_id, sample_id, old: {field: old}, new: {field: new}}`.
- Audit:
  - The app has no shared audit helper. The idiom is `db.add(AuditLog(operation=..., entity_type="worksheet_item", entity_id=str(item.id), details={...}))`.
  - `apply_item_endo_fields` wraps that idiom in its local `audit()`, which is reused here.

```python


# --- Task 4: lock events, release, override history ---------------------------

def _locks_world(client, db, **line):
    """_reader_world's open 2 mL worksheet on the active Endotoxin method
    (vials P-3001-S01 and -S02, 10 mg declared; the 0.05 and 0.01
    cartridges), the parent anchored to Retatrutide, and one Retatrutide
    line for any weight, 1:300 unless `line` says otherwise."""
    w = _reader_world(client, db)
    pep = _peptide(db)
    _anchor(db, "P-3001", pep)
    w.rule = _rule(db, pep, **(line or {"total_dilution": 300}))
    db.commit()
    w.url_a = f"/worksheets/{w.ws.id}/items/{w.a.id}"
    w.url_b = f"/worksheets/{w.ws.id}/items/{w.b.id}"
    return w


def test_every_print_locks_the_unlocked_2ml_endotoxin_rows(client, db):
    w = _locks_world(client, db)
    hplc, _ = _vial_item(db, w.ws, 3, _service(db, "BPC157-PURITY"), role="hplc")
    w.b.prep_rules_off = True
    db.commit()
    assert client.post(f"/worksheets/{w.ws.id}/printed").status_code == 200
    for x in (w.a, w.b, hplc):
        db.refresh(x)
    first = w.a.prep_rule
    assert first == {"rule": endo_prep_rules.snapshot(w.rule), "locked_at": first["locked_at"],
                     "locked_by": "print", "user_id": 1}
    assert first["locked_at"].endswith("Z")
    # A "no rule" row needs no lock; another bench's row is never ruled.
    assert (w.b.prep_rule, hplc.prep_rule) == (None, None)
    # Review Focus 1: the line is edited after the print, and a row is added.
    w.rule.total_dilution = 400
    late, _ = _vial_item(db, w.ws, 4, w.svc)
    db.commit()
    assert client.post(f"/worksheets/{w.ws.id}/printed").status_code == 200
    db.refresh(w.a)
    db.refresh(late)
    assert w.a.prep_rule == first     # the printed row keeps the 1:300 the bench prepped
    assert (late.prep_rule["locked_by"], late.prep_rule["rule"]["total_dilution"]) == ("print", 400)
    items = {it["id"]: it for it in client.get(f"/worksheets/{w.ws.id}").json()["items"]}
    assert items[w.a.id]["prep_rule"] == first


def test_a_row_with_no_matching_line_locks_to_the_standard(client, db):
    w = _reader_world(client, db)     # no lines at all
    assert client.post(f"/worksheets/{w.ws.id}/printed").status_code == 200
    db.refresh(w.a)
    assert (w.a.prep_rule["rule"], w.a.prep_rule["locked_by"]) == (None, "print")


def test_stock_and_completed_worksheets_never_lock(client, db):
    w = _locks_world(client, db)
    w.ws.endo_prep = "stock"
    done = _ws(db, "Completed before slice 5", status="completed")
    old, _ = _vial_item(db, done, 5, w.svc)
    db.commit()
    assert client.post(f"/worksheets/{w.ws.id}/printed").status_code == 200
    assert client.patch(w.url_a, json={"result_reading": "0.8",
                                       "cartridge_type_id": w.c05.id}).status_code == 200
    assert _import(client, w.ws, _reader_line(w.b, "0.8", w.c05)).status_code == 200
    assert client.post(f"/worksheets/{w.ws.id}/complete").status_code == 200
    assert client.post(f"/worksheets/{done.id}/printed").status_code == 200
    for x in (w.a, w.b, old):
        db.refresh(x)
    assert (w.a.prep_rule, w.b.prep_rule, old.prep_rule) == (None, None, None)


def test_the_first_reading_locks_its_row_and_clearing_releases_only_a_result_lock(client, db):
    w = _locks_world(client, db)
    assert client.patch(w.url_a, json={"result_reading": "<0.05",
                                       "cartridge_type_id": w.c05.id}).status_code == 200
    db.refresh(w.a)
    db.refresh(w.b)
    first = w.a.prep_rule
    assert (first["locked_by"], first["rule"]["id"]) == ("result", w.rule.id)
    assert w.b.prep_rule is None
    w.rule.total_dilution = 400
    db.commit()
    assert client.patch(w.url_a, json={"result_reading": "0.8"}).status_code == 200
    db.refresh(w.a)
    assert w.a.prep_rule == first     # a later reading keeps the first result's lock
    # Cleared, a result lock is released: the row follows today's rules again.
    assert client.patch(w.url_a, json={"result_reading": None}).status_code == 200
    db.refresh(w.a)
    assert w.a.prep_rule is None
    # Once the sheet is printed nothing is released: the bench preps any rerun
    # from that paper. Row b takes a result lock before the print, row a a print lock.
    assert client.patch(w.url_b, json={"result_reading": "0.8",
                                       "cartridge_type_id": w.c05.id}).status_code == 200
    assert client.post(f"/worksheets/{w.ws.id}/printed").status_code == 200
    for url in (w.url_a, w.url_b):
        for payload in ({"result_reading": "0.8", "cartridge_type_id": w.c05.id},
                        {"result_reading": None}):
            assert client.patch(url, json=payload).status_code == 200
    db.refresh(w.a)
    db.refresh(w.b)
    assert (w.a.prep_rule["locked_by"], w.a.prep_rule["rule"]["total_dilution"]) == ("print", 400)
    assert (w.b.prep_rule["locked_by"], w.b.prep_rule["rule"]["total_dilution"]) == ("result", 400)


def test_a_reader_import_locks_the_rows_it_gave_a_reading(client, db):
    w = _locks_world(client, db)
    r = _import(client, w.ws, _reader_line(w.a, "<0.05", w.c05))
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    items = {it["id"]: it for it in r.json()["items"]}
    assert items[w.a.id]["prep_rule"]["locked_by"] == "result"
    assert items[w.a.id]["prep_rule"]["rule"] == endo_prep_rules.snapshot(w.rule)
    assert items[w.b.id]["prep_rule"] is None


def test_completion_locks_every_row_not_locked_yet(client, db):
    w = _locks_world(client, db)
    assert client.patch(w.url_a, json={"result_reading": "<0.05",
                                       "cartridge_type_id": w.c05.id}).status_code == 200
    assert client.post(f"/worksheets/{w.ws.id}/complete").status_code == 200
    db.refresh(w.a)
    db.refresh(w.b)
    assert (w.a.prep_rule["locked_by"], w.b.prep_rule["locked_by"]) == ("result", "complete")


def test_an_override_on_a_row_with_a_result_keeps_the_replaced_run(client, db):
    w = _locks_world(client, db)
    assert client.patch(w.url_a, json={"result_reading": "<0.05",
                                       "cartridge_type_id": w.c05.id}).status_code == 200
    db.refresh(w.a)
    lock = w.a.prep_rule
    run = {"by": 1, "why": "override", "lock": lock, "result_reading": "<0.05",
           "reader_test_id": None, "result_entered_at": w.a.result_entered_at.isoformat() + "Z",
           "step1_ratio": 10, "cartridge_type_id": w.c05.id}
    assert client.patch(w.url_a, json={"prep_total_dilution": 500}).status_code == 200
    db.refresh(w.a)
    [first] = w.a.prep_history
    assert first == {**run, "at": first["at"], "total_dilution": 300}
    assert (w.a.prep_total_dilution, w.a.prep_rule) == (500, lock)
    # The switch is an override too: the typed 1:500 run is kept, and "no rule" drops the lock.
    assert client.patch(w.url_a, json={"prep_rules_off": True}).status_code == 200
    db.refresh(w.a)
    second = w.a.prep_history[1]
    assert second == {**run, "at": second["at"], "total_dilution": 500}
    assert (w.a.prep_rules_off, w.a.prep_rule) == (True, None)
    base = {"user_id": 1, "worksheet_id": w.ws.id, "sample_id": w.a.sample_id}
    assert [a.details for a in db.query(AuditLog).filter(
        AuditLog.operation == "endo_prep_override").order_by(AuditLog.id)] == [
        {**base, "old": {"prep_total_dilution": None}, "new": {"prep_total_dilution": 500}},
        {**base, "old": {"prep_rules_off": False}, "new": {"prep_rules_off": True}},
    ]
    # Without a result, a typed change is no override: no history, no override audit.
    assert client.patch(w.url_b, json={"prep_total_dilution": 500}).status_code == 200
    db.refresh(w.b)
    assert w.b.prep_history is None
    assert db.query(AuditLog).filter(AuditLog.operation == "endo_prep_override").count() == 2


def test_switching_back_to_rules_locks_at_once_once_printed_or_read(client, db):
    w = _locks_world(client, db)
    assert client.post(f"/worksheets/{w.ws.id}/printed").status_code == 200
    assert client.patch(w.url_a, json={"prep_rules_off": True}).status_code == 200
    db.refresh(w.a)
    assert (w.a.prep_rules_off, w.a.prep_rule) == (True, None)
    w.rule.total_dilution = 400       # edited while the row was on "no rule"
    db.commit()
    assert client.patch(w.url_a, json={"prep_rules_off": False}).status_code == 200
    db.refresh(w.a)
    assert (w.a.prep_rule["locked_by"], w.a.prep_rule["rule"]["total_dilution"]) == ("switch", 400)
    # On a sheet not printed yet: a row with a result locks, a row without one follows today's rules.
    fresh = _ws(db, "Not printed")
    read, _ = _vial_item(db, fresh, 5, w.svc)
    unread, _ = _vial_item(db, fresh, 6, w.svc)
    read.result_reading, read.cartridge_type_id = "0.8", w.c05.id
    read.prep_rules_off = unread.prep_rules_off = True
    db.commit()
    for row in (read, unread):
        assert client.patch(f"/worksheets/{fresh.id}/items/{row.id}",
                            json={"prep_rules_off": False}).status_code == 200
        db.refresh(row)
    assert read.prep_rule["locked_by"] == "switch"
    assert (unread.prep_rules_off, unread.prep_rule) == (False, None)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("field, value", [
    ("prep_total_dilution", 500), ("prep_steps", [[20, 5], None, None]),
    ("cartridge_type_id", "c01"), ("prep_rules_off", True), ("prep_note", "special: 1:500"),
])
def test_a_promoted_rows_prep_is_final(client, db, field, value):
    """Spec 8 (ruling on deviation B4): a typed value is allowed until the row is promoted."""
    w = _world(client, db)
    done, _ = _row(db, w, "P-4001-S01", state="promoted")
    live, _ = _row(db, w, "P-4002-S01")
    db.commit()
    body = {field: w.c01.id if value == "c01" else value}
    before = getattr(done, field)
    r = client.patch(f"/worksheets/{w.ws.id}/items/{done.id}", json=body)
    assert (r.status_code, r.json()["detail"]) == (409, "the row is promoted; its prep is final")
    db.refresh(done)
    assert getattr(done, field) == before
    # The same change saves on a row that is not promoted.
    assert client.patch(f"/worksheets/{w.ws.id}/items/{live.id}", json=body).status_code == 200
    db.refresh(live)
    assert getattr(live, field) == body[field]


def test_a_promoted_row_still_takes_readings_and_other_fields(client, db):
    w = _world(client, db)
    done, _ = _row(db, w, "P-4001-S01", state="promoted")
    db.commit()
    url = f"/worksheets/{w.ws.id}/items/{done.id}"
    for payload in ({"result_reading": "0.8"}, {"made": True}, {"prep_water_ml": 2.5},
                    # Sent as stored: no change, so no refusal.
                    {"cartridge_type_id": w.c05.id, "prep_note": None}):
        assert client.patch(url, json=payload).status_code == 200, payload
    db.refresh(done)
    assert (done.result_reading, done.prep_water_ml, done.cartridge_type_id) == ("0.8", 2.5, w.c05.id)
    assert done.made_at is not None
```

```python
import math
```

Append at the end of the file, after Task 3's `row_facts`:

```python


# --- Locks, release and history (spec sections 6 to 8) ----------------------

def _iso_now() -> str:
    """Naive UTC with a Z, as the reader import stamps imported_at."""
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None).isoformat() + "Z"


def _new_lock(rule: Optional[dict], event: str, user_id: Optional[int]) -> dict:
    """A lock snapshot (spec 6). The line is copied, so a later edit or
    deactivation of it never reaches the row; None locks to the standard."""
    return {"rule": dict(rule) if rule is not None else None, "locked_at": _iso_now(),
            "locked_by": event, "user_id": user_id}


def lock_rows(db: Session, worksheet, items, *, event: str, user_id: Optional[int]) -> int:
    """Lock every endotoxin row of `items` that is not locked yet and not
    switched to "no rule" (spec 6) to today's match, or to the standard when
    no line matches. Only on an open 2 mL worksheet: a stock one is never
    ruled, and nothing on a completed one changes (the complete route locks
    before it completes). Returns how many rows it locked. Does not commit."""
    if event not in LOCK_EVENTS:
        raise ValueError(f"unknown lock event {event!r}")
    if worksheet.endo_prep != "2ml" or worksheet.status == "completed":
        return 0
    todo = [it for it in items if it.prep_rule is None and not it.prep_rules_off]
    facts = row_facts(db, worksheet, todo) if todo else {}
    if not facts:
        return 0
    rules = load_rules(db)
    for it in todo:
        if it.id in facts:
            it.prep_rule = _new_lock(match_rule(rules, endo_prep=worksheet.endo_prep,
                                                rules_off=False, **facts[it.id]),
                                     event, user_id)
    return len(facts)


def release_on_clear(item) -> None:
    """A cleared reading releases a lock its result took, so the row follows
    today's rules again (spec 6). A lock from a print, completion, Re-apply
    or the switch stays: the bench prepped from that paper."""
    if (item.prep_rule or {}).get("locked_by") == "result":
        item.prep_rule = None


def append_history(item, *, why: str, user_id: Optional[int], effective: dict) -> None:
    """Keep the row's current run before a Re-apply or an override replaces
    it (spec 7): stored facts only, from which the row's history view
    recomputes EU per vial and the verdict. `effective` is
    effective_values() as the row stands. A new list every time: the JSON
    column misses in-place edits."""
    entry = {
        "at": _iso_now(), "by": user_id, "why": why, "lock": item.prep_rule,
        "total_dilution": effective["total_dilution"],
        "step1_ratio": effective["step1_ratio"],
        "cartridge_type_id": effective["cartridge_type_id"],
        "result_reading": item.result_reading,
        "reader_test_id": (item.result_reader or {}).get("test_id"),
        "result_entered_at": (item.result_entered_at.isoformat() + "Z"
                              if item.result_entered_at else None),
    }
    item.prep_history = [*(item.prep_history or []), entry]


def promoted_sample_ids(db: Session, sample_ids) -> set[str]:
    """The rows (by sample id) whose vial's live endotoxin analysis is
    promoted: slice 3's promoted state, read through
    worksheets_endo_promote.vial_endo_analyses in one query. A promoted
    row's prep is final (spec 8) and Re-apply never moves it (spec 7)."""
    from worksheets_endo_promote import vial_endo_analyses  # lazy: it imports this module

    return {sid for sid, pairs in vial_endo_analyses(db, set(sample_ids)).items()
            if any(analysis.review_state == "promoted" for analysis, _svc in pairs)}
```

```python
            raise HTTPException(400, str(exc))
```

with:

```python
    from endo_prep_rules import promoted_sample_ids
    from worksheets_endo import (ENDO_ITEM_FIELDS, PREP_FINAL, PREP_FINAL_FIELDS,
                                 apply_item_endo_fields, check_item_endo_fields,
                                 resolve_endo_method)
    ws = db.execute(select(Worksheet).where(Worksheet.id == worksheet_id)).scalar_one()
    endo_values = {f: getattr(data, f) for f in ENDO_ITEM_FIELDS if f in data.model_fields_set}
    if endo_values:
        if ws.status == "completed":
            raise HTTPException(409, "Worksheet is completed")
        try:
            endo_values = check_item_endo_fields(db, ws, item, endo_values)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(400, str(exc))
        # Endo slice 5 (spec 8): a promoted row's prep is final. A value sent
        # as stored is no change; readings and the other fields keep today's
        # behaviour. One query, and only when a final field would change.
        if (any(f in endo_values and endo_values[f] != getattr(item, f) for f in PREP_FINAL_FIELDS)
                and promoted_sample_ids(db, [item.sample_id])):
            raise HTTPException(409, PREP_FINAL)
```

The guard sits after `check_item_endo_fields`, so it compares the values as they would be stored, a trimmed note included. It also sits before anything in the route writes.

### Task 5: Re-apply

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/endo_prep_rules.py` (import block; append at the end)
- Modify: `backend/main.py` (after `promote_endo_worksheet_rows`, around line 25702)
- Test: `backend/tests/test_endo_prep_rule_rows.py` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Tasks 3 and 4: `row_facts`, `effective_values`, `_typed_values`, `_VALUE_FIELDS`, `append_history`, `_new_lock`, and `promoted_sample_ids`, the same promoted notion as the PATCH guard.
  - Task 1: `match_rule`, `load_rules`.
  - `worksheets_endo_promote.REASON_PROMOTED` (`"already promoted"`).
  - `worksheets_endo.resolve_endo_method` and `endo_run_params`.
- Produces:
  - `reapply_candidates(db, worksheet) -> [{"item_id", "sample_id", "old": <lock>, "new_rule": <snapshot or None>}]`.
  - `apply_reapply(db, worksheet, item_ids, current_user) -> [{"item_id", "applied", "reason"}]`. It does not commit.
  - `GET /worksheets/{worksheet_id}/prep-rules/reapply`. It returns `PrepRuleReapplyPreview`, `{"candidates": [...]}`.
  - `POST /worksheets/{worksheet_id}/prep-rules/reapply` with body `{"item_ids": list[int]}`, at most 500 ids. It returns `PrepRuleReapplyResponse`, `{"results": [...]}`.
  - Both routes return 404 for a missing or staging worksheet.
  - `old` and `new_rule` are typed `dict`, so no key is stripped.
  - Audit row `endo_prep_rule_reapplied`, with `details = {user_id, worksheet_id, sample_id, old: <old lock>, new: <new lock>}`.
- Refusal reasons (exact text, shown by the dialog). The `endo_prep_rules` constants:
  - `REAPPLY_NOT_ON_WORKSHEET = "not on this worksheet"`
  - `REAPPLY_COMPLETED = "the worksheet is completed"`
  - `REAPPLY_STOCK = "prep rules are for 2 mL worksheets"`
  - `REAPPLY_RULES_OFF = "the row is set to no rule"`
  - `REAPPLY_NOT_LOCKED = "the row is not locked; it already follows today's rules"`
  - `REAPPLY_UNCHANGED = "nothing to re-apply: the row already matches today's rule"`
  - promoted: `worksheets_endo_promote.REASON_PROMOTED = "already promoted"`
- A row is a candidate when Re-apply would change it:
  - its locked line differs from today's match by line id, including a line against none;
  - or a value differs on a field the row does not type.
  
  A typed field, or a status, note, band or order edit alone, never lists a row. See deviation B1.

**3a.** `backend/endo_prep_rules.py`. In the import block, replace:

Append at the end of the file:

```python


# --- Re-apply (spec section 7) ------------------------------------------------

# Why Re-apply leaves a row as it is; the dialog shows them as they are. A
# promoted row answers worksheets_endo_promote.REASON_PROMOTED ("already promoted").
REAPPLY_NOT_ON_WORKSHEET = "not on this worksheet"
REAPPLY_COMPLETED = "the worksheet is completed"
REAPPLY_STOCK = "prep rules are for 2 mL worksheets"
REAPPLY_RULES_OFF = "the row is set to no rule"
REAPPLY_NOT_LOCKED = "the row is not locked; it already follows today's rules"
REAPPLY_UNCHANGED = "nothing to re-apply: the row already matches today's rule"


def _moves(item, old: Optional[dict], new: Optional[dict]) -> bool:
    """Re-apply would change the row (spec 7, D6): another line, a line
    against none, or another value on a field the row does not type. A
    typed value is never touched, and a band, status, note or order edit
    alone changes nothing."""
    old, new = old or {}, new or {}
    typed = _typed_values(item)
    return old.get("id") != new.get("id") or any(
        typed[f] is None and old.get(f) != new.get(f) for f in _VALUE_FIELDS)


def _reapply_rows(db: Session, worksheet) -> list[tuple]:
    """Every row of the worksheet, in bench order, as (item, reason, line):
    why Re-apply leaves it (None: it qualifies) and the line that matches it
    today (None: the standard). Spec 7's conditions, judged alike by the
    preview and the apply. A fixed number of queries whatever the row count."""
    from worksheets_endo_promote import REASON_PROMOTED  # lazy: it imports this module

    items = db.execute(select(WorksheetItem).where(WorksheetItem.worksheet_id == worksheet.id)
                       .order_by(WorksheetItem.sort_order, WorksheetItem.id)).scalars().all()
    if worksheet.status == "completed":
        return [(it, REAPPLY_COMPLETED, None) for it in items]
    if worksheet.endo_prep != "2ml":
        return [(it, REAPPLY_STOCK, None) for it in items]
    locked = [it for it in items if it.prep_rule is not None and not it.prep_rules_off]
    promoted = promoted_sample_ids(db, {it.sample_id for it in locked})
    facts = row_facts(db, worksheet, locked)
    rules = load_rules(db) if locked else []
    out = []
    for it in items:
        new = (match_rule(rules, endo_prep=worksheet.endo_prep, rules_off=False, **facts[it.id])
               if it.id in facts else None)
        if it.prep_rules_off:
            reason = REAPPLY_RULES_OFF
        elif it.prep_rule is None:
            reason = REAPPLY_NOT_LOCKED
        elif it.sample_id in promoted:
            reason = REASON_PROMOTED
        elif not _moves(it, it.prep_rule.get("rule"), new):
            reason = REAPPLY_UNCHANGED
        else:
            reason = None
        out.append((it, reason, new))
    return out


def reapply_candidates(db: Session, worksheet) -> list[dict]:
    """GET .../prep-rules/reapply (spec 7): each row Re-apply would move,
    with its lock and the line that matches it now (None: the standard). The
    browser shows old against new from these."""
    return [{"item_id": it.id, "sample_id": it.sample_id, "old": it.prep_rule, "new_rule": new}
            for it, reason, new in _reapply_rows(db, worksheet) if reason is None]


def apply_reapply(db: Session, worksheet, item_ids: list[int], current_user) -> list[dict]:
    """POST .../prep-rules/reapply (spec 7): each confirmed row, in the order
    sent, re-checked against the preview's conditions. A row that qualifies
    keeps its current run in its history, takes today's line as a new lock
    (locked_by "reapply") and gets an endo_prep_rule_reapplied audit row
    with the old and new locks. Typed values and the reading stay as they
    are. A row that no longer qualifies is refused with its reason. Does not
    commit."""
    from worksheets_endo import endo_run_params, resolve_endo_method  # lazy: it imports this module

    rows = {it.id: (it, reason, new) for it, reason, new in _reapply_rows(db, worksheet)}
    user_id = getattr(current_user, "id", None)
    std_total = None
    results = []
    for item_id in item_ids:
        if item_id not in rows:
            results.append({"item_id": item_id, "applied": False,
                            "reason": REAPPLY_NOT_ON_WORKSHEET})
            continue
        it, reason, new = rows[item_id]
        if reason is not None:
            results.append({"item_id": item_id, "applied": False, "reason": reason})
            continue
        if std_total is None:
            method = resolve_endo_method(db, worksheet)
            std_total = endo_run_params(
                method["run_parameters"] if method else None)["std_total_dilution"]
        old = it.prep_rule
        append_history(it, why="reapply", user_id=user_id,
                       effective=effective_values(it, old.get("rule"), std_total=std_total))
        it.prep_rule = _new_lock(new, "reapply", user_id)
        db.add(AuditLog(operation="endo_prep_rule_reapplied", entity_type="worksheet_item",
                        entity_id=str(it.id),
                        details={"user_id": user_id, "worksheet_id": worksheet.id,
                                 "sample_id": it.sample_id, "old": old, "new": it.prep_rule}))
        # Listed twice, the second is refused: the row now has today's line.
        rows[item_id] = (it, REAPPLY_UNCHANGED, new)
        results.append({"item_id": item_id, "applied": True, "reason": None})
    return results
```

```python
    return EndoPromoteResponse(results=promote_rows(
        db, _current_user, ws, data.rows, background_tasks=background_tasks))


class PrepRuleReapplyCandidate(BaseModel):
    """One row Re-apply would move (endo slice 5, spec 7). `old` is the row's
    lock ({"rule", "locked_at", "locked_by", "user_id"}) and `new_rule` the
    line that matches it now (None: the standard), each as stored, every key
    kept."""
    item_id: int
    sample_id: str
    old: dict
    new_rule: Optional[dict] = None


class PrepRuleReapplyPreview(BaseModel):
    candidates: list[PrepRuleReapplyCandidate]


class PrepRuleReapplyRequest(BaseModel):
    # The promote's bound.
    item_ids: list[int] = Field(max_length=500)


class PrepRuleReapplyResult(BaseModel):
    item_id: int
    applied: bool
    reason: Optional[str] = None


class PrepRuleReapplyResponse(BaseModel):
    results: list[PrepRuleReapplyResult]


@app.get("/worksheets/{worksheet_id}/prep-rules/reapply", response_model=PrepRuleReapplyPreview)
def preview_prep_rules_reapply(
    worksheet_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    _current_user=Depends(get_current_user),
):
    """The 2 mL rows whose locked rule differs from today's match, for the
    Re-apply dialog (endo slice 5, spec 7). Writes nothing. A staging
    worksheet 404s; a completed or stock one lists none."""
    from endo_prep_rules import reapply_candidates

    ws = db.execute(select(Worksheet).where(Worksheet.id == worksheet_id)).scalar_one_or_none()
    if not ws or ws.status == "staging":
        raise HTTPException(404, "Worksheet not found")
    return {"candidates": reapply_candidates(db, ws)}


@app.post("/worksheets/{worksheet_id}/prep-rules/reapply", response_model=PrepRuleReapplyResponse)
def apply_prep_rules_reapply(
    worksheet_id: int,
    data: PrepRuleReapplyRequest,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    _current_user=Depends(get_current_user),
):
    """Move the rows the analyst confirmed to today's rule (endo slice 5,
    spec 7). Every row is answered on its own, applied or not and why; the
    applied ones share one commit."""
    from endo_prep_rules import apply_reapply

    ws = db.execute(select(Worksheet).where(Worksheet.id == worksheet_id)).scalar_one_or_none()
    if not ws or ws.status == "staging":
        raise HTTPException(404, "Worksheet not found")
    results = apply_reapply(db, ws, data.item_ids, _current_user)
    db.commit()
    return {"results": results}
```

The paths have three segments under `/worksheets`, so they cannot collide with `GET /worksheets/{worksheet_id}` or the literal `/worksheets/inbox/lanes`.

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest'
// The shared matcher fixture (Task 1): pytest runs the same cases against
// backend/endo_prep_rules.py match_rule.
import fixture from '../../../backend/tests/fixtures/endo_prep_rule_cases.json'
import {
  bandText,
  matchRule,
  rowRule,
  ruleTag,
  ruleText,
  type EndoPrepRule,
  type PrepRuleLock,
} from '@/lib/endo-prep-rules'
import {
  ENDO_RUN_PARAM_DEFAULTS,
  dilutionPlan,
  mk1Value,
  prep2Check,
  targetDilutionOf,
  type EndoCtx,
  type EndoSample,
} from '@/lib/endo-prep-2ml'
import type { WorksheetItemRow } from '@/lib/endo-worksheet'
import { endoRowsFor, endoSampleFor } from '@/lib/endo-worksheet-2ml'
import {
  endoItem,
  endoWorksheet,
} from '@/components/hplc/endo/__tests__/endo-fixtures'

// The JSON's status is a plain string to the compiler.
const RULE_SETS = fixture.rule_sets as unknown as Record<string, EndoPrepRule[]>

function rule(over: Partial<EndoPrepRule> = {}): EndoPrepRule {
  return {
    id: 1,
    peptide_id: 7,
    min_mg: null,
    max_mg: null,
    total_dilution: null,
    step1_ratio: null,
    cartridge_type_id: null,
    status: 'trial',
    note: null,
    sort_order: 1,
    active: true,
    ...over,
  }
}

function lock(r: EndoPrepRule | null): PrepRuleLock {
  return {
    rule: r,
    locked_at: '2026-10-14T15:00:00Z',
    locked_by: 'print',
    user_id: 5,
  }
}

describe('matchRule: the shared fixture', () => {
  it.each(fixture.cases)('$name', c => {
    const rules = RULE_SETS[c.rules]
    if (!rules) throw new Error(`no rule set ${c.rules}`)
    const q = c.query
    expect(
      matchRule(rules, {
        peptideId: q.peptide_id,
        weightMg: q.weight_mg,
        isBac: q.is_bac,
        endoPrep: q.endo_prep,
        rulesOff: q.rules_off,
      })?.id ?? null
    ).toBe(c.expect)
  })
})

describe('rowRule', () => {
  const live = [rule({ id: 1, total_dilution: 300 })]
  const q = { peptideId: 7, weightMg: 10, isBac: false, endoPrep: '2ml' }

  it('follows the live match until the row is locked', () => {
    expect(rowRule({}, live, q)?.id).toBe(1)
  })

  it('keeps the locked line, never the live one (Review Focus 1)', () => {
    const old = rule({ id: 1, total_dilution: 400 })
    expect(rowRule({ prep_rule: lock(old) }, live, q)).toEqual(old)
  })

  it('a row locked to the standard stays on it while a rule matches', () => {
    expect(rowRule({ prep_rule: lock(null) }, live, q)).toBeNull()
  })

  it('a "no rule" row has none, locked or not', () => {
    expect(rowRule({ prep_rules_off: true }, live, q)).toBeNull()
    expect(
      rowRule(
        { prep_rules_off: true, prep_rule: lock(live[0] ?? null) },
        live,
        q
      )
    ).toBeNull()
  })
})

describe('the rule as the lab reads it', () => {
  it('names the band and the values it sets', () => {
    expect(bandText(rule())).toBe('any weight')
    expect(bandText(rule({ max_mg: 30 }))).toBe('up to 30 mg')
    expect(bandText(rule({ min_mg: 30 }))).toBe('above 30 mg')
    expect(bandText(rule({ min_mg: 10, max_mg: 30 }))).toBe(
      'above 10 up to 30 mg'
    )
    expect(
      ruleText(
        rule({ min_mg: 30, total_dilution: 300, cartridge_type_id: 4 }),
        'Retatrutide',
        id => (id === 4 ? '0.01 cartridge' : '?')
      )
    ).toBe('Retatrutide above 30 mg · 1:300 total, 0.01 cartridge')
    expect(ruleText(rule({ max_mg: 30 }), 'Retatrutide')).toBe(
      'Retatrutide up to 30 mg · the standard'
    )
    expect(ruleTag(rule())).toBe('rule (trial)')
    expect(ruleTag(rule({ status: 'validated' }))).toBe('rule')
  })
})

describe('the rule tier in the prep maths: typed > rule > standard, per field', () => {
  const ctx: EndoCtx = {
    params: ENDO_RUN_PARAM_DEFAULTS,
    allowedLambdas: [0.05, 0.01],
  }
  const s = (o: Partial<EndoSample> = {}): EndoSample => ({
    sampleId: 'P-1',
    identity: 'Retatrutide',
    sampleType: null,
    weightMg: 10,
    form: null,
    waterMl: null,
    totalDilution: null,
    steps: null,
    lambda: 0.05,
    reading: null,
    reader: null,
    ...o,
  })

  it('total: the typed one, else the rule, else the standard', () => {
    const r300 = rule({ total_dilution: 300 })
    expect(targetDilutionOf(s(), ctx)).toBe(250)
    expect(targetDilutionOf(s({ rule: r300 }), ctx)).toBe(300)
    expect(targetDilutionOf(s({ rule: r300, totalDilution: 400 }), ctx)).toBe(
      400
    )
    // a rule that leaves the total alone
    expect(targetDilutionOf(s({ rule: rule({ step1_ratio: 5 }) }), ctx)).toBe(
      250
    )
  })

  it('step 1 ratio: the typed one, else the rule, else 1:10; step 2 is derived', () => {
    const r5 = rule({ step1_ratio: 5 })
    const ruled = dilutionPlan(s({ rule: r5 }), ctx)
    expect(
      ruled.steps.map(st => [st.ratio, st.own, st.ruled, st.diluentUl])
    ).toEqual([
      [5, false, true, 80],
      [25, false, true, 480],
      [2, false, false, 125],
    ])
    expect(ruled.total).toBe(250)
    // a ruled step is not the standard, but it was not set by hand either
    expect(ruled.standard).toBe(false)
    const typed = dilutionPlan(
      s({ rule: r5, steps: [{ sampleUl: 20, ratio: 8 }, null, null] }),
      ctx
    )
    expect(typed.steps.map(st => [st.ratio, st.own, st.ruled])).toEqual([
      [8, true, false],
      [15.625, false, false],
      [2, false, false],
    ])
    // typing the method's 1:10 over a rule's 1:5 is a value set by hand
    expect(
      dilutionPlan(
        s({ rule: r5, steps: [{ sampleUl: 20, ratio: 10 }, null, null] }),
        ctx
      ).steps[0]?.own
    ).toBe(true)
  })

  it('a typed total beside a ruled step 1 ratio, field by field', () => {
    const p = dilutionPlan(
      s({
        rule: rule({ total_dilution: 300, step1_ratio: 5 }),
        totalDilution: 400,
      }),
      ctx
    )
    expect(p.target).toBe(400)
    expect(p.steps.map(st => [st.ratio, st.ruled])).toEqual([
      [5, true],
      [40, true],
      [2, false],
    ])
  })

  it('a ruled total moves the LOQ and the EU per vial', () => {
    const r300 = rule({ total_dilution: 300 })
    // 0.05 x 250 x 2 mL = 25 on the standard; 0.05 x 300 x 2 = 30 under the rule
    expect(prep2Check(s(), ctx).loqVial).toBe(25)
    expect(prep2Check(s({ rule: r300 }), ctx).loqVial).toBe(30)
    expect(mk1Value(s({ reading: '<0.05' }), ctx)?.value).toBe('<25')
    expect(mk1Value(s({ rule: r300, reading: '<0.05' }), ctx)?.value).toBe(
      '<30'
    )
    expect(mk1Value(s({ rule: r300, reading: '0.8' }), ctx)).toEqual({
      value: '480',
      unit: 'EU/vial',
      verdict: 'fail',
    })
  })
})

describe('rows resolve their rule and its cartridge', () => {
  // endoWorksheet: run cartridge 3 (0.05, the method default); the method
  // allows 3 and 4 (0.01). endoItem: Retatrutide, 10 mg declared.
  const pep = (over: Partial<WorksheetItemRow> = {}) =>
    endoItem({ sample_peptide_id: 7, ...over })

  it('the rule reaches an unlocked row on a 2 mL worksheet', () => {
    const s = endoSampleFor(
      pep(),
      endoWorksheet([]),
      [],
      [rule({ total_dilution: 300 })]
    )
    expect(s.rule?.id).toBe(1)
    expect(
      targetDilutionOf(s, {
        params: ENDO_RUN_PARAM_DEFAULTS,
        allowedLambdas: [],
      })
    ).toBe(300)
  })

  it('cartridge: typed, then the rule when the method allows it, then the run', () => {
    const ws = endoWorksheet([])
    const r4 = [rule({ cartridge_type_id: 4 })]
    const [ruled, typed] = endoRowsFor(
      [pep(), pep({ cartridge_type_id: 3, cartridge_sensitivity_eu_ml: 0.05 })],
      ws,
      [],
      r4
    )
    expect([
      ruled?.cartridge?.id,
      ruled?.sample.lambda,
      ruled?.ruleCartridge,
    ]).toEqual([4, 0.01, true])
    expect([typed?.cartridge?.id, typed?.inherited?.id]).toEqual([3, 4])
    // a cartridge the method does not allow is passed over
    const [off] = endoRowsFor(
      [pep()],
      ws,
      [],
      [rule({ cartridge_type_id: 99 })]
    )
    expect([off?.cartridge?.id, off?.ruleCartridge]).toEqual([3, false])
  })

  it('bac water, a stock worksheet and a blend are never ruled', () => {
    const r = [rule({ total_dilution: 300 })]
    const bw = pep({
      sample_id: 'BW-0114-S02',
      sample_identity: 'Bacteriostatic Water',
      sample_type: 'Bacteriostatic Water',
    })
    expect(endoSampleFor(bw, endoWorksheet([]), [], r).rule).toBeNull()
    expect(
      endoSampleFor(pep(), endoWorksheet([], { endo_prep: 'stock' }), [], r)
        .rule
    ).toBeNull()
    expect(
      endoSampleFor(pep({ sample_peptide_id: null }), endoWorksheet([]), [], r)
        .rule
    ).toBeNull()
  })

  it('a locked row keeps its line when the live rule changes', () => {
    const locked = pep({ prep_rule: lock(rule({ total_dilution: 400 })) })
    const s = endoSampleFor(
      locked,
      endoWorksheet([]),
      [],
      [rule({ total_dilution: 300 })]
    )
    expect(s.rule?.total_dilution).toBe(400)
  })

  it('with no rules every row resolves as before', () => {
    const items = [pep(), pep({ prep_total_dilution: 300 })]
    const ws = endoWorksheet(items)
    expect(endoRowsFor(items, ws, [], [])).toEqual(endoRowsFor(items, ws))
    expect(
      endoRowsFor(items, ws).map(r => [r.sample.rule, r.ruleCartridge])
    ).toEqual([
      [null, false],
      [null, false],
    ])
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Create `src/lib/endo-prep-rules.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * Method prep rules on the 2 mL worksheet (slice 5, spec
 * docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-endo-slice5-method-rules-design.md):
 * the types the API serves and the matcher, the browser's mirror of
 * backend/endo_prep_rules.py match_rule / row_rule. The shared fixture
 * backend/tests/fixtures/endo_prep_rule_cases.json pins both sides to the
 * same answers. Pure: no DOM, no fetch.
 */
import type { WorksheetItemRow } from '@/lib/worksheet-sla-subjects'

export type EndoPrepRuleStatus = 'trial' | 'validated'

/** One rule line, as GET /endo-prep-rules and a lock snapshot carry it. */
export interface EndoPrepRule {
  id: number
  peptide_id: number
  /** Band: weight > min_mg (null: no lower bound) and <= max_mg (null: no upper bound). */
  min_mg: number | null
  max_mg: number | null
  /** null: the method's standard. */
  total_dilution: number | null
  step1_ratio: number | null
  /** null: the row's usual cartridge chain. */
  cartridge_type_id: number | null
  status: EndoPrepRuleStatus
  note: string | null
  sort_order: number
  active: boolean
}

export type PrepRuleLockEvent =
  | 'print'
  | 'result'
  | 'complete'
  | 'reapply'
  | 'switch'

/** worksheet_items.prep_rule: the line a row locked to (null: the standard). */
export interface PrepRuleLock {
  rule: EndoPrepRule | null
  locked_at: string
  locked_by: PrepRuleLockEvent
  user_id: number | null
}

/** One past run in worksheet_items.prep_history (spec 7): stored facts only. */
export interface PrepHistoryEntry {
  at: string
  by: number | null
  why: 'reapply' | 'override'
  lock: PrepRuleLock | null
  total_dilution: number
  step1_ratio: number
  cartridge_type_id: number | null
  result_reading: string | null
  reader_test_id: string | null
  result_entered_at: string | null
}

export interface MatchQuery {
  peptideId: number | null
  weightMg: number | null
  isBac: boolean
  endoPrep: string | null
  rulesOff: boolean
}

function inBand(r: EndoPrepRule, w: number | null): boolean {
  if (w === null) return r.min_mg === null && r.max_mg === null
  return (
    (r.min_mg === null || w > r.min_mg) && (r.max_mg === null || w <= r.max_mg)
  )
}

/**
 * The active line for a row (spec 4), or null. Never for a stock worksheet,
 * bac water, a sample with no single catalog peptide or a "no rule" row.
 * Of several lines the narrowest band wins, width = (max_mg or infinity) -
 * (min_mg or 0), so any closed band beats an open one; a tie goes to the
 * lowest sort_order, then the lowest id, whatever the order of `rules`.
 */
export function matchRule(
  rules: EndoPrepRule[],
  q: MatchQuery
): EndoPrepRule | null {
  if (q.endoPrep !== '2ml' || q.isBac || q.rulesOff || q.peptideId === null)
    return null
  const w =
    typeof q.weightMg === 'number' && Number.isFinite(q.weightMg)
      ? q.weightMg
      : null
  let best: EndoPrepRule | null = null
  let bestWidth = Infinity
  for (const r of rules) {
    if (!r.active || r.peptide_id !== q.peptideId || !inBand(r, w)) continue
    const width = (r.max_mg ?? Infinity) - (r.min_mg ?? 0)
    const wins =
      best === null ||
      width < bestWidth ||
      (width === bestWidth &&
        (r.sort_order < best.sort_order ||
          (r.sort_order === best.sort_order && r.id < best.id)))
    if (wins) {
      best = r
      bestWidth = width
    }
  }
  return best
}

/**
 * The line a row runs under: none for a "no rule" row, the locked snapshot
 * once the row is locked (a rule edit never reaches it), else the live match.
 */
export function rowRule(
  item: Pick<WorksheetItemRow, 'prep_rule' | 'prep_rules_off'>,
  rules: EndoPrepRule[],
  q: Omit<MatchQuery, 'rulesOff'>
): EndoPrepRule | null {
  if (item.prep_rules_off) return null
  if (item.prep_rule) return item.prep_rule.rule
  return matchRule(rules, { ...q, rulesOff: false })
}

/** The band in the words of the server's warnings: "any weight", "up to
 *  30 mg", "above 30 mg" or "above 10 up to 30 mg". */
export function bandText(r: Pick<EndoPrepRule, 'min_mg' | 'max_mg'>): string {
  if (r.min_mg === null && r.max_mg === null) return 'any weight'
  if (r.min_mg === null) return `up to ${r.max_mg} mg`
  if (r.max_mg === null) return `above ${r.min_mg} mg`
  return `above ${r.min_mg} up to ${r.max_mg} mg`
}

/**
 * A line as the lab reads it: "Retatrutide above 30 mg · 1:300 total", values
 * it leaves at the standard left out ("the standard" when it sets none).
 * `cartridge` names a cartridge type id.
 */
export function ruleText(
  r: EndoPrepRule,
  peptide: string,
  cartridge: (id: number) => string = id => `cartridge #${id}`
): string {
  const values = [
    r.total_dilution !== null ? `1:${r.total_dilution} total` : null,
    r.step1_ratio !== null ? `step 1 1:${r.step1_ratio}` : null,
    r.cartridge_type_id !== null ? cartridge(r.cartridge_type_id) : null,
  ].filter(Boolean)
  return `${peptide} ${bandText(r)} · ${values.length ? values.join(', ') : 'the standard'}`
}

/** The tag text: "rule" or "rule (trial)". */
export function ruleTag(r: Pick<EndoPrepRule, 'status'>): string {
  return r.status === 'trial' ? 'rule (trial)' : 'rule'
}
```

insert:
```ts

/** A rule line as GET /endo-prep-rules lists it (slice 5). */
export interface EndoPrepRuleOut extends EndoPrepRule {
  peptide_name: string | null
  cartridge_name: string | null
  updated_at: string | null
}

/** POST body; PATCH takes any subset. The server sets sort_order. */
export interface EndoPrepRuleIn {
  peptide_id: number
  min_mg: number | null
  max_mg: number | null
  total_dilution: number | null
  step1_ratio: number | null
  cartridge_type_id: number | null
  status: EndoPrepRule['status']
  note: string | null
  active: boolean
}

/** A save's result: the line, and warnings that did not stop it. */
export interface EndoPrepRuleSaved {
  rule: EndoPrepRuleOut
  warnings: string[]
}

/** A locked row whose snapshot differs from today's match (spec 7). */
export interface PrepRulesReapplyCandidate {
  item_id: number
  sample_id: string
  old: PrepRuleLock
  new_rule: EndoPrepRule | null
}

export interface PrepRulesReapplyResult {
  item_id: number
  applied: boolean
  reason: string | null
}

/** Every line, inactive ones included, by sort_order then id. */
export async function getEndoPrepRules(): Promise<EndoPrepRuleOut[]> {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL()}/endo-prep-rules`, {
    headers: getBearerHeaders(),
  })
  if (!response.ok)
    throw new Error(
      await extractErrorMessage(
        response,
        `Load prep rules failed: ${response.status}`
      )
    )
  return response.json()
}

/** 400 carries the save check that refused it, word for word. */
export async function createEndoPrepRule(
  body: EndoPrepRuleIn
): Promise<EndoPrepRuleSaved> {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL()}/endo-prep-rules`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: getBearerHeaders('application/json'),
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  })
  if (!response.ok)
    throw new Error(
      await extractErrorMessage(
        response,
        `Save prep rule failed: ${response.status}`
      )
    )
  return response.json()
}

export async function updateEndoPrepRule(
  id: number,
  body: Partial<EndoPrepRuleIn>
): Promise<EndoPrepRuleSaved> {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL()}/endo-prep-rules/${id}`, {
    method: 'PATCH',
    headers: getBearerHeaders('application/json'),
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  })
  if (!response.ok)
    throw new Error(
      await extractErrorMessage(
        response,
        `Save prep rule failed: ${response.status}`
      )
    )
  return response.json()
}

/** The whole list in its new order; returns it as GET does. */
export async function reorderEndoPrepRules(
  ids: number[]
): Promise<EndoPrepRuleOut[]> {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL()}/endo-prep-rules/reorder`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: getBearerHeaders('application/json'),
    body: JSON.stringify({ ids }),
  })
  if (!response.ok)
    throw new Error(
      await extractErrorMessage(
        response,
        `Reorder prep rules failed: ${response.status}`
      )
    )
  return response.json()
}

export async function getPrepRulesReapply(
  worksheetId: number
): Promise<{ candidates: PrepRulesReapplyCandidate[] }> {
  const response = await fetch(
    `${API_BASE_URL()}/worksheets/${worksheetId}/prep-rules/reapply`,
    { headers: getBearerHeaders() }
  )
  if (!response.ok)
    throw new Error(
      await extractErrorMessage(
        response,
        `Load re-apply preview failed: ${response.status}`
      )
    )
  return response.json()
}

/** Each row is re-checked on the server; a refusal comes back on its row. */
export async function applyPrepRulesReapply(
  worksheetId: number,
  itemIds: number[]
): Promise<{ results: PrepRulesReapplyResult[] }> {
  const response = await fetch(
    `${API_BASE_URL()}/worksheets/${worksheetId}/prep-rules/reapply`,
    {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: getBearerHeaders('application/json'),
      body: JSON.stringify({ item_ids: itemIds }),
    }
  )
  if (!response.ok)
    throw new Error(
      await extractErrorMessage(
        response,
        `Re-apply rules failed: ${response.status}`
      )
    )
  return response.json()
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Create `src/services/endo-prep-rules.ts`**

```ts
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { getEndoPrepRules } from '@/lib/api'

/** The rules list's cache key: the rules page writes it, every 2 mL surface
 *  reads it, and saving a line invalidates it. */
export const ENDO_PREP_RULES_KEY = ['endo-prep-rules'] as const

/** Every rule line (inactive ones too; the matcher skips them). Short-lived:
 *  the lab edits rules several times a week and a row that is not locked
 *  follows them. */
export function useEndoPrepRules(enabled = true) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ENDO_PREP_RULES_KEY,
    queryFn: getEndoPrepRules,
    staleTime: 30_000,
    enabled,
  })
}
```

```ts
function ruleTotalOf(s: EndoSample): number | null {

}

/** The rule's step 1 ratio when it sets one (>= 1), else null. */
function ruleRatioOf(s: EndoSample): number | null {

  return r !== null && r >= 1 ? r : null
}
```

```ts
/**
 * The method rule a row runs under (spec 4, 5): none for bac water, which is
 * never ruled (formOf, so a form typed on the row decides, as the server's
 * promote check does), else rowRule on the parent's single catalog peptide
 * and the weight Box 2 reads.
 */
function ruleFor(
  item: WorksheetItemRow,
  ws: WorksheetRow,
  rules: EndoPrepRule[]
): EndoPrepRule | null {
  const base = baseSample(item)
  if (formOf(base) === 'bac') return null
  return rowRule(item, rules, {
    peptideId: item.sample_peptide_id ?? null,
    weightMg: base.weightMg,
    isBac: false,
    endoPrep: ws.endo_prep ?? null,
  })
}
```

8f. Replace `endoSampleFor` and `endoRowsFor`. The anchor runs from the doc comment just above `export function endoSampleFor(` (two lines, opening with "The row as the 2 mL maths reads it (4.2, 4.3)") through the closing `}` of `endoRowsFor`. Replace it with:
```ts
/** The row as the 2 mL maths reads it (4.2, 4.3). `catalog` is the allowed
 *  list's source when no method resolves; `rules` the live rule lines
 *  (slice 5), which reach only rows that are not locked. */
export function endoSampleFor(
  item: WorksheetItemRow,
  ws: WorksheetRow,
  catalog: ConsumableType[] = [],
  rules: EndoPrepRule[] = []
): EndoSample {
  return resolveRow(item, ws, catalog, ruleFor(item, ws, rules)).sample
}

/** The page's rows, numbered in the order given (Box 1's order, bacWaterLast). */
export function endoRowsFor(
  ordered: WorksheetItemRow[],
  ws: WorksheetRow,
  catalog: ConsumableType[] = [],
  rules: EndoPrepRule[] = []
): EndoRow[] {
  return ordered.map((item, i) => ({
    item,
    n: i + 1,
    ...resolveRow(item, ws, catalog, ruleFor(item, ws, rules)),
  }))
}

/** A row resolved under another rule: Re-apply's "new" side (spec 7). */
export function endoRowUnderRule(
  row: EndoRow,
  ws: WorksheetRow,
  catalog: ConsumableType[],
  rule: EndoPrepRule | null
): EndoRow {
  return { ...row, ...resolveRow(row.item, ws, catalog, rule) }
}
```

### Task 7: The rules page

**Files:**
- Create: `src/components/hplc/endo/EndoPrepRulesPage.tsx`
- Create: `src/components/hplc/endo/__tests__/EndoPrepRulesPage.test.tsx`
- Modify: `src/store/ui-store.ts` (`HPLCAnalysisSubSection`, line 45)
- Modify: `src/components/layout/MainWindowContent.tsx` (import near line 16; route near line 87)
- Modify: `src/components/layout/AppSidebar.tsx` (the HPLC Automation sub-items, line 117)
- Modify: `src/components/hplc/EndoWorksheetView.tsx` (a `headerAction` slot beside `EndoWorksheetActions`, near line 156)
- Modify: `src/components/hplc/endo/EndoWorksheetPage.tsx` (the "Prep rules" button)
- Test: `src/components/hplc/__tests__/EndoWorksheetPage.test.tsx`, `src/lib/__tests__/hash-navigation-worksheet.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - From Task 6: `getEndoPrepRules`, `createEndoPrepRule`, `updateEndoPrepRule`, `reorderEndoPrepRules`, `EndoPrepRuleIn`, `EndoPrepRuleOut`, `EndoPrepRuleSaved`, `ENDO_PREP_RULES_KEY` and `useEndoPrepRules`.
  - `getPeptides()`, read under the existing `['peptides']` key, as `RelabelNativeSlotDialog` reads it.
  - `listConsumables({ kind: 'endo_cartridge' })`, read under the existing `['consumables', 'endo_cartridge']` key, as the worksheet page reads it.
  - The server behaviour from Task 2:
    - POST appends at the end.
    - PATCH takes only the fields sent, and an explicit null clears one.
    - Reorder takes every id, inactive ones included.
    - 400 `detail` texts come back word for word through `extractErrorMessage`.
    - The save response is `{"rule", "warnings"}`.
- Produces:
  - `EndoPrepRulesPage` (named and default export).
  - The subsection `'endo-prep-rules'` under `hplc-analysis`. The hash `#hplc-analysis/endo-prep-rules` round-trips through the generic `navigateTo` branch, so no `hash-navigation.ts` change is needed.
  - `EndoWorksheetView`'s optional `headerAction?: ReactNode`.

There is no reusable catalog search in the app:
- `PeptideSelector` is the HPLC wizard's Select. It auto-selects from folder names, shows calibration badges and lists blends.
- `RelabelNativeSlotDialog` and `ReplaceAnalyteDialog` each carry an inline copy.

The page therefore holds a small `PeptidePicker` built from the app's `Popover` and `Command` (cmdk), the pattern `AddNodePalette` uses. Per the project memory, cmdk filters on `value`, so the item's `value` carries the name and the abbreviation.

Blends are left out, because a blend never matches (spec D2).

```tsx
import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest'
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import {
  fireEvent,
  render,
  screen,
  waitFor,
  within,
} from '@testing-library/react'

import type * as ApiModule from '@/lib/api'
import type { ConsumableType, EndoPrepRuleOut, PeptideRecord } from '@/lib/api'

vi.mock('@/lib/api', async importOriginal => ({
  ...(await importOriginal<typeof ApiModule>()),
  getEndoPrepRules: vi.fn(),
  getPeptides: vi.fn(),
  listConsumables: vi.fn(),
  createEndoPrepRule: vi.fn(),
  updateEndoPrepRule: vi.fn(),
  reorderEndoPrepRules: vi.fn(),
}))

  createEndoPrepRule,

} from '@/lib/api'
```
