```tsx
function rule(over: Partial<EndoPrepRuleOut> = {}): EndoPrepRuleOut {
  return {
    id: 1,
    peptide_id: 1,
    min_mg: null,
    max_mg: 30,
    total_dilution: null,
    step1_ratio: null,
    cartridge_type_id: null,
    status: 'validated',
    note: '1:250 fine to 30 mg',
    sort_order: 0,
    active: true,
    peptide_name: 'Retatrutide',
    cartridge_name: null,
    updated_at: '2026-10-14T15:00:00Z',
    ...over,
  }
}

const RETA_30 = rule()
const RETA_OVER_30 = rule({
  id: 2,
  min_mg: 30,
  max_mg: null,
  total_dilution: 300,
  status: 'trial',
  note: '60 mg PPC low at 1:250',
  sort_order: 1,
})

const peptide = (id: number, name: string, abbreviation: string) =>
  ({
    id,
    name,
    abbreviation,
    active: true,
    is_blend: false,
  }) as PeptideRecord

const cartridge: ConsumableType = {
  id: 4,
  kind: 'endo_cartridge',
  name: 'Endosafe 0.01',
  manufacturer: null,
  part_number: null,
  instrument_id: null,
  specs: { sensitivity_eu_ml: 0.01 },
  active: true,
  in_use: false,
}

// Review Focus 5: the server's step 1 floor refusal, word for word (Task 2).
const FLOOR =
  'total_dilution 1:15 is below 2 x the step 1 ratio 1:10, so step 2 cannot be made'
const OVERLAP =
  'overlaps rule 1 (up to 30 mg) for this peptide; where both match, the narrower band wins (an open band is the widest) and a tie goes to the line listed first'

function setup() {
  const qc = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } })
  const wrapper = ({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) => (
    <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>{children}</QueryClientProvider>
  )
  const view = render(<EndoPrepRulesPage />, { wrapper })
  return { qc, view }
}

/** A line's table row (the new one is "new"). */
function rowOf(id: number | 'new'): HTMLElement {
  const row = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`tr[data-rule="${id}"]`)
  if (!row) throw new Error(`no row for rule ${id}`)
  return row
}

describe('EndoPrepRulesPage', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks()
    vi.mocked(getEndoPrepRules).mockResolvedValue([RETA_30, RETA_OVER_30])
    vi.mocked(getPeptides).mockResolvedValue([
      peptide(1, 'Retatrutide', 'RETA'),
      peptide(2, 'Tesamorelin', 'TESA'),
      { ...peptide(3, 'BPC-157 + TB-500', 'BLEND'), is_blend: true },
    ])
    vi.mocked(listConsumables).mockResolvedValue([cartridge])
  })

  it('lists the lines in order with their values, and counts them', async () => {
    setup()
    expect(await screen.findByText('2 rules, 1 on trial')).toBeInTheDocument()
    const second = rowOf(2)
    expect(
      within(second).getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Peptide' })
    ).toHaveTextContent('Retatrutide')
    expect(within(second).getByLabelText('Above mg')).toHaveValue('30')
    expect(within(second).getByLabelText('Total 1:N')).toHaveValue('300')
    expect(within(second).getByLabelText('Status')).toHaveValue('trial')
    expect(within(second).getByLabelText('Note')).toHaveValue(
      '60 mg PPC low at 1:250'
    )
    expect(within(rowOf(1)).getByText('1')).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(within(second).getByText('2')).toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it('adds a line from the catalog search and saves it with every field', async () => {
    const created = rule({
      id: 3,
      peptide_id: 2,
      max_mg: null,
      total_dilution: 300,
      cartridge_type_id: 4,
      status: 'trial',
      note: null,
      sort_order: 2,
      peptide_name: 'Tesamorelin',
      cartridge_name: 'Endosafe 0.01',
    })
    vi.mocked(createEndoPrepRule).mockResolvedValue({
      rule: created,
      warnings: [],
    })
    setup()
    await screen.findByText('2 rules, 1 on trial')
    const user = userEvent.setup()
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add rule' }))
    const row = rowOf('new')
    await user.click(within(row).getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Peptide' }))
    await user.type(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Search the catalog…'), 'tesa')
    // a blend is never offered: it can never match (spec D2)
    expect(screen.queryByText('BPC-157 + TB-500')).not.toBeInTheDocument()
    await user.click(await screen.findByText('Tesamorelin'))
    fireEvent.change(within(row).getByLabelText('Total 1:N'), {
      target: { value: '300' },
    })
    fireEvent.change(within(row).getByLabelText('Cartridge'), {
      target: { value: '4' },
    })
    await user.click(within(row).getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }))
    expect(createEndoPrepRule).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      peptide_id: 2,
      min_mg: null,
      max_mg: null,
      total_dilution: 300,
      step1_ratio: null,
      cartridge_type_id: 4,
      status: 'trial',
      note: null,
      active: true,
    })
    expect(await screen.findByText('3 rules, 2 on trial')).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(
      within(rowOf(3)).getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Peptide' })
    ).toHaveTextContent('Tesamorelin')
  })

  it("shows the server's 400 text on the row, then a save's warning as a chip (Review Focus 5)", async () => {
    vi.mocked(updateEndoPrepRule)
      .mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error(FLOOR))
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({
        rule: { ...RETA_OVER_30, min_mg: 20 },
        warnings: [OVERLAP],
      })
    setup()
    await screen.findByText('2 rules, 1 on trial')
    const row = rowOf(2)
    fireEvent.change(within(row).getByLabelText('Total 1:N'), {
      target: { value: '15' },
    })
    fireEvent.click(within(row).getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }))
    // only the changed field is sent
    expect(updateEndoPrepRule).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(2, {
      total_dilution: 15,
    })
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent(
      `Not saved: ${FLOOR}`
    )
    fireEvent.change(within(row).getByLabelText('Total 1:N'), {
      target: { value: '300' },
    })
    fireEvent.change(within(row).getByLabelText('Above mg'), {
      target: { value: '20' },
    })
    fireEvent.click(within(row).getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }))
    expect(await screen.findByText(OVERLAP)).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.queryByRole('alert')).not.toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it('refuses a value that is not a number on the row, without sending it', async () => {
    setup()
    await screen.findByText('2 rules, 1 on trial')
    const row = rowOf(1)
    fireEvent.change(within(row).getByLabelText('Step 1 1:N'), {
      target: { value: '1,5' },
    })
    fireEvent.click(within(row).getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }))
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent(
      'Not saved: Step 1 1:N must be a number'
    )
    expect(updateEndoPrepRule).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  })

  it('moves a line down with the whole order, and shows the order the server returns', async () => {
    vi.mocked(reorderEndoPrepRules).mockResolvedValue([
      { ...RETA_OVER_30, sort_order: 0 },
      { ...RETA_30, sort_order: 1 },
    ])
    setup()
    await screen.findByText('2 rules, 1 on trial')
    expect(
      within(rowOf(1)).getByRole('button', { name: 'Move up' })
    ).toBeDisabled()
    fireEvent.click(within(rowOf(1)).getByRole('button', { name: 'Move down' }))
    expect(reorderEndoPrepRules).toHaveBeenCalledWith([2, 1])
    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(
        [...document.querySelectorAll('tr[data-rule]')].map(r =>
          r.getAttribute('data-rule')
        )
      ).toEqual(['2', '1'])
    )
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create the page**

Create `src/components/hplc/endo/EndoPrepRulesPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Fragment, useState } from 'react'
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import {
  AlertCircle,
  ArrowDown,
  ArrowUp,
  ChevronsUpDown,
  ListOrdered,
  Loader2,
  Plus,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { toast } from 'sonner'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card'
import {
  Command,
  CommandEmpty,
  CommandInput,
  CommandItem,
  CommandList,
} from '@/components/ui/command'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { NativeSelect, NativeSelectOption } from '@/components/ui/native-select'
import {
  Popover,
  PopoverContent,
  PopoverTrigger,
} from '@/components/ui/popover'
import { Switch } from '@/components/ui/switch'
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from '@/components/ui/table'
import {
  createEndoPrepRule,
  getPeptides,
  listConsumables,
  reorderEndoPrepRules,
  updateEndoPrepRule,
  type ConsumableType,
  type EndoPrepRuleIn,
  type EndoPrepRuleOut,
  type EndoPrepRuleSaved,
  type PeptideRecord,
} from '@/lib/api'
import { fmt } from '@/lib/endo-prep'
import type { EndoPrepRuleStatus } from '@/lib/endo-prep-rules'
import {
  ENDO_PREP_RULES_KEY,
  useEndoPrepRules,
} from '@/services/endo-prep-rules'

/** A row as typed: the numbers stay text until saved, so a stray entry is
 *  refused on the row instead of sent as null. */
interface Draft {
  peptide_id: number | null
  min_mg: string
  max_mg: string
  total_dilution: string
  step1_ratio: string
  cartridge_type_id: number | null
  status: EndoPrepRuleStatus
  note: string
  active: boolean
}

const NUMBER_FIELDS = [
  ['min_mg', 'Above mg'],
  ['max_mg', 'Up to mg'],
  ['total_dilution', 'Total 1:N'],
  ['step1_ratio', 'Step 1 1:N'],
] as const

const STATUS_TONE: Record<EndoPrepRuleStatus, string> = {
  trial:
    'border-amber-500/45 text-amber-700 dark:border-amber-400/50 dark:text-amber-300',
  validated:
    'border-teal-500/45 text-teal-700 dark:border-teal-400/50 dark:text-teal-300',
}

const text = (v: number | null | undefined) => (v == null ? '' : String(v))

function draftOf(r: EndoPrepRuleOut | null): Draft {
  return {
    peptide_id: r?.peptide_id ?? null,
    min_mg: text(r?.min_mg),
    max_mg: text(r?.max_mg),
    total_dilution: text(r?.total_dilution),
    step1_ratio: text(r?.step1_ratio),
    cartridge_type_id: r?.cartridge_type_id ?? null,
    status: r?.status ?? 'trial',
    note: r?.note ?? '',
    active: r?.active ?? true,
  }
}

/** Blank is null (the standard); anything else must be a number. */
function numOf(t: string): number | null | undefined {
  const s = t.trim()
  if (!s) return null
  const n = Number(s)
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : undefined
}

/** The draft as the API body, or why it cannot be sent. The server makes
 *  every other check (spec 3) and its 400 text shows on the row. */
function bodyOf(d: Draft): EndoPrepRuleIn | string {
  if (d.peptide_id === null) return 'Pick a peptide'
  const bad = NUMBER_FIELDS.find(([key]) => numOf(d[key]) === undefined)
  if (bad) return `${bad[1]} must be a number`
  return {
    peptide_id: d.peptide_id,
    min_mg: numOf(d.min_mg) ?? null,
    max_mg: numOf(d.max_mg) ?? null,
    total_dilution: numOf(d.total_dilution) ?? null,
    step1_ratio: numOf(d.step1_ratio) ?? null,
    cartridge_type_id: d.cartridge_type_id,
    status: d.status,
    note: d.note.trim() || null,
    active: d.active,
  }
}

/** The PATCH body: only the fields that differ from the stored line. */
function changedFields(
  body: EndoPrepRuleIn,
  rule: EndoPrepRuleOut
): Partial<EndoPrepRuleIn> {
  return Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(body).filter(
      ([key, value]) => rule[key as keyof EndoPrepRuleIn] !== value
    )
  ) as Partial<EndoPrepRuleIn>
}

/** The catalog peptide search: active single peptides only (a blend never
 *  matches, spec D2). cmdk filters on `value`, so it carries the names. */
function PeptidePicker({
  label,
  peptides,
  disabled,
  onPick,
}: {
  label: string | null
  peptides: PeptideRecord[]
  disabled: boolean
  onPick: (id: number) => void
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const options = peptides
    .filter(p => p.active && !p.is_blend)
    .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
  return (
    <Popover open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
      <PopoverTrigger asChild>
        <Button
          variant="outline"
          size="sm"
          role="combobox"
          aria-label="Peptide"
          aria-expanded={open}
          disabled={disabled}
          className="h-8 w-44 justify-between font-normal"
        >
          <span className="truncate">{label ?? 'Pick a peptide'}</span>
          <ChevronsUpDown className="h-3.5 w-3.5 opacity-50" />
        </Button>
      </PopoverTrigger>
      <PopoverContent className="w-64 p-0" align="start">
        <Command>
          <CommandInput placeholder="Search the catalog…" />
          <CommandList>
            <CommandEmpty>No matching peptide.</CommandEmpty>
            {options.map(p => (
              <CommandItem
                key={p.id}
                value={`${p.name} ${p.abbreviation} #${p.id}`}
                onSelect={() => {
                  onPick(p.id)
                  setOpen(false)
                }}
              >
                {p.name}
                <span className="ml-auto text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  {p.abbreviation}
                </span>
              </CommandItem>
            ))}
          </CommandList>
        </Command>
      </PopoverContent>
    </Popover>
  )
}

/** One line, editable in place: Save sends the changed fields (a new line,
 *  all of them); the server's 400 text shows under the row until the next
 *  save, its warnings as chips. */
function RuleRow({
  n,
  rule,
  peptides,
  cartridges,
  warnings,
  canUp,
  canDown,
  moving,
  onMove,
  onSaved,
  onCancel,
}: {
  n: number | null
  rule: EndoPrepRuleOut | null
  peptides: PeptideRecord[]
  cartridges: ConsumableType[]
  warnings: string[]
  canUp: boolean
  canDown: boolean
  moving: boolean
  onMove: (by: -1 | 1) => void
  onSaved: (saved: EndoPrepRuleSaved) => void
  onCancel?: () => void
}) {
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(() => draftOf(rule))
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const dirty =
    rule === null || JSON.stringify(draft) !== JSON.stringify(draftOf(rule))
  const set = <K extends keyof Draft>(key: K, value: Draft[K]) =>
    setDraft(d => ({ ...d, [key]: value }))
  const peptideName =
    peptides.find(p => p.id === draft.peptide_id)?.name ??
    (rule && rule.peptide_id === draft.peptide_id ? rule.peptide_name : null) ??
    (draft.peptide_id === null ? null : `#${draft.peptide_id}`)
  // Active endotoxin cartridges, and the line's own if it was retired since.
  const options = cartridges
    .filter(
      c =>
        c.kind === 'endo_cartridge' &&
        (c.active || c.id === draft.cartridge_type_id)
    )
    .sort(
      (a, b) =>
        Number(b.specs.sensitivity_eu_ml) - Number(a.specs.sensitivity_eu_ml)
    )

  async function save() {
    const body = bodyOf(draft)
    if (typeof body === 'string') {
      setError(body)
      return
    }
    setSaving(true)
    setError(null)
    try {
      const saved = rule
        ? await updateEndoPrepRule(rule.id, changedFields(body, rule))
        : await createEndoPrepRule(body)
      if (rule) setDraft(draftOf(saved.rule))
      onSaved(saved)
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Save failed')
    } finally {
      setSaving(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <Fragment>
      <TableRow
        data-rule={rule?.id ?? 'new'}
        className={draft.active ? '' : 'opacity-60'}
      >
        <TableCell className="font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          {n ?? 'new'}
        </TableCell>
        <TableCell>
          <PeptidePicker
            label={peptideName}
            peptides={peptides}
            disabled={saving}
            onPick={id => set('peptide_id', id)}
          />
        </TableCell>
        {NUMBER_FIELDS.map(([key, label]) => (
          <TableCell key={key}>
            <Input
              aria-label={label}
              inputMode="decimal"
              placeholder={key === 'min_mg' || key === 'max_mg' ? '-' : 'std'}
              value={draft[key]}
              onChange={e => set(key, e.target.value)}
              className="h-8 w-20 text-right font-mono"
            />
          </TableCell>
        ))}
        <TableCell>
          <NativeSelect
            aria-label="Cartridge"
            value={draft.cartridge_type_id ?? ''}
            onChange={e =>
              set(
                'cartridge_type_id',
                e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : null
              )
            }
            className="h-8 w-40 py-1 text-xs"
          >
            <NativeSelectOption value="">usual cartridge</NativeSelectOption>
            {options.map(c => (
              <NativeSelectOption key={c.id} value={c.id}>
                {`${fmt(Number(c.specs.sensitivity_eu_ml))} · ${c.name}${c.active ? '' : ' (retired)'}`}
              </NativeSelectOption>
            ))}
          </NativeSelect>
        </TableCell>
        <TableCell>
          <NativeSelect
            aria-label="Status"
            value={draft.status}
            onChange={e => set('status', e.target.value as EndoPrepRuleStatus)}
            className={`h-8 w-28 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${STATUS_TONE[draft.status]}`}
          >
            <NativeSelectOption value="trial">trial</NativeSelectOption>
            <NativeSelectOption value="validated">validated</NativeSelectOption>
          </NativeSelect>
        </TableCell>
        <TableCell>
          <Input
            aria-label="Note"
            value={draft.note}
            onChange={e => set('note', e.target.value)}
            placeholder="why: PPC result, date"
            className="h-8 w-48 text-xs"
          />
        </TableCell>
        <TableCell>
          <Switch
            aria-label="Active"
            checked={draft.active}
            onCheckedChange={v => set('active', v)}
          />
        </TableCell>
        <TableCell className="whitespace-nowrap">
          {rule && (
            <>
              <Button
                variant="ghost"
                size="icon"
                aria-label="Move up"
                title="Move up"
                disabled={!canUp || moving}
                onClick={() => onMove(-1)}
                className="h-7 w-7"
              >
                <ArrowUp className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              </Button>
              <Button
                variant="ghost"
                size="icon"
                aria-label="Move down"
                title="Move down"
                disabled={!canDown || moving}
                onClick={() => onMove(1)}
                className="h-7 w-7"
              >
                <ArrowDown className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              </Button>
            </>
          )}
        </TableCell>
        <TableCell className="whitespace-nowrap">
          <Button
            size="sm"
            disabled={!dirty || saving}
            onClick={() => void save()}
            className="h-8"
          >
            {saving && <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" />}
            Save
          </Button>
          {onCancel && (
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              size="sm"
              disabled={saving}
              onClick={onCancel}
              className="ml-1 h-8"
            >
              Cancel
            </Button>
          )}
        </TableCell>
      </TableRow>
      {(error || warnings.length > 0) && (
        <TableRow className="hover:bg-transparent">
          <TableCell />
          <TableCell colSpan={10} className="pt-0">
            {error && (
              <p
                role="alert"
                className="text-xs font-semibold text-red-600 dark:text-red-400"
              >
                Not saved: {error}
              </p>
            )}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
              {warnings.map(w => (
                <span
                  key={w}
                  className="rounded border border-amber-500/40 bg-amber-500/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-amber-700 dark:border-amber-400/45 dark:bg-amber-400/15 dark:text-amber-300"
                >
                  {w}
                </span>
              ))}
            </div>
          </TableCell>
        </TableRow>
      )}
    </Fragment>
  )
}

/**
 * The 2 mL prep rules (slice 5, spec 9): per catalog peptide and weight
 * band, the total, the step 1 ratio and the cartridge a 2 mL row defaults
 * to. Editable in place; the order breaks ties between lines (spec 4). A
 * row that is printed, has a result or whose worksheet completed keeps the
 * line it locked to; edits here reach only rows that are not locked.
 */
export function EndoPrepRulesPage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const { data: rules = [], isLoading, error } = useEndoPrepRules()
  const { data: peptides = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['peptides'],
    queryFn: () => getPeptides(),
    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000,
  })
  const { data: cartridges = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['consumables', 'endo_cartridge'],
    queryFn: () => listConsumables({ kind: 'endo_cartridge' }),
    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000,
  })
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false)
  const [warnings, setWarnings] = useState<Record<number, string[]>>({})
  const [moving, setMoving] = useState(false)
  const trial = rules.filter(r => r.status === 'trial').length

  function saved({ rule, warnings: w }: EndoPrepRuleSaved) {
    queryClient.setQueryData<EndoPrepRuleOut[]>(ENDO_PREP_RULES_KEY, list =>
      list?.some(r => r.id === rule.id)
        ? list.map(r => (r.id === rule.id ? rule : r))
        : [...(list ?? []), rule]
    )
    setWarnings(prev => ({ ...prev, [rule.id]: w }))
  }

  async function move(i: number, by: -1 | 1) {
    const ids = rules.map(r => r.id)
    const [id] = ids.splice(i, 1)
    if (id === undefined) return
    ids.splice(i + by, 0, id)
    setMoving(true)
    try {
      queryClient.setQueryData(
        ENDO_PREP_RULES_KEY,
        await reorderEndoPrepRules(ids)
      )
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Reorder failed')
    } finally {
      setMoving(false)
    }
  }

  const rowProps = { peptides, cartridges, moving, onSaved: saved }

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col gap-4 p-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <ListOrdered className="h-6 w-6 text-primary" />
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Endotoxin prep rules</h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              The 2 mL prep per catalog peptide and weight band. A blank value
              keeps the standard. A row keeps the line it locked to at print,
              its first result or completion; Re-apply on the worksheet moves it
              on.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button onClick={() => setAdding(true)} disabled={adding}>
          <Plus className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" />
          Add rule
        </Button>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <Card className="border-destructive">
          <CardContent className="flex items-center gap-2 py-3">
            <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 text-destructive" />
            <span className="text-sm text-destructive">{error.message}</span>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      <Card className="flex-1 overflow-auto">
        <Table>
          <TableHeader>
            <TableRow>
              <TableHead className="w-10">#</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Peptide</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Above mg</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Up to mg</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Total 1:N</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Step 1 1:N</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Cartridge</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Status</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Note</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Active</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Order</TableHead>
              <TableHead />
            </TableRow>
          </TableHeader>
          <TableBody>
            {isLoading ? (
              <TableRow>
                <TableCell colSpan={12} className="py-8 text-center">
                  <Loader2 className="mx-auto h-5 w-5 animate-spin text-muted-foreground" />
                </TableCell>
              </TableRow>
            ) : (
              rules.map((rule, i) => (
                <RuleRow
                  key={rule.id}
                  n={i + 1}
                  rule={rule}
                  warnings={warnings[rule.id] ?? []}
                  canUp={i > 0}
                  canDown={i < rules.length - 1}
                  onMove={by => void move(i, by)}
                  {...rowProps}
                />
              ))
            )}
            {adding && (
              <RuleRow
                n={null}
                rule={null}
                warnings={[]}
                canUp={false}
                canDown={false}
                onMove={() => undefined}
                onCancel={() => setAdding(false)}
                {...rowProps}
                onSaved={s => {
                  saved(s)
                  setAdding(false)
                }}
              />
            )}
          </TableBody>
        </Table>
      </Card>
      <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
        {rules.length} {rules.length === 1 ? 'rule' : 'rules'}, {trial} on trial
      </p>
    </div>
  )
}

export default EndoPrepRulesPage
```

What it does:
- **Save** sends only the changed fields on an existing line, and every field on a new one.
  - The numbers stay text until saved. A non-number such as "1,5" is refused on the row and never reaches the server. Without this check, `JSON.stringify(NaN)` would send a silent null.
  - Every rule check of spec 3 is the server's. Its 400 text shows under the row as `Not saved: <text>`.
  - The warnings from a save show as amber chips under the row until that line's next save.
- **Order:** "Move up" and "Move down" send the whole id list to `POST /endo-prep-rules/reorder`, which requires every id. The page then shows the list the server returns.
- The page writes the rules cache directly from each response. It never refetches, so a list already open in another tab picks up the change within the 30-second stale time.

### Task 8: Box 2: tags, the "no rule" switch, History and Re-apply

Box 2 is `EndoSampleDilutionBox` (`src/components/hplc/endo/EndoSampleDilutionBox.tsx`). The page mounts it as a child of `EndoWorksheetView` with `boxProps`.

Box 2 has no single row editor: each cell edits one field inline (`PrepField`). The "no rule" switch and its note therefore sit in the row's Total cell, under the total.

Box 2 stays free of react-query, because its suite renders it with no `QueryClientProvider`. The page owns the Re-apply read and write, and passes them in as optional props.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/components/hplc/endo/EndoPrepRuleBits.tsx` (`RuleTags`, `NoRuleEditor`, `PrepHistory`, `ReapplyRules`)
- Create: `src/components/hplc/endo/__tests__/EndoPrepRuleBits.test.tsx`
- Modify: `src/components/hplc/endo/EndoBox.tsx` (`EndoBoxProps`, line 74)
- Modify: `src/components/hplc/endo/EndoSampleDilutionBox.tsx`:
  - `nextPrepSteps`, lines 67 to 93;
  - the box body, from 244;
  - the Sample ID cell, 354;
  - the Total cell, 404 to 452;
  - `StepCell`'s `d`, 457.
- Modify: `src/components/hplc/endo/EndoWorksheetPage.tsx` (the preview query, `reapplyRules`, `boxProps`)
- Modify: `src/hooks/use-worksheet-drawer.ts` (`mergeItemPatch`, line 128)
- Modify: `src/components/hplc/endo/__tests__/endo-fixtures.ts` (`endoBoxProps` gains a `rules` argument)
- Test: `src/components/hplc/__tests__/EndoWorksheetPage.test.tsx` (one mock line)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - From Task 6: `EndoSample.rule`, `EndoStepPlan.ruled`, `endoRowsFor(..., rules)`, `endoRowUnderRule`, `ruleText`, `ruleTag`, `getPrepRulesReapply`, `applyPrepRulesReapply`, `PrepRulesReapplyCandidate`, `PrepRulesReapplyResult`, `PrepHistoryEntry`, `PrepRuleLock`, and `WorksheetItemPatch.prep_rules_off` / `prep_note`.
  - From Task 5, the server:
    - `GET` returns `{"candidates": [{item_id, sample_id, old, new_rule}]}`.
    - `POST {item_ids}` returns `{"results": [{item_id, applied, reason}]}`.
  - Also from the server: the item PATCH accepts `prep_rules_off` and `prep_note`. A refusal comes back as the 400 text through the page's `updateItem`, as it does today.
- Produces (`EndoBoxProps`, all optional):
  - `catalog?: ConsumableType[]`
  - `reapplyCandidates?: PrepRulesReapplyCandidate[]`
  - `onReapply?: (itemIds: number[]) => Promise<PrepRulesReapplyResult[]>`
- Produces (the fixture): `endoBoxProps(items, over = {}, isCompleted = false, rules: EndoPrepRule[] = [])`. Existing callers are unchanged.

What the screen does:
- **Tags** (in the Sample ID cell, under the id):
  - "rule" (teal) on a ruled row, or amber "rule (trial)" while the line is on trial;
  - "locked" (slate) once the row's lock holds that line;
  - "special prep" (violet) on a "no rule" row.
  - A row no rule reaches has no tag. That includes a row locked to the standard, so an empty rules table changes nothing on screen.
  - Hovering a rule tag or its "locked" tag shows the line, its status, its note and the lock event. Hovering "special prep" shows the note.
- **The row editor:** the "no rule" switch, and, while it is on, the special-prep note. Both save through `onUpdateItem`, so a server refusal shows on the row as today. They are hidden on a completed worksheet. Bac water's row has neither, because bac water is never ruled.
- **The row's defaults follow its rule:**
  - The Total placeholder shows the rule's total, and the step 1 ratio placeholder shows the rule's ratio.
  - A ruled ratio is not marked as set by hand.
  - Typing the row's default stores null, so the row keeps following its rule.
  - Typing the method's standard over a rule stores it as the row's own value (D6).
  - A step 1 sample volume typed on its own carries only a typed ratio, so a ruled ratio is never pinned as typed. The wire form is `[sample_ul, null]`, which the server reads as "the ratio is the default" (`validate_prep_steps`, `_step1_ratio`). On a row with no rule this sends `[30, null]` where it used to send `[30, 10]`. The server computes the same total either way, and the null keeps a future rule able to reach the row.
- **"History (n)"** opens the row's past runs, newest first.
  - Each run is rebuilt from its stored total, ratio, cartridge and reading, on the row's weight, water and step volumes, with `rule: null`. Its EU per vial and verdict come from `prep2Result`, the current maths.
  - The cartridge's sensitivity is looked up across the whole catalog, retired types included.
- **"Re-apply rules (n)"** appears in Box 2's actions when there are candidates to offer.
  - It opens a dialog: one checkbox per row, and old against new for the total, the ratio, the cartridge, the LOQ, EU per vial and the verdict. The browser works out all of them, with the new side resolved by `endoRowUnderRule`.
  - Confirm sends the ticked rows and shows each row's outcome.
  - The page then invalidates `worksheetPageKey(id)`, which refetches the worksheet and the preview with it.
- **The UI side of Review Focus 2.** A candidate is offered only if all three hold:
  - its row is on the page;
  - the row is not switched to "no rule";
  - the new line changes at least one field the row has not typed.

  A typed override that covers every changed field gains nothing from a re-apply, so it is never offered. A row whose line changed but whose three values did not (its band, status or note) is offered. This filter is a guard on top of Task 5's preview, not a replacement for it.

```tsx
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest'

import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event'

function rule(over: Partial<EndoPrepRule> = {}): EndoPrepRule {
  return {
    id: 1,
    peptide_id: 7,
    min_mg: null,
    max_mg: null,
    total_dilution: 300,
    step1_ratio: null,
    cartridge_type_id: null,
    status: 'trial',
    note: '60 mg PPC low at 1:250',
    sort_order: 0,
    active: true,
    ...over,
  }
}

function lock(r: EndoPrepRule | null): PrepRuleLock {
  return {
    rule: r,
    locked_at: '2026-10-14T15:00:00Z',
    locked_by: 'print',
    user_id: 5,
  }
}

const pep = (over: Partial<WorksheetItemRow> = {}) =>
  endoItem({ sample_peptide_id: 7, ...over })

function setup(
  items: WorksheetItemRow[],

  extra: {
    isCompleted?: boolean

    onReapply?: (ids: number[]) => Promise<PrepRulesReapplyResult[]>
  } = {}
) {
  const { props, onUpdateItem } = endoBoxProps(
    items,
    {},
    extra.isCompleted ?? false,
    rules
  )
  render(
    <EndoSampleDilutionBox
      {...props}
      reapplyCandidates={extra.candidates}
      onReapply={extra.onReapply}
    />
  )
  return { onUpdateItem }
}

function rowOf(sampleId: string): HTMLElement {
  const row = screen.getByText(sampleId).closest('tr')
  if (!row) throw new Error(`no row for ${sampleId}`)
  return row
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Checkbox } from '@/components/ui/checkbox'
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogTitle,
} from '@/components/ui/dialog'
import { Switch } from '@/components/ui/switch'
import type {
  ConsumableType,
  PrepRulesReapplyCandidate,
  PrepRulesReapplyResult,
  WorksheetItemPatch,
  WorksheetListItem,
} from '@/lib/api'
import { fmt } from '@/lib/endo-prep'
import {
  dilutionPlan,
  fmtEu,
  prep2Check,
  prep2Result,
  type EndoCtx,
  type EndoSample,
} from '@/lib/endo-prep-2ml'
import {
  ruleTag,
  ruleText,
  type EndoPrepRule,
  type PrepHistoryEntry,
  type PrepRuleLock,
} from '@/lib/endo-prep-rules'
import {
  endoRowUnderRule,
  type EndoCartridgeOption,
  type EndoRow,
} from '@/lib/endo-worksheet-2ml'
import { TH } from '@/components/hplc/EndoWorksheetTable'
import { WorkedTip } from './EndoWorkedTip'
import { VERDICT_LABEL } from './EndoResultsBox'

/*
 * Slice 5's pieces of Box 2 (spec 9): the rule tags with their hover, the
 * "no rule" switch and its note, a row's past runs, and Re-apply. Pure
 * presentation: the page owns the reads and the writes.
 */

const TAG =
  'inline-block rounded border px-1 py-px font-sans text-[10px] font-semibold leading-tight'
const TAG_TONE = {
  validated:
    'border-teal-500/40 bg-teal-500/10 text-teal-700 dark:border-teal-400/45 dark:bg-teal-400/15 dark:text-teal-300',
  trial:
    'border-amber-500/40 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-700 dark:border-amber-400/45 dark:bg-amber-400/15 dark:text-amber-300',
  locked:
    'border-slate-500/35 bg-slate-500/10 text-slate-700 dark:border-slate-400/40 dark:bg-slate-400/15 dark:text-slate-300',
  special:
    'border-violet-500/40 bg-violet-500/10 text-violet-700 dark:border-violet-400/45 dark:bg-violet-400/15 dark:text-violet-300',
} as const

const LOCK_EVENT: Record<PrepRuleLock['locked_by'], string> = {
  print: 'print',
  result: 'its first result',
  complete: 'completion',
  reapply: 'Re-apply',
  switch: 'switching rules back on',
}

const when = (iso: string) =>
  new Date(iso).toLocaleString(undefined, {
    month: 'short',
    day: 'numeric',
    hour: 'numeric',
    minute: '2-digit',
  })

/** A cartridge type id as the lab reads it: its sensitivity. */
function cartridgeLabel(
  cartridges: EndoCartridgeOption[]
): (id: number) => string {
  return id => {
    const c = cartridges.find(o => o.id === id)
    return c ? `${fmt(c.sensitivity_eu_ml)} cartridge` : `cartridge #${id}`
  }
}

/** The hover on a ruled row's tags: the line, its status, its note and
 *  the lock event. */
function ruleTip(
  rule: EndoPrepRule,
  lock: PrepRuleLock | null,
  identity: string,
  cartridges: EndoCartridgeOption[]
): string {
  return [
    ruleText(rule, identity, cartridgeLabel(cartridges)),
    `Status: ${rule.status} (a label only; the maths is the same)`,
    rule.note ? `Note: ${rule.note}` : null,
    lock
      ? `Locked at ${LOCK_EVENT[lock.locked_by]}, ${when(lock.locked_at)}: later edits to the rule do not reach this row`
      : 'Not locked: it follows edits to the rule until print, a result or completion',
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join('\n')
}

/** "rule" or amber "rule (trial)", "locked" once locked, or "special prep"
 *  on a "no rule" row; nothing on a row no rule reaches. */
export function RuleTags({
  row,
  cartridges,
}: {
  row: EndoRow
  cartridges: EndoCartridgeOption[]
}) {
  const { item, sample } = row
  if (item.prep_rules_off)
    return (
      <span className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap gap-1">
        <WorkedTip
          tip={`No rule: a special preparation, typed by hand.\n${item.prep_note ? `Note: ${item.prep_note}` : 'No note'}`}
          className={`${TAG} ${TAG_TONE.special}`}
        >
          special prep
        </WorkedTip>
      </span>
    )
  const rule = sample.rule ?? null
  if (!rule) return null
  const lock = item.prep_rule ?? null
  const tip = ruleTip(rule, lock, sample.identity, cartridges)
  return (
    <span className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap gap-1">
      <WorkedTip tip={tip} className={`${TAG} ${TAG_TONE[rule.status]}`}>
        {ruleTag(rule)}
      </WorkedTip>
      {lock && (
        <WorkedTip tip={tip} className={`${TAG} ${TAG_TONE.locked}`}>
          locked
        </WorkedTip>
      )}
    </span>
  )
}

/** The row editor's "no rule" switch and, while it is on, the optional
 *  special-prep note (spec 8), saved through the item PATCH. */
export function NoRuleEditor({
  row,
  onSend,
}: {
  row: EndoRow
  onSend: (data: WorksheetItemPatch) => void
}) {
  const { item } = row
  const off = item.prep_rules_off === true
  return (
    <div className="mt-1.5 flex flex-col items-start gap-1">
      <label className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 font-sans text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
        <Switch
          aria-label="No rule (special prep)"
          checked={off}
          onCheckedChange={v => onSend({ prep_rules_off: v })}
          className="scale-75"
        />
        no rule
      </label>
      {off && (
        <input
          key={item.prep_note ?? ''}
          aria-label="Special prep note"
          placeholder="why (optional)"
          defaultValue={item.prep_note ?? ''}
          onBlur={e => {
            const note = e.target.value.trim() || null
            if (note !== (item.prep_note ?? null)) onSend({ prep_note: note })
          }}
          onKeyDown={e => {
            if (e.key === 'Enter') e.currentTarget.blur()
          }}
          className="h-6 w-full rounded-[3px] border bg-transparent px-1.5 font-sans text-[11px] focus:border-teal-500 focus:outline-none dark:focus:border-teal-400"
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

/** A cartridge's sensitivity from the whole catalog, retired types too, so
 *  a past run on a retired cartridge still works out. */
function lambdaOf(
  id: number | null,
  row: EndoRow,
  cartridges: EndoCartridgeOption[],
  catalog: ConsumableType[]
): number | null {
  if (id === null) return row.inherited?.sensitivity ?? null
  const fromCatalog = catalog.find(c => c.id === id)?.specs.sensitivity_eu_ml
  if (fromCatalog != null) return Number(fromCatalog)
  const allowed = cartridges.find(c => c.id === id)?.sensitivity_eu_ml
  if (allowed != null) return allowed
  return id === row.item.cartridge_type_id
    ? (row.item.cartridge_sensitivity_eu_ml ?? null)
    : null
}

/** A past run as the maths reads it: its stored total, step 1 ratio,
 *  cartridge and reading, on the row's weight, water and step volumes. */
function pastRunSample(
  row: EndoRow,
  e: PrepHistoryEntry,
  lambda: number | null
): EndoSample {
  const s = row.sample
  return {
    ...s,
    rule: null,
    totalDilution: e.total_dilution,
    steps: [
      { sampleUl: s.steps?.[0]?.sampleUl ?? null, ratio: e.step1_ratio },
      s.steps?.[1] ?? null,
      s.steps?.[2] ?? null,
    ],
    lambda,
    reading: e.result_reading,
    reader: null,
  }
}

const HCELL = 'border-b border-border/60 px-2 py-1.5 align-top'

/** "History (n)": the row's past runs (spec 7), each one's EU per vial and
 *  verdict worked out again from what was stored, with today's maths. */
export function PrepHistory({
  row,
  ctx,
  cartridges,
  catalog,
}: {
  row: EndoRow
  ctx: EndoCtx
  cartridges: EndoCartridgeOption[]
  catalog: ConsumableType[]
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const entries = row.item.prep_history ?? []
  if (!entries.length) return null
  return (
    <>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
        className="mt-1 block font-sans text-[11px] font-semibold text-teal-700 underline-offset-2 hover:underline dark:text-teal-300"
      >
        History ({entries.length})
      </button>
      <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
        <DialogContent className="flex max-h-[88vh] w-[860px] max-w-[96vw] flex-col gap-3 sm:max-w-[96vw]">
          <DialogTitle className="text-sm font-semibold">
            {row.item.sample_id}: past runs
          </DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription className="text-xs">
            Each run as it was stored when it was replaced, newest first. EU per
            vial and the verdict are worked out again from those values; the
            validity checks of the reader are not kept with a past run.
          </DialogDescription>
          <div className="min-h-0 overflow-auto">
            <table className="w-full border-collapse text-[12.5px]">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th className={TH}>Replaced</th>
                  <th className={TH}>Why</th>
                  <th className={TH}>Rule</th>
                  <th className={`${TH} !text-right`}>Total</th>
                  <th className={`${TH} !text-right`}>Step 1</th>
                  <th className={`${TH} !text-right`}>Cartridge</th>
                  <th className={`${TH} !text-right`}>Reading</th>
                  <th className={`${TH} !text-right`}>EU/vial</th>
                  <th className={TH}>Verdict</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {[...entries].reverse().map(e => {
                  const lambda = lambdaOf(
                    e.cartridge_type_id,
                    row,
                    cartridges,
                    catalog
                  )
                  const rs = prep2Result(pastRunSample(row, e, lambda), ctx)
                  const line = e.lock?.rule
                    ? `${ruleText(e.lock.rule, row.sample.identity, cartridgeLabel(cartridges))} (${e.lock.rule.status})`
                    : 'the standard'
                  return (
                    <tr key={`${e.at}-${e.why}`}>
                      <td className={`${HCELL} whitespace-nowrap`}>
                        {when(e.at)}
                      </td>
                      <td className={HCELL}>
                        {e.why === 'reapply' ? 'Re-apply' : 'override'}
                      </td>
                      <td className={HCELL}>{line}</td>
                      <td className={`${HCELL} text-right font-mono`}>
                        1:{fmt(e.total_dilution)}
                      </td>
                      <td className={`${HCELL} text-right font-mono`}>
                        1:{fmt(e.step1_ratio)}
                      </td>
                      <td className={`${HCELL} text-right font-mono`}>
                        {fmt(lambda) || '-'}
                      </td>
                      <td className={`${HCELL} text-right font-mono`}>
                        {e.result_reading ?? '-'}
                      </td>
                      <td className={`${HCELL} text-right font-mono`}>
                        {fmtEu(rs.lt === true, rs.euPerVial) || '-'}
                      </td>
                      <td className={HCELL}>
                        {rs.verdict ? VERDICT_LABEL[rs.verdict] : '-'}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  )
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </>
  )
}

type RuledField = 'total_dilution' | 'step1_ratio' | 'cartridge_type_id'
const RULED_FIELDS: RuledField[] = [
  'total_dilution',
  'step1_ratio',
  'cartridge_type_id',
]

/**
 * The candidates Re-apply offers (Review Focus 2): a row on this page that
 * is not switched to "no rule", whose new line would change a field the row
 * has not typed. A typed value beats any rule, so a row whose changed
 * fields are all typed would gain nothing; one whose line changed but none
 * of its three values (its band, status or note) is offered.
 */
function offered(
  candidates: PrepRulesReapplyCandidate[],
  rows: EndoRow[]
): { candidate: PrepRulesReapplyCandidate; row: EndoRow }[] {
  return candidates.flatMap(candidate => {
    const row = rows.find(r => r.item.id === candidate.item_id)
    if (!row || row.item.prep_rules_off) return []
    const typed: Record<RuledField, boolean> = {
      total_dilution: row.item.prep_total_dilution != null,
      step1_ratio: row.item.prep_steps?.[0]?.[1] != null,
      cartridge_type_id: row.item.cartridge_type_id != null,
    }
    const changed = RULED_FIELDS.filter(
      f =>
        (candidate.old.rule?.[f] ?? null) !== (candidate.new_rule?.[f] ?? null)
    )
    if (changed.length && changed.every(f => typed[f])) return []
    return [{ candidate, row }]
  })
}

/** Old against new, as Box 2 and Box 3 would show them. */
function figures(row: EndoRow, ctx: EndoCtx) {
  const plan = dilutionPlan(row.sample, ctx)
  const k = prep2Check(row.sample, ctx)
  const rs = prep2Result(row.sample, ctx)
  return [
    `1:${fmt(plan.target)}`,
    `1:${fmt(plan.steps[0]?.ratio)}`,
    fmt(row.cartridge?.sensitivity) || '-',
    fmt(k.loqVial) || '-',
    fmtEu(rs.lt === true, rs.euPerVial) || '-',
    rs.verdict ? VERDICT_LABEL[rs.verdict] : '-',
  ]
}

const FIGURES = ['Total', 'Step 1', 'Cartridge', 'LOQ', 'EU/vial', 'Verdict']

function Outcome({ result }: { result: PrepRulesReapplyResult | undefined }) {
  if (!result)
    return <span className="text-muted-foreground">no reply for this row</span>
  return result.applied ? (
    <span className="text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300">Re-applied</span>
  ) : (
    <span className="text-red-600 dark:text-red-400">
      Not re-applied: {result.reason}
    </span>
  )
}

/**
 * Box 2's "Re-apply rules (n)" (spec 7, D3): locked rows whose line differs
 * from today's match, each with its old and new figures and a checkbox.
 * Only the ticked rows are sent; each one's outcome shows on its row. The
 * list is the one shown when it opened, so the refresh after an apply never
 * rewrites it under its results.
 */
export function ReapplyRules({
  worksheet,
  rows,
  ctx,
  cartridges,
  catalog,
  candidates,
  onReapply,
}: {
  worksheet: WorksheetListItem
  rows: EndoRow[]
  ctx: EndoCtx
  cartridges: EndoCartridgeOption[]
  catalog: ConsumableType[]
  candidates: PrepRulesReapplyCandidate[]
  onReapply: (itemIds: number[]) => Promise<PrepRulesReapplyResult[]>
}) {
  const offers = offered(candidates, rows)
  const [listed, setListed] = useState<typeof offers | null>(null)
  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set())
  const [results, setResults] = useState<Map<
    number,
    PrepRulesReapplyResult
  > | null>(null)
  const [sending, setSending] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  function openList() {
    setListed(offers)
    setPicked(new Set(offers.map(o => o.row.item.id)))
  }

  function close() {
    setListed(null)
    setResults(null)
    setError(null)
  }

  async function send() {
    if (!picked.size || sending) return
    setSending(true)
    setError(null)
    try {
      const res = await onReapply([...picked])
      setResults(new Map(res.map(r => [r.item_id, r])))
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Re-apply failed')
    } finally {
      setSending(false)
    }
  }

  if (!offers.length && listed === null) return null
  return (
    <>
      <Button
        size="sm"
        variant="outline"
        className="mt-2"
        disabled={!offers.length}
        onClick={openList}
      >
        Re-apply rules ({offers.length})
      </Button>
      <Dialog
        open={listed !== null}
        onOpenChange={open => {
          if (!open && !sending) close()
        }}
      >
        {listed && (
          <DialogContent className="flex max-h-[88vh] w-[980px] max-w-[96vw] flex-col gap-3 sm:max-w-[96vw]">
            <DialogTitle className="text-sm font-semibold">
              Re-apply rules
            </DialogTitle>
            <DialogDescription className="text-xs">
              These rows are locked to a line that differs from the current
              rules. Re-applying moves a ticked row to the current line and
              keeps its current run in its history; typed values and the reading
              stay as they are. Promoted rows and completed worksheets are never
              changed.
            </DialogDescription>
            <div className="min-h-0 overflow-auto">
              <table className="w-full border-collapse text-[12.5px]">
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th className={TH} />
                    <th className={TH}>Sample ID</th>
                    <th className={TH}>Line, old to new</th>
                    {FIGURES.map(f => (
                      <th key={f} className={TH}>
                        {f}
                      </th>
                    ))}
                    {results && <th className={TH}>Result</th>}
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {listed.map(({ candidate, row }) => {
                    const id = row.item.id
                    const next = endoRowUnderRule(
                      row,
                      worksheet,
                      catalog,
                      candidate.new_rule
                    )
                    const before = figures(row, ctx)
                    const after = figures(next, ctx)
                    const line = (r: EndoPrepRule | null) =>
                      r
                        ? `${ruleText(r, row.sample.identity, cartridgeLabel(cartridges))} (${r.status})`
                        : 'the standard'
                    return (
                      <tr key={id} data-item={id}>
                        <td className={HCELL}>
                          <Checkbox
                            aria-label={`Re-apply ${row.item.sample_id}`}
                            checked={picked.has(id)}
                            disabled={sending || results !== null}
                            onCheckedChange={on =>
                              setPicked(prev => {
                                const s = new Set(prev)
                                if (on === true) s.add(id)
                                else s.delete(id)
                                return s
                              })
                            }
                          />
                        </td>
                        <td className={`${HCELL} font-mono`}>
                          {row.item.sample_id}
                        </td>
                        <td className={HCELL}>
                          {line(candidate.old.rule)}
                          <span className="block text-teal-700 dark:text-teal-300">
                            to {line(candidate.new_rule)}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        {before.map((b, i) => (
                          <td
                            key={FIGURES[i]}
                            className={`${HCELL} whitespace-nowrap font-mono`}
                          >
                            {b === after[i] ? (
                              b
                            ) : (
                              <>
                                {b}
                                <span className="font-semibold text-teal-700 dark:text-teal-300">
                                  {` to ${after[i]}`}
                                </span>
                              </>
                            )}
                          </td>
                        ))}
                        {results && (
                          <td className={HCELL}>
                            <Outcome result={results.get(id)} />
                          </td>
                        )}
                      </tr>
                    )
                  })}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
            {error && (
              <p
                role="alert"
                className="text-xs text-red-600 dark:text-red-400"
              >
                {error}
              </p>
            )}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-t pt-3">
              {results ? (
                <Button size="sm" onClick={close}>
                  Done
                </Button>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Button
                    size="sm"
                    disabled={!picked.size || sending}
                    onClick={() => void send()}
                  >
                    Re-apply {picked.size} {picked.size === 1 ? 'row' : 'rows'}
                  </Button>
                  <Button
                    size="sm"
                    variant="outline"
                    disabled={sending}
                    onClick={close}
                  >
                    Cancel
                  </Button>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          </DialogContent>
        )}
      </Dialog>
    </>
  )
}
```

The preview key sits under `worksheetPageKey(id)`. Each existing `invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['worksheets-list'] })` therefore refreshes it as well: after a print (which locks rows), an item save and a reader import.

### Task 9: Bench sheet marks and the "Method rules used" summary

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/lib/endo-bench-sheet-2ml.ts`:
  - the header comment, 14 to 18;
  - the import of `endo-prep-rules` (Task 6's) and of `endo-worksheet-2ml`;
  - `EndoSheet2Row` 71 and `EndoSheet2Doc` 86;
  - `buildEndoSheet2Doc` 134 to 165;
  - new CSS after `SHEET2_CSS` (227);
  - helpers before `cartPrint` (294);
  - `rowMinFor` 453 to 491 and `rowHeight` 498;
  - `fitSheet` 583;
  - in `buildEndoBenchSheet2Html`: `tally` 640, `boxOf` 664, `stepPrint` 686 to 694, `rowPrint` 721 to 729, the `<style>` 785, the readnote 821 to 834, and the summary 873.
- Test: `src/lib/__tests__/endo-bench-sheet-2ml.test.ts` (one import; a nested `describe` inside `describe('buildEndoBenchSheet2Html')`; one new `describe`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: from Task 6, `EndoSample.rule`, `EndoStepPlan.ruled`, `EndoRow.ruleCartridge`, `ruleTag`, `ruleText`, `allowedCartridges` and `buildEndoSheet2Doc(..., rules)`.
- Produces:
  - `EndoSheet2Row` gains `locked?`, `rulesOff?`, `note?` and `cartridgeRuled?`.
  - `EndoSheet2Doc` gains `cartridgeLabels?: Record<number, string>`.
  - All of them are optional, so the existing tests' hand-built rows and docs still compile.

What prints:
- **A ruled step** prints its ratio note as `1:15 &middot; rule`, or `1:15 &middot; rule (trial)` while the line is on trial.
  - The sheet has no total column, so a ruled total of 1:300 shows through step 2's derived ratio, 1:15 (Dennis efbc160).
  - The mark is added only where a note prints already, that is, on a step off the standard. An over-tube note keeps its own text.
  - A ruled step is not boxed as non-standard, and it is never `own`, so it is never underlined as set by hand.
  - Its note still prints its ratio and "rule", so the bench reads the ratio under the numbers.
  - A step that a rule set but whose sample volume was also typed by hand (`own`) keeps its box.
- **A ruled cartridge** prints a superscript R: `<sup class="rmark">R</sup>`. "Ruled" means the row has no cartridge of its own and the rule's cartridge is one the method allows.
- **A "no rule" row** prints `special prep: <note>`, or just `special prep`, under its peptide. It uses the name's own 10pt type, so the fitter measures it as name lines.
- **The summary** lists "Method rules used in this run", Dennis's layout:
  - one line per rule used, with its status chip, the line ("Retatrutide above 30 mg · 1:300 total"), the rows it covered, how many of those are locked, and its note;
  - at most 6 lines, then "and N more", so the summary stays one sheet.
  - The peptide name comes from the rows' identity, because the lock snapshot has no `peptide_name`.
- **The readnote** gains one sentence when a mark printed.
- **The fitter.**
  - A ruled note can wrap under its numbers, as an over-tube note can. "1:15 · rule (trial)" is 133 px with the test ruler, in the 126 px step column. That row is estimated at `STEP_NOTE2_PX`.
  - A special prep's lines are added to the Peptide cell's stack, each with the mg line's 1pt margin.
- **Byte for byte without rules.**
  - The new CSS (`SHEET2_RULES_CSS`) is emitted only for a sheet with a rule or a special prep. Every other addition is conditional too.
  - A sheet built with no rules is therefore unchanged. A dry run diffed the HTML, the preview HTML and the CSV of a 15-row fixture at c9ccd773 against this task's output, and they were identical. The fixture has a 1:300 row, an over-tube row, a liquid row, a blend, bac water and a page break.

```ts
// Slice 5 (Dennis efbc160, on paper only, so no dark variants): the R on a
```

3f. Before the anchor `/** The Cart. cell: the cartridge, or a pill when the prep cannot read the limit. */`, insert:
```ts
/** A ruled step's mark after its ratio: "rule", or "rule (trial)" while
 *  its line is on trial; '' for any other step. */
function ruleMark(
  st: EndoStepPlan,
  rule: EndoPrepRule | null | undefined
): string {
  return st.ruled && rule ? ruleTag(rule) : ''
}

/** A "no rule" row's line under its peptide; null for any other row. */
function specialPrepOf(r: EndoSheet2Row): string | null {
  if (!r.rulesOff) return null
  return r.note ? `special prep: ${r.note}` : 'special prep'
}

```

3g. Replace the head of `rowMinFor` and its `wraps` block. The anchor starts at the doc comment opening with the line ` * A row's minimum height: two lines for the step cells (units over the`. It runs through the `  )` that closes `const wraps = p.steps.some(`, just above `  return Math.max(wraps ? STEP_NOTE2_PX : STEP_NOTE_PX, stack)`. Replace that whole span with:
```ts
/**
 * A row's minimum height: two lines for the step cells (units over the
 * numbers), three when a step is off the standard or over the tube (its note
 * adds a line); the Peptide cell (name plus the declared mg, and a special
 * prep's line) and the Due cell can be taller still.
 */
function rowMinFor(
  s: EndoSample,
  ctx: EndoCtx,
  std: EndoStepPlan[],
  measure: TextMeasure,
  special: string | null = null
): number {
  const w = PAGE_CONTENT_W * PEP2_COL - H_PAD_PX
  const bac = formOf(s) === 'bac'
  const mg = !bac && s.weightMg !== null
  const pepLines =
    (s.identity ? lineCount(s.identity, w, PEP_FONT, measure) : 0) +
    (mg ? 1 : 0) +
    (special ? lineCount(special, w, PEP_FONT, measure) : 0)
  // Mk1: Dennis's stack leaves out the mg line's margin, about 1 px a row,
  // which overran the sheet on a page of wrapping names; the special prep
  // line has the same 1pt margin
  const stack = Math.max(
    DUE2_PX,
    pepLines * PEP_LINE_PX + (mg ? MG_TOP_PX : 0) + (special ? MG_TOP_PX : 0)
  )
  if (bac) return Math.max(42, stack)
  const p = dilutionPlan(s, ctx)
  const odd =
    !p.fits ||
    p.steps.some(
      (st, si) => st.ratio !== std[si]?.ratio || stepDiffers(st, std[si])
    )
  if (!odd) return Math.max(42, stack)
  // an over-tube note, or a ruled one ("1:15 · rule (trial)"), as stepPrint
  // prints it, can wrap under its numbers
  const noteW = PAGE_CONTENT_W * STEP_COL - H_PAD_PX
  const wraps = p.steps.some((st, si) => {
    const mark = ruleMark(st, s.rule)
    const note = !st.fits
      ? st.diluentUl !== null && !!st.sampleUl
        ? `1:${fmt(st.ratio)} \u00b7 over ${fmt(TUBE_UL / 1000)} mL`
        : ''
      : mark && (st.ratio !== std[si]?.ratio || stepDiffers(st, std[si]))
        ? `1:${fmt(st.ratio)} \u00b7 ${mark}`
        : ''
    return note !== '' && lineCount(note, noteW, NOTE_FONT, measure) > 1
  })
```
The function's last line, `  return Math.max(wraps ? STEP_NOTE2_PX : STEP_NOTE_PX, stack)`, is unchanged.

With no special prep and no rule, `pepLines`, `stack` and `wraps` compute exactly what they did before.

3h. Replace `rowHeight`. The anchor is:
```ts
function rowHeight(
  s: EndoSample,
  ctx: EndoCtx,
  std: EndoStepPlan[],
  measure: TextMeasure
): number {
  const sidW = PAGE_CONTENT_W * SID2_COL - H_PAD_PX
  return Math.max(
    rowMinFor(s, ctx, std, measure),
    sidLineCount(s.sampleId, sidW, measure) * SID_LINE_PX
  )
}
```
Replace it with:
```ts
function rowHeight(
  r: EndoSheet2Row,
  ctx: EndoCtx,
  std: EndoStepPlan[],
  measure: TextMeasure
): number {
  const sidW = PAGE_CONTENT_W * SID2_COL - H_PAD_PX
  return Math.max(
    rowMinFor(r.sample, ctx, std, measure, specialPrepOf(r)),
    sidLineCount(r.sample.sampleId, sidW, measure) * SID_LINE_PX
  )
}
```

```ts
  // The lines this run used (Dennis efbc160): each with its status, the
  // rows it covered and how many of those hold it as their lock, and its
  // note.
  const cartLabel = (id: number) =>
    doc.cartridgeLabels?.[id] ?? `cartridge #${id}`
  const used = new Map<
    string,
    { rule: EndoPrepRule; line: string; rows: number; locked: number }
  >()
  for (const r of doc.rows) {
    const rule = r.sample.rule
    if (!rule) continue
    const line = ruleText(rule, r.sample.identity, cartLabel)
    const key = `${rule.id}|${line}`
    const u = used.get(key) ?? { rule, line, rows: 0, locked: 0 }
    u.rows++
    if (r.locked) u.locked++
    used.set(key, u)
  }
  const usedList = [...used.values()]
  const rulesUsed = usedList.length
    ? '<h2 class="ruleshead">Method rules used in this run</h2><div class="usedrules">' +
      usedList
        .slice(0, RULES_LISTED)
        .map(
          u =>
            `<div><span class="st ${u.rule.status}">${u.rule.status}</span>` +
            `<span class="rl">${escapeHtml(u.line)}</span>` +
            `<span class="rn">${u.rows} ${u.rows === 1 ? 'sample' : 'samples'}${u.locked ? ` (${u.locked} locked)` : ''}` +
            `${u.rule.note ? ` &middot; ${escapeHtml(u.rule.note)}` : ''}</span></div>`
        )
        .join('') +
      (usedList.length > RULES_LISTED
        ? `<div class="rn wide">and ${usedList.length - RULES_LISTED} more</div>`
        : '') +
      '</div>'
    : ''
```
The same line under two different snapshots, such as a row locked before an edit and a row following the edit, is listed twice, once with each set of values. The key is the id plus the printed line.

3m. In the summary, replace the anchor:
```ts
    readnote +
    '<h2 class="ruleshead">How each figure is worked out</h2>' +
```
with:
```ts
    readnote +
    rulesUsed +
    '<h2 class="ruleshead">How each figure is worked out</h2>' +
```

The CSV is unchanged: it has no rule columns. The spec asks for none, and the sheet and the Box 2 history carry the rule.

1. **Boot.** The table and the four columns are present, with no `migration_skipped`.

7. **Promote.** Promote a ruled row with a reading through `POST /worksheets/{id}/promote`, with the browser-shaped payload computed at the ruled total. Expect it promoted: the I-3 check uses the locked total.

9. **Complete.** `POST /worksheets/{id}/complete`. Re-apply then lists nothing, and an apply request is refused for every row.
10. **Clean up.**
   - Deactivate the test rules.
   - Leave the worksheet as rehearsal data.
   - Record any ids.
